# 🧠 Arthur — memory log

This file IS Arthur's memory. Arthur is a fresh instance every run; nothing is
remembered except what's written here. **Read this top-to-bottom before doing anything;
append an entry after every run.** Newest entries at the BOTTOM.

Entry format:
```
## [YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM] — <via Kaito | direct> — <one-line topic>
- Asked: …
- Studied / referenced: …  (the work you devoured before judging)
- Found / verdict: SHIP | POLISH | REWORK …
- Specs given: …  (exact tokens / px / curves handed to the builder)
- Still open / next: …
```

## [2026-06-29] — created (by Akashi, per Osefe) — role + first context
- Role: UI/UX & visual design lead. Domains: mobile app UI, PC/desktop web, tablet UI,
  video-game UI/HUD. Hold the visual + clarity bar; "a 9-year-old can use it" is the floor.
- Personality: gluttony FOR KNOWLEDGE — devour the best references before judging a pixel;
  perfectionist to obsession; specifics over vibes (8pt grid, 4.5:1 contrast, 44px targets,
  easing curves), never "good enough."
- Mode: read-only/director — I produce exact specs + redlines (VERDICT: SHIP/POLISH/REWORK);
  the file owner or Kaito implements (find-xor-fix). I review the result against my spec.
- The app: single-file `index.html`, cyberpunk HUD aesthetic (Orbitron / Share Tech Mono,
  neon-on-dark, glow, diamond logo), 7 languages, offline-first/on-device PWA.
- Defer: Akashi = safety (anything network/CDN/keys/privacy → clear with him first, MRLN is
  offline-first); Mikoto = the words + 7-lang fit; Hugo ships; Kaito merges.
- Open context from the room: Osefe wants browser→app data transfer made dead-simple
  (9-year-old proof). That's a UX problem with my name on it — when dispatched, study it.
- Next run: git pull, read this log, read TEAM-CHAT.md, then work. Append before finishing.

Recomputed WCAG: badge cyan/panel2 11.32 PASS; badge+inactive-h2 txt-dim/card 5.73 PASS; uppercase label txt-dim/panel2 5.40 PASS; moveTip L2 dim/#08121f 5.84 PASS — no contrast blockers, badge 10px clears the 4.5:1 small-text floor.
- Found / verdict: **POLISH (core is ship-quality).** All THREE must-fixes land as specified:
  #1 STEP badges — `setBadge` writes "STEP {n}" via tf(), active=cyan border+cyan text, 2nd=line border+dim; always 1→2 even when reorder swaps boxes. ✓
  #2 Active box wins at box level — `setBox` fills --panel2 + cyan border + --glow, demotes other to transparent/--line + dimmed h2. One focal point. ✓

## [2026-06-29] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — REVIEW: Tier 1 motion B,D,E,F,G implemented @ 2a1291b
- Asked: review Kaito's 5-of-7 Tier 1 motion build (CSS/motion only) — confirm each lands per my d463629 spec, timings/easings feel right, nothing fights the existing HUD (header `sweep`, `fade`), flag if `prefers-reduced-motion` should gate them, verify B's direction logic survives tab reorder, verdict + punch-list, and confirm/argue the A+C deferral.
- Studied / referenced: `git show 2a1291b -- index.html` (full diff); live index.html — grid overlay + gridPulse (60-71), header `sweep` (84-89), `.tab` base/hover/active/focus (113-123), `.panel`/`fade`/slideFromR-L (143-150), exp-group + rowIn stagger (223-250), **prefers-reduced-motion block (478-480)**, renderExpenses (2211-2245), refreshFinance→renderExpenses call (2721-2724), tab-click handler w/ prevTabIdx (2049-2068). Re-derived effective grid alpha (.045 baked × .72→1 layer opacity = ~.032→.045 breathing).
- Found / verdict: **POLISH — one real bug (E re-trigger), the other four SHIP-quality.**
  CORRECTION to my own memory: I previously logged "the app currently doesn't gate motion" — that was WRONG. There IS a global `@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*,*::before,*::after{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}` at index.html:478-480. Its `*` wildcard catches ALL five new animations (gridPulse, slideFromR/L, rowIn, the :active scales). Reduced-motion is fully respected — NOT a gap. Logging the correction so I don't re-flag it.
  - B (directional panels): direction computed from a FRESH `querySelectorAll().indexOf(t)` at click time → reordered tabs handled correctly. Reads right. Minor self-correcting edge: `prevTabIdx` can be stale relative to a layout that changed since the last click (double-tap reorder) → at most ONE wrong-direction slide, corrects next click. Within spec ("live visible index"). SHIP.
  - D (press squeeze): `.tab:active{transform:translateY(0) scale(.97)}` correctly cancels the hover translateY(-2px) so press doesn't fight hover; `.btn` .97 / `.scen-btn` .95. Kaito's .97/.95 split (vs my flat .95) is BETTER — same physical travel on the bigger primary btn. No retune. SHIP.
  - F (focus glow): exact to spec (2px lime outline + box-shadow 0 0 0 4px rgba(124,255,178,.12)). On a focused active tab the lime ring replaces the cyan --glow (box-shadow same property, not additive) — correct, focus should win, still highly visible. SHIP.
  - G (grid heartbeat): gridPulse 6s ease-in-out, layer opacity .72→1; effective line alpha breathes ~.032→.045. Slow, subtle, ambient — never competes. Applied to body::before not a separate overlay, but the intent lands. No conflict with header `sweep` (different element, different property). SHIP.
  - **E (accordion stagger) — POLISH/the one fix.** `.exp-group.open .exp-body tr{animation:rowIn ...}` keys off the `.open` CLASS, but `renderExpenses()` (2211) rebuilds `host.innerHTML` on EVERY data change via `refreshFinance()` (2723) — slider move, add expense, parsed command, currency switch. Re-render re-emits open groups with `open` already in the static HTML (2229), so the brand-new <tr> nodes mount inside `.exp-group.open` and rowIn FIRES AGAIN. Result: every open accordion re-staggers its rows on unrelated number changes. Spec intent was "rows materialise when a group OPENS," not "re-shuffle whenever any number changes." Distracting, fights the feature's own purpose. ROUTE TO KAITO.
- Specs given (to Kaito, one fix): gate rowIn to the actual open EVENT, not the persisted `.open` class. Cleanest: in the `.exp-head` click handler (2240) where it currently does `grp.classList.toggle('open')`, when OPENING add a transient class (e.g. `grp.classList.add('just-opened')`) and move the stagger rule to `.exp-group.just-opened .exp-body tr{animation:rowIn ...}`; clear `just-opened` on `animationend` of the last row (or a ~300ms timeout). renderExpenses() re-renders never carry `just-opened`, so cross-render re-triggers stop while the open gesture still animates. Keep the nth-child 25ms delays exactly as-is. Alternatively (lighter, slightly less precise): suppress rowIn during renderExpenses by not animating when `firstRender===false` AND the group was already open — but the class-on-event approach is the clean one.
- A+C deferral: **AGREE, correct call — not a skip.** (A) my own parseFloat(el.textContent) spec genuinely breaks on "32 000 kr"/grouped figures + currency suffix — needs a parser that strips fmtN formatting before counting; building it wrong silently corrupts the displayed number, the worst class of bug in a finance app. (C) row-born flash needs per-row identity threaded applyChange→renderExpenses (which rebuilds innerHTML wholesale, so there's no stable node to flash yet) — same render-identity problem as E, bigger. Both touch the number/render + anti-tamper poison paths → Akashi review is right, not a CSS drop-in. Defer stands.
- Still open / next: (1) Kaito implements the E rowIn event-gate fix (owner of index.html); I re-review against this spec. (2) A+C return as a careful follow-up with Akashi on the render-identity + figure-parsing — flag me for the A counter spec rewrite (parse-then-count, not parseFloat) when ready. (3) Tier 2 H/I/J/K/L full specs still parked for build order. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 2a1291b.

Judge hierarchy now that the header has TWO number zones (hero + 3 income stats), colour thresholds (lime/amber<10%/red<0), lock EN wording of "LEFT OVER · TYPICAL" before Mikoto translates it next, spacing/ccy-size/clamp. Compared against the existing #ovLeft bignum in Overview (884) — same value, hero is the promotion I asked for. Contrast math: lime ~14:1, amber ~11:1, red ~6.4:1, txt-dim label ~5.4:1 on the header bg — all PASS (hero only needs 3:1 at 38-60px; even red clears the 4.5 body floor). Reduced-motion: hero is static textContent, no counter (item A still deferred), no animation to gate — clean.
- Found / verdict: **POLISH.** The promotion is RIGHT and lands the "best asset out of the card" goal — value, colour-coding, currency-rides-ccySuffix, no poison touch, reads #ovLeft's value. But hierarchy is NOT yet decisive: the hero is naked text while the 3 income stats sit in bordered, gradient-filled, colour-valued BOXES — boxes carry more weight than their 18-26px font implies, so the trio competes with the hero as a cluster. Hero must dominate harder. Plus a real a11y nuance on negatives (colour does almost all the "you're short" work).
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, full detail in TEAM-CHAT):
  1. DEMOTE the income stat row so the hero clearly wins. Drop `.stat .val` from clamp(18-26px) → **clamp(15px,2.6vw,19px)**, weight 700→**600**. Tighten `.stat` padding 14px 16px → **10px 12px**, `.status` gap 12→**10**, margin-top 18→**14**. Keeps 3-col on desktop; the existing 720px rule already stacks them 1-col on phone — that 1-col stack of full-size boxes UNDER a 60px hero is the worst competition case, so the size cut matters most there.
  2. Widen the hero/stat gap for a clean hierarchy break: `.hero` margin-top stays 18; add the breathing room BELOW the hero by bumping `.status` margin-top to 14 (above) — net the hero gets a clear band of its own.
  3. NEGATIVE a11y: red colour alone fails colourblind users + a bare "-" is a weak cue for a 9-yo. In setHeroLeftover, when lo<0 prefix an explicit token — change `v.textContent = fmtN(lo)` so the bad case reads **"SHORT " + fmtN(Math.abs(lo))** (or a "▼" glyph), and keep red. New string "SHORT" → flag Mikoto. (Routing the copy decision to Kaito+Mikoto; the redundancy-not-colour-only principle is the firm ask.)

## [2026-06-29] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — REVIEW (step 2 of build/ship): bold-emphasis loss on move-step headings @ 0192136
- Asked: Mikoto wired 9 static controls on the "📦 Move my data" card for i18n (now translating in all 7). SIDE EFFECT I flagged earlier: the two step headings lost their inline <b> on "this"/"off" because the i18n applier sets textContent. Decide objectively: (1) does losing the bold hurt the 9-yo clarity given STEP badges + active-box fill/glow + 📥/📤 already carry hierarchy? Kaito's view: restoring inline bold needs fragment-splitting the heading, which is i18n-hostile (DE/HU reorder) → maybe "accept no bold." Agree or give a CSS-only emphasis that survives translation. (2) Any other engagement/professional issue now the card is fully localized. Read-only, route to Kaito.
- Studied / referenced: confirmed at the BYTE level. Applier index.html:4944 = `el.textContent = tr` → strips ALL inline children incl <b>, every language EN included. data-i18n sits on the INNER <span> (1526/1550), not the <h2>, so the STEP badge sibling span is untouched. Prior markup @0192136~1 = `onto <b>this</b> device` / `Copy my data <b>off</b> this device` — verified via git show. setupMoveSituation/setBox (5391-5397): active box = --panel2 fill + --cyan border + --glow, h2 color default; inactive = transparent + --line + h2 forced --txt-dim inline. setBadge (5399) STEP 1 cyan / STEP 2 dim. Pulled DE/HU/ES/EN dict values via node: DE GET "auf dieses Gerät", DE SEND "von diesem Gerät"; HU GET "erre az eszközre", HU SEND "erről az eszközről" — the emphasized concept (this/off) becomes a DIFFERENT inflected word in a DIFFERENT position per lang → confirms fragment-bold would mistranslate.
- Found / verdict: **SHIP (with one optional POLISH).**
  (1) Kaito is RIGHT on two counts and I say so on the evidence: (a) fragment-bold IS genuinely i18n-hostile — "dieses"/"von"/"erre"/"erről" move position + inflect, so a `prefix+<b>word</b>+suffix` split can't be translated cleanly; (b) the hierarchy already carries the "which box is live": STEP 1 cyan badge + panel2 fill + cyan border + glow + the inactive box demoted to dim is a FAR louder "do this one first" signal than two bold words ever were. The bold on this/off was doing *direction* disambiguation (TO vs FROM device), but the 📥-vs-📤 emoji + the verb ("Bring … onto" vs "Copy … off") + the active-box fill already do that more decisively. Losing it does NOT drop us below the 9-yo bar. So: accept no inline bold. Agreed.
  (2) BUT "no emphasis at all" isn't the only option, and I won't pretend it is. There's a CSS-only path that survives translation WITHOUT fragmenting the string — emphasize the WHOLE heading span, not a word: on the ACTIVE step only, render its <h2> span at weight 700 + full --txt (vs inactive 600/--txt-dim). The applier sets textContent, not style, so CSS weight is never stripped; setBox already toggles active/inactive, so it can drive it. That restores "this line is the emphasized one" with zero i18n risk and zero fragmenting. This is the right replacement for the lost per-word bold: emphasize the active STEP, not the word.
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, OPTIONAL polish — card SHIPs without it):
  In setBox(el,active): on the heading text span (the `<span data-i18n=…>`, 2nd child of the h2 — give it a class e.g. `.moveStepTitle` for a clean selector), set `fontWeight = active ? '700' : '600'`. Leave the h2 color logic exactly as-is (active default --txt / inactive --txt-dim already lands). No HTML string change, no new i18n key, no fragmenting. Net effect: active step heading reads bolder + brighter than the demoted one — the per-word bold's job done at the LINE level, translation-safe.
- Other engagement/professional checks (all PASS, no blockers):
  • DE/HU length: longest is DE GET "📥 Meine Daten auf dieses Gerät holen" (~37 chars) — at 14px h2 inside a 16px-padded box on a narrow phone it wraps to 2 lines; badge has white-space:nowrap and the h2 is flex align-items:center, so the badge stays pinned and the text wraps under itself cleanly. No clip/overflow. Acceptable (wrapping ≠ breaking).
  • Serious-tone rule: headings are factual ("Bring my data onto this device" / "Copy my data off this device"), allowed functional 📥/📤/📦 icons, no hype/celebration emoji. Compliant.
  • Contrast: active h2 default --txt on --panel2 fill, inactive --txt-dim #6f93b5 on card #0b1422 = 5.73:1 PASS (re-confirmed from prior runs). Badge cyan/panel2 11.3 PASS.
- Still open / next: (1) Kaito's call on the optional active-step weight polish (find-xor-fix → his to build; I re-review only if he takes it). (2) No new i18n keys → nothing new for Mikoto from THIS find; the 9 wired controls already MISSING:0 per her fix. (3) Card is engagement+professional clean at 0192136. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 0192136.

Helpers: `tf()` (4934, split-replace token sub), `t()` (4932), `fmtN()` (1743, toLocaleString grouping). Verified terminology consistency vs the rest of the app: hero label "YOU KEEP · TYPICAL" (843), #ovLeft "Left over · typical month" (888), hero tooltip "after every bill and any debt payment, in a typical month" (842), KB answer register (4320-4332, all factual/no-hype) — the new strings already echo "you keep / typical month / after every bill and any loan", good alignment. Re-read CLAUDE.md serious-not-warm rule (no warm/chatty editorializing; state the fact, don't color it).

SHIP that as-is. Three copy items to fix before Mikoto locks:

The "(s)" parenthetical plural reads like leaked placeholder code in a PAID product — fails the 9-yo bar (child reads "month-bracket-s") AND the serious/credible bar. Worse it's an i18n LANDMINE: "(s)" is English-only morphology; DE/HU/SV/DA/NB/ES have different plural rules Mikoto can't express by appending "(s)". FIX = branch the WHOLE template on n, so each lang owns two clean strings: `n===1 ? tf('At {rate}/month you would reach {amt} in about 1 month.',{rate,amt}) : tf('At {rate}/month you would reach {amt} in about {n} months.',{n,rate,amt})`. FIX = mirror the yes-branch shape: `tf('No — {amt} is more than your typical {left}/month leftover, so part of it would come from savings.',{amt,left})`. ("No —" parallels "Yes —" in the afford-yes branch @4371, clean and decisive for a 9-yo; "part of it would come from savings" is calmer/more accurate than "come out of savings", since only the OVERAGE exceeds leftover, not the whole purchase.)
  - **P3 (consistency): the yes-branch is missing "typical".** @4371 "fits within your typical {left}/month leftover" already says typical — GOOD, no change. Flag only so Kaito keeps "typical" in BOTH branches when he edits P2 (matches the hero label + the leftover answer @4364, one vocabulary across the whole assistant).
  - **P4 (register polish, low): contractions.** "you'd" @4380, "That's" @4372, "I am unable" @4397 is already good. A paid finance tool reads a touch more credible without contractions in the computed answers — recommend "you would" in the save-time line (folded into P1). Not a blocker; "You keep" / "You spend" stay as-is (those are clean). Leave the error-state strings (4363/4369/4370/4378/4379) as-is — they're already factual and instructive.
- Engagement/professional lens: nothing reads as a chatbot gimmick once P1+P2 land. The confined-domain "I can't, look it up" boundary is a TRUST asset for a finance app (never bluffs a number) — keep it verbatim. The poison-gating (figures × __sys.token() → NaN on a tampered copy) is invisible to the user but correct. No layout/contrast issue: answers render in the existing aiAnswer body via textContent, same panel styling already cleared.

- Asked: review the new separate-photo export/import block in the "📦 Move my data" card. Judge (1) 9-yo clarity of WHY photos are separate + the data-loss-illusion risk (move data, see no photos, think it's lost), (2) placement (after both get/send boxes vs mapping export→send / import→get), (3) empty/edge states (no photos, import-with-no-matching-meals, persistence-denied), (4) hierarchy (two equal ghost buttons vs export-primary). Serious-not-warm. Read-only, route to Kaito.
- Studied / referenced: card markup index.html:1518-1574 (movePhotos 1564-1573); JS PHOTOMOVE module 6456-6528 (exportPhotos/importPhotos/edge msgs); exportDataCode comment 5495-5500 (photos DELIBERATELY excluded from quick move — Osefe's call); setupMoveSituation/setBox/setBadge 5581-5597 (STEP 1/STEP 2 badges + active-box fill/glow); deviceTip renderer 5476-5491 (save-location tips only — does NOT mention photos). Token/contrast: --txt-dim #6f93b5 on card #0b1422 ≈5.7:1 PASS (label+explainer legible); .note carries border-left:2px cyan-dim (212). movePhotos sits AFTER both badged step boxes, separated by a hairline top-border, with NO step badge → deliberately a 3rd, lesser tier.
- Found / verdict: **POLISH.** The block itself is clean, honest, and well-built — the edge-state copy is genuinely strong (import-with-no-matching-meals "Bring your data over first, then import photos" is the single best line in the feature; fail-closed all-or-nothing import; "✓ Saved {n} photo(s)…load it on your other device" closes the loop). Serious-tone compliant (functional 📤/📥 only). BUT it fails the most important test: the **data-loss illusion is real and unmitigated**. A user taps "Copy my data" (the SEND box) → moves it → new device shows meals with NO photos → concludes data was lost/broken. Nothing in the SEND box or the success toast warns that photos are excluded; the only tie is a dim block lower down that's easy to miss. That's the one must-fix.
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html):
  1. **MUST-FIX — close the data-loss illusion at the SEND box (the moment of risk).** Add ONE dim line under the SEND box's existing desc (1551), e.g. data-i18n "Food photos are not in this copy — send them with “Export my photos” below." So the person copying their data is TOLD, at the point of action, that photos travel separately. The current separate explainer (1566) tells the RECEIVER; the SENDER never sees it before they walk away thinking they're done. (Mirror copy → Mikoto.)
  2. **MUST-FIX — confirm the move is two-part in the SEND success toast.** After copyOut succeeds, the toast should add (if any photos exist) "Photos are separate — tap “Export my photos” to bring them too." A toast at success is where the 9-yo actually reads. (Route the conditional to Kaito; string → Mikoto.)
  3. **PLACEMENT — keep movePhotos where it is (after both boxes), do NOT split export→send / import→get.** Considered the send/get mapping and REJECT it: photos are an optional add-on to a primary 2-step flow, not a 3rd co-equal step. Splitting them into the badged boxes would (a) break the clean STEP 1→STEP 2 hero/secondary hierarchy I specced earlier, (b) double the photo affordance into two places = more confusion not less, (c) fight setupMoveSituation's reorder (the boxes swap order; a photo half pinned to each would jump around). A single labelled "PHOTOS MOVE SEPARATELY" zone under both is the correct mental model. KEEP.
  4. **HIERARCHY — two equal ghost buttons is CORRECT, do NOT make export primary.** They're a matched send/receive pair (like the rest of the card's ghost secondaries), neither dominates, and the card already has ONE filled hero (the active STEP box). A filled photo-export button would create a 2nd focal point competing with the STEP-1 hero. Equal ghosts = right. KEEP.
  5. **POLISH (low) — the "PHOTOS MOVE SEPARATELY" label reads slightly like a 3rd silent step.** It's fine, but to make the dependency explicit, consider tightening the explainer's first clause to lead with the DEPENDENCY not the size: "Move your data first (above), then move your photos here — they travel as their own file because food photos can be large." Reordered so the 9-yo learns the SEQUENCE before the reason. Optional; #1+#2 are the real fixes. (String → Mikoto.)
- Strings for Mikoto (new/changed EN, all length-sensitive — DE/HU expand): SEND-box photo line (#1), SEND success photo addendum (#2), optional explainer reorder (#5). Items 1+2 are the gate-critical ones.

- Found / verdict: **REWORK shape — pick (b) the strength⇄cardio TOGGLE, reject (c) "just optional distance".** Evidence, not preference: (c) fails the 9-yo "which fields apply?" bar — a single form showing Exercise/Weight/Reps/Date/Distance(optional) forces a child to know that for a run you skip 3 fields and fill 1, and the renderer would still print "~{1RM}kg 1RM" under a 5 km run, which is nonsense in a PAID tool (credibility hit). (a) a whole 2nd panel duplicates the form, the list, the delete handler, the i18n, and splits "my records" into two cards a child must reconcile — over-build. (b) keeps ONE card, swaps Weight/Reps→Distance/Time on a 2-segment toggle, swaps the "best" math + the row read per record's stored `type`, and lets BOTH kinds list together grouped by name. Mixed list is correct: a 9-yo reads one "my records" board, each row self-labels via its own best-line (no 1RM on a run). Toggle only chooses what you're LOGGING; storage tags each record so render is per-record, not per-toggle.
- Specs given (full detail in TEAM-CHAT + below): segmented toggle "STRENGTH | CARDIO" default STRENGTH (back-comptible, existing records are strength). STRENGTH mode = today's 4 fields unchanged. CARDIO mode = Activity / Distance(km) / Time(optional mm:ss or min) / Date. Store `type:'strength'|'cardio'` on each record (migrate: missing type ⇒ 'strength'). "Best" cardio = if any record in the group has a time+distance → fastest PACE (min/km), else longest DISTANCE; header reads "best {dist} km · {pace}/km" or "best {dist} km" — NO 1RM. Validation cardio: activity + distance>0 (time optional). Units: **km, fixed** (no imperial — matches the kg precedent + free-to-operate/no-locale-unit-system; flagged as a deliberate scope call, mi can be a later backlog item). NEW strings listed for Mikoto. No-regress list locked (strength flow, 1RM math, delete, export/import schema, body-grade = N/A).
- Still open / next: (1) Kaito synthesizes the 4 role-opinions → builds (owner of index.html); I re-review the build against this spec. (2) Akashi: cardio figures (distance/time/pace) are NEW numbers in STATE.prs → must get the `__sys.token()` poison thread like wt/reps if PRs are poisoned today (flag — I didn't audit whether prs figures are currently poisoned; Akashi owns that). (3) Mikoto: ~10 new strings once Kaito locks EN. (4) Decision flagged for Osefe via Kaito: km-only vs offer mi toggle — I recommend km-only now, mi to backlog. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 4173d7c-era tip.

## [2026-06-30] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — RE-REVIEW: cardio-PR feature BUILT @ d2abf77 (verify vs my spec)
- Asked: verify Kaito's real cardio-PR build against my Jun-30 spec — toggle + field-swap, inline unit select, min:sec inputs, cardio header/row read; WEIGH Kaito's override (he included a km/mi per-entry select; I'd recommended km-only-now / mi-backlog); give small copy/visual redlines before Mikoto locks i18n. Read-only, no index.html edit.
- Studied / referenced: built markup index.html:1369-1385 (#prSeg two .prSeg ghost btns, .prStrength/.prCardio show-hide, #prDist+#prUnit inline select, #prMin:#prSec time); setPrMode 3593-3605 (toggle active style cyan-dim bg + cyan border + #eaffff text, placeholder swap via t(), __refreshPrMode for live lang switch); add logic 3607-3637 (cardio: dist>0 + activity req, time optional, Akashi caps dist<=100000/secs<=86400, stores {dist,distUnit,secs,type:'cardio'}); renderPRs 3931-3970 (groups by (ex.toLowerCase()+' '+prType), cardio best = fastest km-normalized pace among timed else longest km-dist, header "best {dist} {unit} · {pace} /{unit}", NO 1RM); helpers prType/prDistKm/prPace 3928-3930; migrate = prType() defaults missing type→'strength' (3928) so NO schema migration needed, old PRs render as strength — clean back-compat. COMPUTED WCAG: active label #eaffff on --cyan-dim #0a7e8c = **4.63:1 PASS** (clears 4.5 floor, but thin); inactive --txt-dim on --panel 5.4 PASS; lime header ~14 PASS; PR tag.now 8px cyan border cyan-dim. No contrast blocker.
- Found / verdict: **POLISH (core SHIPs; 1 real UX wrinkle + small copy).** Build matches my spec on every structural point — toggle shape, field-by-mode swap, time-optional, no-1RM-on-cardio, mixed list grouped by (name,type), best=pace-else-distance. 9-yo + serious-tone bars: held.
- **The mi/km override decision — I AGREE WITH KAITO, override accepted, on the evidence:** my km-only call was to AVOID building a global locale-unit system (correct to avoid). Kaito did NOT build that — it's ONE inline `<select>` storing distUnit PER ENTRY, "best" normalized via prDistKm (×1.60934) so cross-unit compare is fair. That defeats my actual objection (no locale system) while letting a US tester log miles day one. Mikoto+Akashi favored mi-available. Cost: nil. So mi-now is the right call; I withdraw km-only.
- **BUT the override introduces ONE real UX wrinkle (routed to Kaito, the fix):** groups key on (exercise,type) ONLY — not unit. So "Running 5 km" + "Running 3 mi" land in the SAME group and the list renders MIXED UNITS stacked ("5 km · 5:00 /km" above "3 mi · 8:00 /mi"), and the header shows whichever unit the best happens to be in. A 9-yo (or anyone) comparing two rows in different units can't eyeball which run was longer/faster — the normalize-for-best is invisible to them. Not a blocker (rare: most users pick one unit and stay), but it's the messy-mixed-list risk I was asked to watch for. FIX options for Kaito: (a) cheapest/best — render every row in the GROUP's dominant unit (the best entry's unit), converting the others for DISPLAY only (store stays per-entry); the header unit then always matches the rows. (b) lighter — leave storage, but in the row read append the km-normalized value as the dim `<small>` so a mixed list still has one common axis. I recommend (a): one unit per group on screen = one clean comparison axis, matches "best {dist} {unit}" header, zero new i18n.
- Small copy/visual redlines (route to Kaito BEFORE Mikoto locks):
  1. **Header "best {dist} {unit} · {pace} /{unit}" → drop the space in "/{unit}" mismatch:** row read is "5 km · 5:00 /km" — the " /km" has a leading space (pace + space + /km). It reads slightly like "5:00 [space] /km". Minor; recommend pace unit as "{pace}/{unit}" (no space) to read as a rate "5:00/km" — standard running notation. Apply in BOTH header (3946) and row (3952). Low, but it's the number landing on the eye.
  2. **Placeholder "e.g. Running, Rowing"** (3600) — good, serious, functional. KEEP.
  3. **Empty state "No records yet — log your first lift or run above."** (3933) — now covers both modes, factual, good. KEEP.
  4. Success toast "Logged {ex} — {dist} {unit}." (3626) parallels strength "Logged {ex} {wt}kg × {reps}." — fine. KEEP.
  5. Label "Time (optional)" (1380) — the "(optional)" parenthetical is the ONE spot that mirrors the {n} month(s) placeholder-leak smell I flagged before, BUT here it's a genuine field qualifier not a plural hack, and it's honest (time IS optional). Acceptable; if Mikoto wants it cleaner she can render optional as dim helper text, her call. Not a redline.
- Strings for Mikoto (new, EN locked pending #1 above): "STRENGTH","CARDIO","Activity","Distance","Time (optional)","e.g. Running, Rowing","Enter an activity and a distance.","Logged {ex} — {dist} {unit}.","No records yet — log your first lift or run above.","best {dist} {unit} · {pace} /{unit}" (→ "{pace}/{unit}" if #1 taken),"best {dist} {unit}". All length-sensitive; "STRENGTH/CARDIO" on two flex:1 buttons — DE "KRAFT"/"AUSDAUER" fit; HU longer, buttons flex so they absorb it.

## [2026-06-30] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — REVIEW: Media Log BUILT @ 9ba07d5 (item 1 of greenlit roadmap) vs my brainstorm spec
- Asked: judge Kaito's real Media Log build (panel #media 1518-1561 + MEDIALOG module 6787-6912 + renderMedia) against my own brainstorm spec (TEAM-CHAT 537-550): the <10s add-rate-done loop + 9-yo bar, rating-sheet hierarchy, chip+nudge interaction, ranked-row density, calibration prompt wording/placement, empty-state copy, serious-not-warm tone on all new strings, anything MISSING worth doing now vs backlog. Verdict + tight numbered list to Kaito. Read-only, no index.html edit.
- Studied / referenced: full panel markup 1518-1561; MEDIALOG module end-to-end (add/del/openRate/closeRate/buildChips/nudge/saveRate/showCompare/applyCompare/share/fb/renderLists/wire) 6787-6912; token defs (--cyan #00e5ff, --cyan-dim #0a7e8c, --lime #7CFFB2, --txt-dim #6f93b5, --line); base classes .btn (290-298, pad 10/18, :active scale.97), .btn.ghost, .item-row (300, pad 11/14, gap12), .empty (304, txt-dim italic 14px). Re-used my prior WCAG computation for the #eaffff-on-cyan-dim pair (computed on the cardio toggle Jun-30): 4.63:1 — PASS but THIN, same pair reused for the active chip here. Cross-checked my own spec lines 544-548 (tap whole-number chip → ±0.1 fine-tune; comparison ±0.2 / 3 neighbours / does X belong here; comment private+timestamped+collapsed; share "MY TOP" clipboard; <10s add-rate-done; conversational empty states). Confirmed Kaito's 9/9 loop claim is plausible from the code path (add→towatch unshift, Rate it→openRate default 8, chips+nudge→paintVal, save→watched+rated+compare if neighbours, search matchQ over title+comment, share ranked top-20).
- Found / verdict: **POLISH — the build is genuinely close to ship; structure matches my spec on every major point (two-state inbox→ranked, whole-chip+nudge rating, big live /10, ±0.2 calibration with 3 neighbours + Better/About right/Worse, private timestamped collapsed comment, MY TOP clipboard share, conversational empties). It clears the <10s loop and the 9-yo bar.** But four real wrinkles fail my own bars and one tone nit; none are blockers but all are cheap and worth folding before gate.
  1. **CHIP TOUCH TARGET (real a11y/9-yo miss) — index.html:1552 + buildChips 6826-6828.** The 1–10 chips are `min-width:38px;padding:7px 0` → ~30–32px tall. My own invariant is **≥44px on touch**, and these are the single most-tapped control in the feature (a child taps "8"). 32px on a row of ten packed chips = mis-taps. FIX: bump chip to `min-width:42px;padding:11px 0` (≈44px tall, still wraps to 2 rows on a narrow phone — fine). The ±0.1 ghost buttons (min-width:56px, .btn pad 10/18 ≈40px) and "Rate it" are borderline-OK; the chips are the one that actually fails.
  2. **"Done" button is a SAVE TRAP (9-yo bar) — medRateClose 1549 + closeRate 6821/6899.** A child taps chip 8, watches the big "8.0 / 10", then taps the top-right **"Done"** expecting it to commit — but `closeRate()` DISCARDS the draft; only the bottom "Save rating" commits. Two finish-looking buttons, one saves and one silently throws the score away = exactly the confusion my bar forbids. FIX (pick one, route to Kaito): (a) relabel the top-right control from "Done" to **"Cancel"** (or an ✕) so only "Save rating" reads as the commit — cleanest; or (b) make closeRate on an *unsaved-but-scored* draft commit the rating instead of dropping it. I recommend (a): "Cancel" top-right + "Save rating" bottom is the unambiguous pair. (Mikoto: "Cancel" string if (a).)
  3. **POST-SAVE FLOW AMBIGUITY — saveRate 6834-6842 + showCompare 6843-6849.** When neighbours exist, Save writes the rating, then reveals the calibration box IN-CARD with the "Save rating" button STILL sitting below it. So after the user already saved, they see Better/About right/Worse AND a "Save rating" button under it — unclear whether they must save again. The calibration nudges already autosave (applyCompare 6852) and close the card; the lingering "Save rating" button is dead weight at that moment and reads like a required second step. FIX: when the compare box is shown, **hide the "Save rating" button** (and ideally hide the comment textarea) so the only choices are the three calibration buttons — one decision on screen. Re-show Save when the card is reopened. (No new string.)
  4. **LIVE VALUE COLOR IS ALWAYS LIME — medRateVal 1551 (`color:var(--lime)`).** The big "/10" is hard-lime regardless of score, so a 2.0 and a 9.5 read with the same "good/green" affect. Minor, but in a tool whose whole point is honest self-calibration, a 3/10 glowing lime undercuts the meaning. OPTIONAL POLISH (not a blocker): tint the live value by band — red <4, amber 4–6.9, lime ≥7 (reuse the hero thresholds language we already ship). Low priority; flag only.
- Empty states (asked specifically): **GOOD, ship as-is.** To-watch empty "Nothing queued. Add something you want to watch." and ranked empty "What's the last thing you finished? Add it above, then tap Rate it." both hit my "conversational not blank dashes" spec, are factual (no hype), and the ranked one even teaches the exact next tap ("tap Rate it"). The search-miss "Nothing here matches." is clean. I would NOT sharpen them — they're already the right register. One micro-note: ranked-empty references "Rate it" which is the To-watch button label — that cross-reference only works if the user has something queued; if their list is totally empty the line still reads fine as an instruction. Keep.
- Serious-not-warm tone (asked): **mostly compliant, one nit.** Functional emoji 🎬/📥/🏆/📋 are allowed comprehension icons (tab/section/action) — fine per CLAUDE.md. The desc, "Added "{title}" to your To-watch list.", "Rated "{title}" {r}.", "Copied your ranked list — paste it anywhere." are all factual. The ONE soft spot is the **comment placeholder "Why this score? What did it make you feel?"** (1559) — "what did it make you feel?" leans warm/chatty for the serious register. RECOMMEND tightening to **"Why this score? Note it for your future self."** (keeps the private-future-self framing from my spec without the feelings-prompt). The calibration line "It sits with these — does "{title}" really belong here?" is good — decisive, not warm. (Mikoto: revised placeholder string if Kaito takes it.)
- MISSING vs my spec — worth-now vs backlog:
  • **TO WATCH→WATCHED discoverability via "Rate it" — ADEQUATE, ship.** Asked if it's discoverable enough. It is: the To-watch row's primary (filled cyan) button literally says "Rate it", and rating IS the act that promotes it to WATCHED/ranked. A child reads "Rate it" → taps → scores → it appears in Ranked. That's the right single affordance; no separate "mark watched" needed (rating-without-watching is meaningless here). Keep. No change.
  • **Comment collapsed-indicator — PRESENT (the "note" toggle only renders if m.comment exists), good.** Matches my "dim indicator if one exists, expand on tap" spec. Ship.
  • **Backlog (NOT now):** rating-band color (#4 above), and a future "sort by recently-rated" view — neither blocks item-1 ship.
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review): items 1 (chip 42px/11px ≈44px), 2 (relabel "Done"→"Cancel" OR commit-on-close), 3 (hide Save+comment while compare box shown) are the POLISH set; 4 (band color) + placeholder copy are optional. Strings for Mikoto IF taken: "Cancel" (item 2a), revised comment placeholder. All other Media Log strings lock as written.
- Still open / next: (1) Kaito folds 1–3 (the real wrinkles); I re-review against this spec. (2) Mikoto: only +1-2 new/changed strings IF Kaito takes 2a + the placeholder; the rest of the Media Log dict is final EN — she runs the 7-lang sync. (3) Akashi: Media Log stores user text (title/comment) only, no owner figures → no poison thread expected, but the FEATURE IS NEW so it needs his SAFE pass per the pipeline (esp. esc() on title/comment in renderLists — I see esc() on every sink, looks clean, but his call). (4) Hugo: new feature → wants a media_test suite + GREEN. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 9ba07d5.

## [2026-06-30] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — BUILD-SPEC: Desktop Alive (roadmap item 3) — exact timing curves + CSS/JS
- Asked: Kaito ready to build Desktop Alive (my brainstorm item 3). Produce the precise, build-ready spec for all 8 effects — exact keyframes/durations/easings/px/selectors — and be decisive IN-vs-BACKLOG for v1 (cosmetic feature, don't over-scope). Constraints (Kaito builds to these EXACTLY): all CSS/JS, NO network/external assets (Akashi); ALL motion wrapped in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` (state per effect); desktop-targeted effects (parallax + hover-lift) gated to `(hover:hover) and (min-width:1024px)` / JS pointer check, never fire on touch/phone (mobile is primary, don't drain battery); HUD tokens only; must COMPOSE with existing gridPulse / sweep / .panel slide-ins / tabMoved, not fight them. Flag i18n strings. Read-only, no index.html edit.
- Studied / referenced: GROUNDED the spec in the real file — tokens (--bg #05080f, --cyan #00e5ff, --cyan-dim #0a7e8c, --lime #7CFFB2, --line #1b3a5c, --txt-dim #6f93b5, --glow `0 0 18px rgba(0,229,255,.35)`, fonts Orbitron/Rajdhani/Share Tech Mono @import line 16). Existing motion I must not collide with: `body::before` grid @ `gridPulse 6s ease-in-out infinite` opacity .72↔1 (59-71) + base bg radial-gradients (35-39); `header.hud::after` sweep 6s linear (84-89); `.tab:hover{transform:translateY(-2px)}` (131); `.panel` fade/slideFromR/slideFromL (156-161); `tabMoved` (141); `@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*,*::before,*::after{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}` (489-491) — so a `reduce` user ALREADY gets everything killed; my job is to ADD the alive layer only under `no-preference` so I don't even rely on the wildcard, belt-and-braces. Hero = `.hero-num` (113, clamp 38-60 Orbitron900 lime, text-shadow `0 0 24px rgba(124,255,178,.32)`). Logo = `h1` (91, text-shadow var(--glow)). Active tab = `.tab.active` (132, box-shadow var(--glow)). PWA infra ALREADY EXISTS (huge): module @ 7892-7943 — `installed()` (display-mode:standalone||navigator.standalone), `beforeinstallprompt` deferred, `#installBanner`/`#installBack`/`#installBody` modal, `canShow()`, and a DESKTOP branch already in buildModalBody (7926-7929: "Click the install icon in the address bar (right side)"). manifest.webmanifest = display:standalone, NO display_override. Number count-up = my Tier-1 item A, STILL DEFERRED (parseFloat breaks on "32 000 kr" grouped + ccy suffix; needs parse-then-count w/ Akashi) — boot's "numbers count up" depends on A, noted below.
- Found / verdict: **SHIP-shaped spec. v1 = 5 effects IN (ambient pulse, polyphonic glow, 2nd scanline, card-hover-lift, cursor parallax). 3 BACKLOG/CUT (data glyphs CUT-to-backlog, boot sequence BACKLOG, WCO BACKLOG). Install nudge = NOT a new build — the desktop branch already exists; one small copy/visibility tweak only.** Rationale: the 5 IN effects are pure ambient CSS (+ one tiny rAF parallax), zero network, zero data-path, compose cleanly, and carry the entire "it breathes" payoff. The 3 cut items each carry real risk (boot annoyance / WCO manifest+drag-region surface / glyph-on-mobile no-home) disproportionate to a cosmetic tier — defer, don't gold-plate.

### EFFECT 1 — AMBIENT BACKGROUND PULSE — **IN v1**
- Carries on a NEW fixed layer `body::after` (the existing `body::before` is the grid + already runs gridPulse — don't overload it). Property animated = **opacity of a radial-gradient glow layer** (NOT hue — per constraint). 
- `body::after{content:"";position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:0;pointer-events:none;background:radial-gradient(1100px 620px at 70% -8%, rgba(0,229,255,.05), transparent 62%);opacity:.55;}`
- `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference){ body::after{ animation:ambientBreath 6s ease-in-out infinite; } }`
- `@keyframes ambientBreath{0%,100%{opacity:.40}50%{opacity:.70}}`  (a true slow sine feel via ease-in-out; ~.40↔.70 is a subtle depth swell, never a flash).
- Duration **6s** (matches gridPulse 6s so the two breathe on the same clock — coherent, not two competing tempos). z-index:0 keeps it UNDER content (.app is above) and over --bg. Reduced-motion: no animation → static .55 glow, screen still looks intentional.

### EFFECT 2 — POLYPHONIC GLOW RHYTHM — **IN v1**
- 3 elements, ONE shared keyframe, OFFSET phase delays (negative delays so all are mid-cycle at load, never a synchronized "blink on"). Animate **text-shadow / box-shadow intensity only** (glow blur+alpha), never position/size (no layout cost, never competes with the number's legibility).
- Shared: `@keyframes hudGlowPulse{0%,100%{filter:brightness(1)}50%{filter:brightness(1.18)}}` — use `filter:brightness` on the glowing element so it lifts BOTH the text and its existing text-shadow together, token-free, cheap, and it can't fight the per-element shadow values already set.
- Wrap ALL three in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference){ … }`:
  - `h1{animation:hudGlowPulse 5s ease-in-out infinite;animation-delay:0s}`  (logo)
  - `.hero-num{animation:hudGlowPulse 5s ease-in-out infinite;animation-delay:-1.7s}`  (hero number)
  - `.tab.active{animation:hudGlowPulse 5s ease-in-out infinite;animation-delay:-3.4s}`  (active tab)
- Period **5s** (deliberately ≠ the 6s ambient/grid so the layers drift in and out of phase = the "heartbeat with harmonics" polyphony, not a single thump). Offsets 0 / -1.7s / -3.4s = three evenly-spread phases across the 5s cycle. brightness 1→1.18 is SUBTLE (per the "keep it subtle" ask) — at 1.18 the lime hero gains glow without clipping to white. Reduced-motion: static, full legibility.
- NOTE on .tab.active: brightness on the active tab's cyan gradient lifts it gently; verify it doesn't fight `tabMoved` (one-shot 1.4s on reorder) — they're different properties (filter vs box-shadow/transform) and tabMoved is one-shot, so they layer fine; if Kaito sees a flash on reorder, drop the .tab.active line and keep glow on logo+hero only (2-voice still reads). Low risk.

### EFFECT 3 — SECOND (SLOWER) SCANLINE LAYER — **IN v1**
- The existing sweep (`header.hud::after`, 6s linear) is HEADER-only. The brainstorm "depth in the scan" wants a second, slower, full-viewport-feel layer. Put it on a NEW fixed pseudo-layer so it doesn't disturb header layout. Reuse `body::after`? No — that's the ambient glow. Add it as a dedicated fixed element is overkill; cleanest is a 2nd pseudo on an always-present wrapper. Use **`.app::before`** (the main app container; confirm the wrapper's class with Kaito — if it's `#app`/`.wrap`, use that) OR a single inserted `<div class="scan2">`. Spec the pseudo form:
- `.app::before{content:"";position:fixed;left:0;right:0;top:0;height:140px;pointer-events:none;z-index:1;background:linear-gradient(180deg, transparent, rgba(0,229,255,.05), transparent);opacity:.30;}`
- `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference){ .app::before{ animation:scanSlow 15s linear infinite; } }`
- `@keyframes scanSlow{0%{transform:translateY(-160px)}100%{transform:translateY(102vh)}}`  (a soft 140px-tall band drifting top→bottom once per 15s).
- Duration **15s**, opacity **.30** (exactly per brainstorm: 15s / 30%). linear so it's a steady drift, not eased. z-index:1 sits above the grid/ambient but below content text? It's a faint tint band — keep it BELOW interactive content (z-index under .app's content) so it never dims a number it crosses; if Kaito finds it muddies text, drop opacity to .20. Header's own 6s sweep + this 15s band = two scan tempos = depth. Reduced-motion: static (the band just sits at top, .30 — unobtrusive; or set `.app::before{display:none}` under reduce if a static band looks odd — Kaito's call, both acceptable).

### EFFECT 4 — CARD HOVER DEPTH — **IN v1, DESKTOP-GATED**
- Gate: `@media (hover:hover) and (pointer:fine) and (min-width:1024px){ … }` — fires ONLY on a real mouse on a ≥1024px screen; phones/tablets/touch never get it (no battery/perf hit, no sticky-hover-stuck-state on touch). ALSO wrap in no-preference (nest, or combine).
- `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) and (hover:hover) and (pointer:fine) and (min-width:1024px){ .card{transition:transform .18s cubic-bezier(.25,.1,.25,1), box-shadow .18s ease} .card:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 6px 22px rgba(0,229,255,.10), var(--glow)} }`
- Lift **2px** (per brainstorm), transition **180ms** ease-out-ish (`cubic-bezier(.25,.1,.25,1)`), glow = a soft cyan drop + the existing --glow so it reads "panel illuminated". `.card` already has border/radius/padding (164); only transform+box-shadow added, no layout reflow (transform is compositor-only). Reduced-motion OR touch OR <1024px → no transition, no lift, identical to today. (Reuses the 2px language of `.tab:hover translateY(-2px)` — consistent depth vocabulary across the HUD.)

### EFFECT 5 — CURSOR PARALLAX ON THE GRID — **IN v1, DESKTOP-GATED, JS**
- The ONLY JS effect. Moves the grid layer (`body::before`) 3–4px OPPOSITE the cursor → "looking at the HUD through glass." rAF-throttled, pointer-gated, reduced-motion-gated.
- CSS: add a transform var to the grid layer, default 0:
  `body::before{ transform:translate3d(var(--px,0px),var(--py,0px),0); transition:transform .25s ease-out; will-change:transform; }`  (the .25s ease-out smooths between rAF samples so it glides, not jitters; will-change promotes it to its own layer = cheap.)
- JS (guarded; pseudocode for Kaito — wrap in an IIFE near the other init):
  ```
  var fine = matchMedia('(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine) and (min-width:1024px)').matches;
  var moOK = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)').matches;
  if(fine && moOK){
    var tx=0,ty=0,raf=0;
    addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
      if(e.pointerType && e.pointerType!=='mouse') return;   // mouse only, ignore touch/pen
      // range ±3.5px, inverted (opposite cursor)
      tx = -((e.clientX/innerWidth)  - .5) * 7;   // → ±3.5px
      ty = -((e.clientY/innerHeight) - .5) * 7;
      if(!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(function(){
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--px', tx.toFixed(2)+'px');
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--py', ty.toFixed(2)+'px');
        raf=0;
      });
    }, {passive:true});
  }
  ```
- Range **±3.5px** (mid of the 3–4px brainstorm spec; ×7 over a -.5..+.5 normalized axis = ±3.5). INVERTED (the leading `-`) = grid shifts opposite cursor = depth-through-glass. **rAF-coalesced** (one DOM write per frame max). `{passive:true}`. Mouse-only (`pointerType` check) + the matchMedia gate = never runs on touch/phone (no listener attached at all → zero mobile cost). Reduced-motion → branch never attaches. The grid's gridPulse (opacity) is a DIFFERENT property than transform, so parallax + heartbeat coexist on body::before with no conflict.

### EFFECT 6 — DATA GLYPHS in empty space — **CUT from v1 → BACKLOG**
- DECISION: **CUT.** Reasoned, not lazy: (1) the brainstorm itself flagged "where would they even go on mobile?" — there IS no empty sidebar on phone (single-column), so this is desktop-only by nature, which means building a desktop-only decorative layer that the primary platform never sees — lowest payoff/effort ratio of the 8. (2) Faint 2–3% Share Tech Mono chars risk reading as RENDER GARBAGE / a bug to a 9-yo ("why is there grey text I can't read?") — fails the clarity bar for near-zero ambient gain once effects 1–3 already supply texture. (3) Placement is unsolved (which container's margins? does it reflow at 1024–1440?). The ambient pulse + 2nd scanline already deliver "the system is computing behind everything" without literal glyphs. **Backlog**: revisit ONLY if, after 1–5 ship, desktop ≥1440px still feels empty in the gutters — then spec a fixed, `aria-hidden`, pointer-events:none, ≤3% opacity Share Tech Mono column pinned to the viewport edge, desktop-gated. Not now.

### EFFECT 7 — BOOT SEQUENCE — **BACKLOG (not v1)**
- DECISION: **defer.** Two real reasons: (1) TRIGGER risk — the safe trigger is **installed standalone PWA ONLY, once per app-launch** (`installed()` already exists @7895). A boot animation on every BROWSER load (or every tab switch / SPA re-render) would annoy fast — exactly the "costs attention, distracts from the number" failure my own invariant 7 forbids. Gating it correctly (standalone + true cold-launch, not every hashchange) is fiddly and worth its own careful pass. (2) DEPENDENCY — the finale "every number counts up from zero" needs item **A (number count-up)**, which is STILL DEFERRED (parseFloat breaks on grouped "32 000 kr" + ccy suffix; needs parse-then-count with Akashi). Shipping boot WITHOUT the count-up amputates its best beat. So boot waits on A. **When built (spec locked now so it's ready):** trigger = `installed() && cold-launch` only, never browser, run ONCE. ~1.5s total over a full-screen `#boot` overlay (fixed, z-index top, `--bg` fill, `aria-hidden`, removed from DOM on end), all under no-preference (reduced-motion → skip the overlay entirely, show app instantly):
  - 0–250ms: black hold (`--bg`).
  - 250–650ms: grid fades in (opacity 0→1 on a boot-grid, ease-out).
  - 500–1050ms: one sweep line runs across (reuse `sweep` keyframe, single iteration).
  - 700–1150ms: logo scales+fades into existence (`transform:scale(.92)→1`, opacity 0→1, glow brightens — reuse hudGlowPulse easing).
  - 1100–1400ms: `// MRLN COMMAND CENTER — SYSTEM READY` types/fades in, Share Tech Mono, `--cyan`, letter-spacing .28em, ~13px, uppercase (matches `.eyebrow` 90).
  - 1400ms: overlay fades out (200ms), app revealed; THEN the count-up (item A) animates the real figures 0→value. Total ≈1.5s.
- The system line **`// MRLN COMMAND CENTER — SYSTEM READY`** = **Share Tech Mono**, `--cyan`, uppercase, letter-spacing ~.28em (the established eyebrow/system-label voice). This string + any boot label = NEW i18n key → @Mikoto (factual/serious; the "//" comment prefix is on-brand command-center, keep it).

### EFFECT 8 — DESKTOP INSTALL NUDGE + WINDOW CONTROLS OVERLAY
- **INSTALL NUDGE — IN v1, but it's a TWEAK not a build.** The infra ALREADY exists: `#installBanner` + modal + `beforeinstallprompt` deferred + `canShow()` (7903) + a DESKTOP branch in buildModalBody (7926-7929 "Click the install icon in the address bar (right side)"). So desktop users who haven't installed ALREADY get a banner when `deferred` fires. The brainstorm worry ("PC users don't know to look at the address bar") is HALF-solved — the modal tells them, but only after they click the banner. ONE precise ask to Kaito: ensure the `#installBanner` copy itself (the always-visible banner, not just the modal) reads clearly on desktop — currently optimized for "Get the app" on phone. RECOMMEND: on desktop+installable, the banner CTA reads "Install MRLN" and the modal's desktop step gets a tiny visual cue (an inline ⊕/address-bar glyph) so "the install icon in the address bar" is findable. **Dismissible + never-nag**: `dismissed` flag already exists (7935) — confirm it persists (currently in-memory; recommend persisting to a `prefs` flag so a dismissed nudge stays dismissed across reloads, not just the session). NOT a new component — copy + one persistence line. New/changed strings → @Mikoto (banner CTA + the desktop step cue). No new network, no asset.
- **WINDOW CONTROLS OVERLAY — BACKLOG (not v1).** Payoff (logo+streak in the titlebar, edge-to-edge HUD) is real but SMALL and desktop-installed-only (a sliver of users today); cost+risk is NOT small: needs `display_override:["window-controls-overlay"]` in manifest.webmanifest, a `titlebar-area-*` env() draggable region with `-webkit-app-region:drag` (and `no-drag` on every interactive control inside it or buttons stop clicking — a real footgun), reflow of the header into the overlay zone, and a non-WCO fallback path. That's a manifest change + a new draggable layout surface + cross-browser testing — disproportionate to a cosmetic tier. **Recommend BACKLOG**; if Osefe wants it, it's its own scoped feature (manifest + Akashi review of the manifest change + a careful drag-region pass), not folded into Desktop Alive v1.

- Specs given (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review vs this spec):
  v1 BUILD = Effects 1 (ambientBreath, body::after, 6s), 2 (hudGlowPulse, logo/hero/tab, 5s, offsets 0/-1.7/-3.4), 3 (scanSlow, 15s, .30), 4 (card hover-lift 2px/180ms, desktop+no-pref gated), 5 (cursor parallax ±3.5px inverted, rAF, mouse+desktop+no-pref gated) + the install-nudge copy/persistence tweak. EVERY motion effect wrapped in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`; effects 4+5 ALSO gated `(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine) and (min-width:1024px)` / JS pointer check. No network, no asset, HUD tokens only.

## [2026-06-30] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — RE-REVIEW: Desktop Alive v1 BUILT @ 7aa3231 → SHIP
- Asked: verify Kaito's Desktop Alive v1 build matches my BUILD-SPEC (entry above @ line 323) to the number; judge (1) all 5 IN-v1 effects vs exact durations/opacities/phases/gates, (2) the `html::before` scanline substitution (he swapped my `.app::before` to dodge a stacking-context issue — risk of dimming header text / wrong z-order?), (3) whether 3 glow voices + 2 ambient layers = too much, (4) reduced-motion + desktop/mobile gating correctness. Verdict + exact tweaks. Read-only, NO index.html edit.
- Studied / referenced: read the built CSS (index.html 73-95) + the parallax IIFE (7896-7909) byte-for-byte against my spec; re-derived the stacking model from first principles (CSS 2.1 painting order — equal z-index in the same stacking context paints in tree order). Confirmed `body` itself sets NO stacking context (no transform/opacity/filter/z-index on the `body` rule, 35-51) → `html::before`(z:1), `.wrap`(z:1, line 97), `body::before/after`(z:0, 77-81) ALL share the ROOT stacking context. Confirmed the global `reduce` wildcard kill survives (513-514 `*,*::before,*::after{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}`). Confirmed parallax matchMedia gates on BOTH `(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine) and (min-width:1024px)` AND `(prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference)` (7898-7899) → no listener on touch/reduced-motion. Checked tempos/properties for collision.
- Found / verdict: **SHIP @ 7aa3231.** Every one of the 5 effects matches my spec to the decimal — nothing off:
  1. Ambient breath — `body::after` radial 1100×620 @70%/-8% rgba(.05) base opacity .55; `ambientBreath{.40↔.70}` 6s ease-in-out (77-78,82,86). EXACT.
  2. Polyphonic glow — `hudGlowPulse{brightness 1→1.18}` 5s on h1(0)/.hero-num(-1.7s)/.tab.active(-3.4s) (83,88-90). EXACT (5s≠6s drift preserved, evenly-spread phases).
  3. 2nd scanline — `scanSlow{-160px→102vh}` 15s linear, 140px band rgba(.05) cyan, opacity .30 (79,84,87). EXACT; selector substituted `html::before` (see below).
  4. Card hover-lift — translateY(-2px)+`0 6px 22px rgba(0,229,255,.10),var(--glow)`, .18s cubic-bezier(.25,.1,.25,1), gated no-pref+hover+pointer:fine+min-width:1024px (92-94). EXACT.
  5. Cursor parallax — `-tx*7`/`-ty*7` = inverted ±3.5px, rAF-coalesced (one DOM write/frame), `pointerType!=='mouse'` early-return, `{passive:true}`, try/caught, double-gated (7896-7909). EXACT. `body::before` carries `translate3d(var(--px,0px),var(--py,0px),0)` + transition .25s ease-out + will-change (81). EXACT.
- Q2 — `html::before` substitution: **KEEP. Correct call, arguably better than my `.app::before`.** The stacking math holds: `body` makes no stacking context, so `html::before`(z:1) and `.wrap`(z:1) compare directly in the root context; equal z-index → tree order; `html`'s `::before` paints as html's first generated box, BEFORE `body` and all its descendants incl `.wrap`'s content. So the .05-alpha cyan band paints UNDER every number/label — it physically cannot dim header text. My original `.app::before` would have needed `.wrap` (the real wrapper) and risked exactly the stacking-context muddle Kaito avoided. No change.
- Q3 — too much at once? **No — balanced, not a disco. SHIP.** Distinct periods (grid 6s / ambient 6s same-clock / glow 5s drift / scanline 15s / sweep 6s) on distinct properties (opacity / brightness-filter / transform) → nothing fights the same property on the same element; tabMoved (box-shadow+transform, one-shot) vs .tab.active glow (filter) confirmed non-colliding as specced. All magnitudes sub-threshold: breath .40↔.70 on a .05-alpha base = a faint depth swell; brightness 1→1.18 = gentle; scanline .30 on .05 cyan = a whisper. The only zone with 3 simultaneous effects is the hero (glow-brightness + ambient-breath behind + parallax-grid under), but different layers/properties + all subtle = "alive," not "busy."
- Q4 — reduced-motion + gating: **CORRECT, belt-and-braces.** Every Desktop Alive animation is DECLARED only inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference)` (never emitted for reduce users) AND the global reduce-wildcard (513-514) would kill it anyway. Parallax JS gates on no-preference too → no listener attached. Mobile: effects 4+5 require fine-pointer+≥1024px; touch gets no card transition and the parallax branch returns before `addEventListener` → phones attach zero listeners, pay zero cost. Exactly my mobile-perf + a11y invariants. Kaito's 8/8 browser check (1440px: all anims + --px set + card transition present; 390px touch: parallax NOT active, no card-hover transition) matches what the values predict.
- Specs given: **NONE required — SHIP as-is, no blocker.** TWO OPTIONAL dial-backs (neither blocks, Kaito's call, I'd ship without them): (a) the 15s scanline is the only element that visibly TRAVELS across the header — if on a real 1440px screen it reads as a faint moving bar over the hero rather than ambient depth, drop `html::before` opacity .30→.22; (b) if the .tab.active brightness ever flashes against tabMoved on reorder, drop the `.tab.active` glow line (2-voice logo+hero still reads) — low risk, already flagged in my spec, leave unless seen.
- Still open / next: (1) Desktop Alive v1 is Arthur-SHIP @ 7aa3231 → proceeds down the pipeline (Akashi SAFE on the new CSS+rAF — pure, no network/data/storage, manifest UNCHANGED; Mikoto NO new strings for v1 ambient; Hugo GREEN; Osefe ship). (2) BACKLOG still parked: data-glyphs CUT, boot sequence (waits on item A count-up parse-then-count w/ Akashi), WCO (own scoped feature), install-nudge copy/persistence tweak (flag me when Kaito picks it up). (3) Flag me to finalize the boot spec when item A is greenlit. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 7aa3231.

## [2026-07-01] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — BUILD-SPEC: Save Safety (whole-app fail-loud + always-accessible open/copy/install)
- Asked: full UX build-spec for Save Safety — (1) the loud "not saving" banner (placement/copy/color, coexist with saveBadge), (2) the always-accessible open/copy/install affordance (where it lives so it's ALWAYS reachable "through the app", incl lock screen), (3) per-browser "Open in browser" guidance mirroring lk-guide style, (4) cohesion w/ existing install-banner/lk-guide patterns, (5) v1 vs backlog. Osefe directive verbatim: "make it so that it IS always accessible to open link even through the app." Honest constraint: a web page CANNOT force an iOS webview to jump to Safari; affordance can only show+copy/share the link, offer Install/A2HS, and show "⋯ → Open in browser" steps. Read-only, no index.html edit. Akashi owns the detection contract (durability probe); I own placement/copy/treatment.
- Studied / referenced: existing infra I MUST reuse — `.install-banner` (CSS 396-413, markup 878-883: ib-icon/ib-text/ib-go filled cyan/ib-x), install modal `#installBack`+`buildModalBody()` (8071-8096) which ALREADY branches iOS-Safari / iOS-non-Safari / Android / desktop step lists; `.lk-guide` (CSS 415-422, markup 715-730: lk-guide-h + ol.lk-guide-steps, ios/android flanking the lockcard, hidden when standalone via lockInstallGuides 5177-5180); `saveBadge` (markup 1826, CSS 354 `.savebar .badge` Share Tech Mono 11px txt-dim pill; states: 'loaded' / '● unsaved — auto-saved locally' amber / '● storage full…' red / '● try mode…' amber TEMPLATE_MODE / '✓ exported…' lime); autosave() 2029-2040 + _warnStorageFull() 2017-2021 (the fail-loud precedent); the EXISTING durability probe `navigator.storage.persist()` 8039 that reveals `#persistWarn` (1726, amber .note) on denial — THIS is the seed of Akashi's contract, already wired. Tokens: --amber #ffb547, --red #ff5c7c, --cyan #00e5ff, --panel #0b1422, --panel2 #0e1a2e, --line #1b3a5c, --txt-dim #6f93b5. Header order: hero → status → mealBack… localebar 893. Web refs (gluttony): iOS webview escape patterns (Snapchat/IG in-app browser "⋯ → Open in Safari/Chrome" — the ONLY escape a page can instruct, can't force); PWA A2HS eviction (WebKit 7-day script-writable-storage eviction for non-installed non-persisted sites — the exact mechanism that ate Osefe's titles); "fail loud on non-durable storage" (banking/notes apps that detect private mode + warn before data loss, e.g. localStorage try/catch + storage-estimate probes); Nielsen "visibility of system status" (silent data loss is the cardinal sin). Confirmed the honest constraint against Apple WebKit docs: no `window.open`/scheme from inside an iOS webview reliably breaks out; the copyable canonical link + the "⋯" instructions are the real deliverable.
- Found / verdict: **BUILD-SPEC (decisive). The fix is a 3-part system, all reusing existing patterns:** (A) a persistent LOUD banner when durability is not guaranteed, (B) an always-present "Open / Copy link / Install" control that lives in BOTH the localebar (in-app) AND the lock screen (first landing), (C) the per-webview "⋯ → Open in browser" guide reusing `.lk-guide` + `buildModalBody()`'s existing detection branches. Split IN v1 vs BACKLOG below. This is a TRUST feature — treatment reads solid/serious, never apologetic-cute; amber for "not durable" (warning, act now), RED reserved for actual loss/quota (already in use). The banner is dismissible-per-session but RE-ARMS on reload (data loss risk doesn't go away because you tapped ✕ once). Full copy + tokens + placement handed to Kaito (below + in TEAM-CHAT). Depends on Akashi's `window.__storageDurable()` contract (probe: private-mode/webview/persist-denied → false); I specced the UI to consume a single boolean+reason string so his detection and my UI decouple cleanly.
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review; Akashi owns the detection boolean; Mikoto owns every new string):
  1. LOUD BANNER `#saveSafetyBanner` — new element, TOP of app content, ABOVE the localebar (first thing after unlock; sits above the hero so it can't be scrolled past unseen). REUSE `.install-banner` structure but AMBER-themed (new modifier `.install-banner.warn`: left border 3px --amber, ib-icon ⚠, bg rgba(255,181,71,.07), ib-text b in --amber). Shown ONLY when `window.__storageDurable()===false`. Copy (9-yo, serious, factual): b="This browser won't keep your data" / span="Anything you add here can disappear when you close or refresh. Install MRLN or open it in your browser to save for good." CTA button (ib-go, cyan filled) = "Fix this" → opens the SAME `#installBack` modal but in its Save-Safety mode (see #3). ✕ (ib-x) dismisses for the SESSION only; re-arms on next load (do NOT persist dismissal — the risk persists). It coexists with saveBadge: banner = the WHY + fix (durability), saveBadge stays the per-save heartbeat (saved/unsaved/quota) — two different jobs, no overlap. When durable, banner never renders (display:none default).
  2. ALWAYS-ACCESSIBLE AFFORDANCE — the core of Osefe's ask, must be reachable even when saving works. Add ONE compact iconbtn to the localebar (reuse `.localebar .iconbtn` 683-685, cyan pill Share Tech Mono 12px), label "↗ Open / Save" (functional ↗ only, no hype), `#openSaveBtn`, placed as the LAST .lc-adjacent control in the localebar so it's on every screen (localebar is app-global, renders above every tab). Tapping it opens the `#installBack` modal in Save-Safety mode (#3). ON THE LOCK SCREEN: add a matching text link UNDER `.lk-foot` (713) — `#lkOpenSave`, Share Tech Mono 12px --cyan, "↗ Open in browser · Copy link · Install" — because the lock screen is the #1 in-app-browser LANDING (they open the link from Snap/IG and hit the gate first). It reuses the same modal. So the link is accessible at the gate AND inside the app, durable or not.
  3. THE MODAL — extend the EXISTING `#installBack` / `buildModalBody()` (8071), do NOT build a new modal. Rename its intent to "Open & Save MRLN". Three stacked zones, top→bottom by priority:
     ZONE 1 (always) COPY LINK — a read-only row showing the canonical URL (sagemrln.github.io/my-first-repo/ + current #F-id if present, from location.href) + a filled cyan "📋 Copy link" button (reuse .ib-now full-width). On copy → button text→"✓ Copied" 1.5s (mirror the saveBadge ✓ pattern). This is the "always openable link" guarantee — even in a webview where nothing else works, they can copy + paste into a real browser. Sub-note (dim): "Paste this in Safari or Chrome to open MRLN where your data is safe."

Media Log: panel #media index.html:1646-1692 (add row 1650-1654, To-watch card 1658-1664, Ranked card 1666-1672, rate card 1674-1691), MEDIALOG module 7011-7141 end-to-end (items/watched/ranked/add 7022 {id,title,status:'towatch',rating:null,comment,added,rated}/del/openRate/saveRate/showCompare/applyCompare/share/renderLists 7094 — the two .filter()s at 7097 To-watch + 7105 Ranked are the filter hook; matchQ 7020 title+comment; empty-states 7102/7106). Schema: DEFAULTS.media:[] (2008), migrate media:(pick&&pick.media)||[] (2031), exportBlank nulls STATE.media (4921) + restores (4947) → new fields can't leak (Akashi's strip already covers). esc() on every title/comment sink confirmed (7099/7109/7112/7075). Reusable toggle: the cardio `.prSeg` ghost-pill pattern (active cyan-dim bg + cyan border + #eaffff, min-height 44px, flex:1) — reviewed Jun-30, THE model for the flip control. Tokens --cyan #00e5ff / --cyan-dim #0a7e8c / --line #1b3a5c / --txt-dim #6f93b5 / .item-row (324) / .btn.ghost (319). My own Jun-30 IMDb note (log line 626-627): I'd floated /find?q=; Osefe's ask uses the newer /find/?q= — adopting his path. Cursor-wake: Desktop Alive CSS 73-95 (body::after ambient z:0, body::before grid z:0, html::before scan z:1), `.wrap{position:relative;z-index:1;max-width:1100px;margin:0 auto}` (97) → CONFIRMS wide-screen black gutters + the centered content occludes the middle at z:1. Lock particle canvas #lkfx (my Jun-30 spec, built 75f03b5→ceiling 140) = the exact discipline for the wake canvas (DPR cap 2, capped count, rAF, stop-when-idle, reduced-motion skip). Web/craft refs from memory: Letterboxd/MAL type+genre tagging (segmented type filter is the established media-tracker pattern), IMDb /find/?q= covers film/TV/game (one endpoint, no branching), iOS segmented control (3-way, no dropdown = 9-yo simple), water-ripple canvas trails (expanding concentric ring + alpha-decay + additive blend = the standard "wake" idiom; throttle-by-distance-traveled = continuous-on-drag/stop-when-still).
- Found / verdict: **TWO BUILD-SPECS (decisive, both grounded, both reuse existing patterns → zero new visual language).**
  A) MEDIA LOG v2: (1) TYPE+FILTER — `#medFilter` segmented All·Films·Shows·Games at top of list stack (reuse .prSeg), filters BOTH lists via +`matchType(m)` on 7097/7105; Games filter = the "video-game tier". Default All. ADD-row 3-way type picker (Film·Show·Game, .prSeg mini, default Show). Store `type:'film'|'show'|'game'`, absent⇒'show', NO migration. (2) GENRE — free-text `#medGenre` on add row + `#medGenreEdit` in rate card, stored raw, shown as dim Share Tech Mono `<small>` after title in both lists (only if set), feeds matchQ. i18n-free (user's own word). (3) IMDb LINK-OUT — `↗ IMDb` .btn.ghost, LEFTMOST action per row (before note/edit/✕), opens `https://www.imdb.com/find/?q=<encodeURIComponent(title)>` in new tab; Akashi owns rel/target (spec'd noopener noreferrer), I own visual; all 3 types use it (IMDb covers games). App fetches nothing. (4) SEED — on TOTALLY-empty list, tap-to-add suggestion chips UNDER (not replacing) the empty-state, add to To-watch UNRATED (user rates themselves — a pre-baked score = my taste in their list). 8 cross-genre shows: Breaking Bad, Game of Thrones, The Office, Attack on Titan, Chernobyl, Planet Earth, Stranger Things, The Last of Us. Chips vanish on first add. Curated/offline, no network.
  B) DESKTOP ALIVE v2 CURSOR-WAKE: full-viewport `<canvas id="wakefx">` fixed inset:0 pointer-events:none z-index:0 — BEHIND `.wrap`(z:1) so centered content OCCLUDES the middle → ripples visible only in gutters (occlusion, NOT a CSS mask — confirmed the approach). Gated `(hover:hover) and (pointer:fine) and (min-width:1024px)` AND `no-preference` → NO listener/canvas otherwise. Physics: throttled pointermove (mouse-only), spawn every ≥45ms OR ≥22px traveled; each ring birth 6px→max 46px, +1.6px/frame (~0.4s life), lineWidth 1.4→0.5px, alpha 0.42→0 linear, stroke rgba(0,229,255,a), additive 'lighter'. Rings trail the path = wake not dots. Perf: rAF runs ONLY while ripples>0, cancels when last dies, re-arms on move (no always-on loop); DPR cap min(dpr,2); hard cap 60 rings (drop oldest); resize-debounced. Zero data/network/storage. v1 = single ring; BACKLOG = double-ring, hue-drift, radial displacement-fill.
- Specs given: both full-detail in TEAM-CHAT (the two [Jul-01] Arthur posts) + above. Build order: Media Log v2 FIRST, cursor-wake second (Osefe).

Token CSS: `.btn` pad 10/18 (314), `.btn.ghost` (319), `.item-row` gap12 pad11/14 (324), `.empty` (328). Cross-checked against the .prSeg model I specced (1498-1500 flex:1, NO padding override → inherits .btn 40px). MEASURED live in Chromium @390px (arthur_medshot.mjs/arthur_rankrow.mjs/arthur_tw.mjs): typeBtn/filterBtn=32px, IMDb=23px, Rate/edit/del=27px, addBtn=40px. Ranked row title `.meta`=**41px** (title truncates to "Eld…", genre wraps 3 lines) while imdb65+edit49+✕30+rating28 hold full width. To-watch long title=**105px** of 344 (truncates "The Lord of…"). Viewed arthur_med_phone.png + arthur_med_empty.png.
- Found / verdict: **POLISH-then-ship. ONE real SHIP-blocker (title space starvation — hierarchy inversion), rest is POLISH. The seed first-run and both segmented controls are genuinely GOOD.**
  BLOCKER B1 — TITLE LOSES THE FIGHT FOR SPACE (both lists, worst on Ranked). The single most important element (the title) is compressed to 41px on Ranked / 105px on To-watch while fixed-width secondary actions (↗IMDb 65px, edit 49, ✕ 30, rating 28) hold their size. Result: "Elden Ring"→"Eld…", "The Lord of the Rings"→"The Lord of…", and the DIM genre sub-line wraps to 3 lines under a 3-char title = hierarchy inverted (secondary tag gets 3 lines, primary title gets 3 chars). The `.meta{flex:1;min-width:0}` collapses because nothing forces a floor and the action cluster is unshrinkable. This fails the 9-yo "what is this row?" bar. Ranked packs 6-7 children into one 390px flex row — too many.
  GOOD (say why): (a) SEED first-run — arthur_med_empty.png reads clean and self-explanatory; chips render tidy, "New here? Tap a few to start your list:" is plain + serious (no hype), chips vanish on first add, added UNRATED so the score stays the user's. A 9-yo gets it. SHIP. (b) TWO SEGMENTED CONTROLS distinguishable — type picker (Film/Show/Game) and filter (All/Films/Shows/Games) share ONE paint language via paintSeg (cyan-dim fill + cyan border + #eaffff text) so they read as one family, and the search field + the "To watch/Ranked" cards physically separate them (~76px + a card gap) so they're never confused. Active-state affordance is clear on both (filled vs ghost). SHIP. (c) IMDb prominence — `.btn.ghost` 11px is correctly quieter than the filled cyan "Rate it" and the lime rating number; it's discoverable, doesn't compete. The CONCEPT is right (it's only the width-math in B1 that hurts it). (d) sub-line legibility — Share Tech Mono 11px txt-dim on panel = ~5.7:1, PASS, and "Show · Anime" reads instantly WHEN it has room.
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review):
  B1 (BLOCKER) — protect the title, shrink the row's action load. THREE coordinated moves:
    (1) Give `.meta` a real floor: on the media rows set `min-width:0` (keep) BUT add `flex:1 1 auto` AND a hard `min-width:120px` on the title wrapper so it can't collapse below a readable ~14-char width; let the ACTION cluster wrap under the title instead of crushing it. Cleanest: wrap the per-row actions in a single `<div class="med-actions" style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex:0 0 auto;margin-left:auto">…</div>` and let THAT div wrap to a 2nd line (`flex-wrap` on the row already present on Ranked) below the title when space is tight — title always gets the full width, actions drop beneath. 
    (2) DEMOTE ↗IMDb from an always-visible button to a lighter affordance so it stops eating 65px on every row. Options, pick one: (a) make it icon-only "↗" (drop the "IMDb" word) with an aria-label/title="Find on IMDb" — recovers ~35px/row; OR (b) show it only in the RATE card / on row-tap, not inline on every row. My pref: (a) icon-only ↗ inline — keeps discovery, frees the width. Confirm the a11y label wording with Mikoto.
    (3) On Ranked, the note toggle + edit + ✕ + IMDb + rating is a lot; once actions wrap under the title (move 1) this is fine, but verify at 360px (smallest common phone) the action row doesn't itself wrap to 3 lines — if it does, icon-only IMDb (move 2a) + keeping edit/✕ as glyphs is the release config.
  P1 (POLISH) — tap targets under 44px. typeBtn/filterBtn=32, IMDb=23, Rate/edit/del=27. My spec called the .prSeg model which is 44px (flex:1, no padding override). The build added `padding:7px 0` which shrank them. FIX: on `.medTypeBtn`/`.medFilterBtn` set `min-height:44px` (drop the `padding:7px 0`, let flex center) — matches the cardio prSeg floor I specced. For the row action buttons (IMDb/Rate/edit/✕) bump to `min-height:36px` minimum (40 ideal); they're secondary but 23-27px is below the honest-touch floor. Not a ship-blocker on its own but it compounds B1's mis-taps.
  P2 (POLISH) — Ranked row, once actions wrap, keep the rating `<b>` (lime 10.0) adjacent to the title, NOT trailing the action cluster — the score is the 2nd-most-important datum after the title and should sit with it, not float among buttons. Place order: rank# · title+sub-line · rating · [actions wrap below].
- Strings for Mikoto: IF IMDb goes icon-only (B1 move 2a) → new aria-label/title "Find on IMDb" (functional, serious). No other new strings; seed titles stay raw proper nouns (data-i18n-skip, correct).
