## [2026-07-01] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — BUILD-SPEC: PREMIUM LOADING / COLD-BOOT screen (first-paint beat before the lock gate)
- Asked: Osefe wants the app's FIRST impression on open to feel premium — a "living, breathing" boot/splash BEFORE the lock screen. Produce a precise director-only BUILD-SPEC (I don't code; Kaito builds): exact layers/motion curves/timings (ms+easing), particle/canvas budget w/ phone-vs-desktop caps, colors (existing CSS vars), the boot-order integration point, the teardown chokepoint, reduced-motion fallback, copy strings (minimal/serious, flag i18n), the loading↔lock relationship, "why premium" rationale, and what I'm deliberately NOT doing. HARD RAILS: living+breathing but SERIOUS (no hype/emoji, factual, game-grade cold-boot not a party); pure decoration ZERO data (no poison, no network, system/bundled fonts only); MUST NOT fake-delay usability (overlap real readiness, short min on-screen, graceful fade, exit on a real event not a long hardcoded timer); teardown to zero cost like __lockFX; prefers-reduced-motion gated w/ calm static fallback; responsive + DPR-capped + notch-safe.
- Studied / referenced: BOOT PATH byte-verified — the `<script>` runs at parse; `LOCK.boot()` fires SYNCHRONOUSLY at index.html:5229 (NOT wrapped in DOMContentLoaded/load); boot() 5129 adds `lk-noscroll`, wires lkBtn/lkInput, then either auto-unlocks (saved valid key → `verify(saved).then(...)` ASYNC → unlock()→__lockFX.stop()) or leaves the gate up; line 5231 starts __lockFX if still locked. `#lockScreen` (markup 705, FIRST body child before `.wrap` 898) = fixed inset:0 z-index:100000 opaque (radial + var(--bg)); `#lockScreen.unlocked{display:none}` = INSTANT hide, no overlay fade today. `<html>`/`<body>` first-paint bg = `--bg #05080f` + theme-color `#03070e`. So TODAY first paint IS the lock screen with its wordmark boot-glitch (lkBoot 1.1s steps(1) once → lkLogoGlow); there is NO splash before it. The lock screen ALREADY carries a full living field: self-contained grid (#lockScreen::before 40px .05 + radial-mask + parallax), breath (::after lkBreath 7s .35↔.65), scan band (.lk-scan lkScan 9s .22), and the HEADLINE capped cyan particle canvas #lkfx (my Jun-30 spec built 75f03b5, cap `min(round(W*H/4600),140)` ≈72/phone, DPR≤2, additive 'lighter', rAF, stop()=cancelRAF+removeListener+clearRect on unlock, matchMedia reduce-skip). My own DEFERRED boot spec (Desktop Alive Effect 7, log line 385-392): ~1.5s cold-boot, black→grid→sweep→logo→"// MRLN COMMAND CENTER — SYSTEM READY"→count-up; I'd deferred it behind item A (count-up) + a safe standalone-only trigger. Refs (gluttony): Mass Effect / Cyberpunk 2077 cold-boot (black→scanline sweep→wordmark resolves→"system online", ~1.2-1.8s, never a spinner), Destiny 2 character-select restraint (slow drift never flashes), Stripe/Linear/Apple app-open discipline (the splash EXITS the instant content is ready — it overlaps load, never gates it), iOS launch-screen contract (static first-frame that dissolves into app), Material motion "expressive but honest" (a splash that outlives readiness reads as SLOW = the opposite of premium). WCAG: wordmark #eaffff on --bg ≈ 15:1, cyan hairline decor-only.
- Found / verdict: **BUILD-SPEC — decisive. The premium move is a LIGHTWEIGHT pre-lock COLD-BOOT beat that fades DIRECTLY INTO the lock screen; NOT a second particle canvas.** The single most important architectural call, and the whole point of "coherence": the lock screen is ALREADY the flagship living surface (grid+breath+scan+72-mote canvas+wordmark glitch). Stacking a second heavy additive canvas splash in front of it = two full rAF canvas fields back-to-back on cold start = jank on a mid phone + redundant + it would UPSTAGE the lock field it's about to hand off to. So the boot layer is deliberately CANVAS-FREE — it's a ~900ms CSS-only "machine coming alive" sweep (dark field → hairline grid ignites → a single scan sweep → the MRLN wordmark resolves out of a glitch → one system line) that then CROSS-FADES into the lock screen, whose living field (and its ONE canvas) is the payoff the boot was promising. One canvas total, ever. The boot re-uses the lock's exact visual language (same grid 40px/.05, same wordmark, same Share Tech Mono system-line voice) so it reads as ONE continuous cold-boot that happens to reveal the gate — not two screens.

### RELATIONSHIP TO THE LOCK SCREEN (the coherence decision, justified)
- **Loading FADES DIRECTLY INTO the lock screen (continuous living field). It is NOT a distinct heavy second beat.** Reason: the lock field already exists and is opaque; the boot's job is to make the FIRST ~0.9s feel like a machine powering on, then dissolve to reveal the gate that's already alive. The wordmark is the through-line — the boot resolves "MRLN" from a glitch, the lock card shows "MRLN" already settled → the eye reads one continuous power-on. Hand-off = the boot overlay cross-fades out (opacity→0, 260ms) revealing #lockScreen underneath (which was there all along, painting its own field). Because #lockScreen is body's first child and already painted, there is NO second paint cost — the boot overlay simply lifts.
- **One canvas, ever.** The boot uses ZERO canvas. Only #lkfx (the lock's existing mote field) runs. No back-to-back canvases.

### BOOT-ORDER INTEGRATION POINT (exact)
- The boot overlay `#boot` is the FIRST child of `<body>` (before #lockScreen at 705), so it paints on frame 1 over everything (its own z-index 100001 > lockScreen 100000). It ships in the HTML STATIC (not JS-injected) so it's on the very first paint with no flash of unstyled lock screen. Its inner content (wordmark + system line) is static markup; CSS animations auto-run on load.
- Trigger to START: nothing — it's just there, visible, on first paint (that's the premium first-frame).
- Trigger to EXIT (the anti-fake-delay contract): the boot dismisses on `max(READY, MIN_MS)` where READY = the moment `LOCK.boot()` has run + the app's DOM is interactive, and MIN_MS = 900ms minimum on-screen. Concretely: at the END of the existing sync init block (right after line 5231 where __lockFX starts), call `window.__bootFX.ready()`. `__bootFX` records its own start timestamp at parse; `.ready()` schedules the exit for `Math.max(0, 900 - (now - startTs))` ms later, then runs the exit. So a fast device that's ready in 200ms still shows the boot for the full 900ms floor (premium, deliberate), and a slow device that takes 1.4s to parse dismisses ~immediately after ready (never ADDS delay). MIN floor 900ms, exit fade 260ms → worst added time on a fast device ≈ 1.16s, which is the Cyberpunk/Mass-Effect cold-boot length, not a spinner.
- IMPORTANT: the boot must NOT block the auto-unlock. Auto-unlock is async (verify().then). Sequence for a returning user w/ a valid key: frame1 boot shows → sync init runs → __bootFX.ready() schedules exit at 900ms → meanwhile verify() resolves async → unlock() hides #lockScreen (display:none) UNDER the boot overlay → boot cross-fades out at 900ms revealing the ALREADY-UNLOCKED app. So a returning user sees: cold-boot → app (the lock gate flashes for 0 frames because it unlocked while hidden behind the boot). A first-time / expired user sees: cold-boot → lock gate revealed. Both correct, both premium.

### LAYERS & TIMING (CSS-only, all under `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`)
`#boot{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:100001;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:14px;background:var(--bg);padding:max(24px,env(safe-area-inset-top)) 24px max(24px,env(safe-area-inset-bottom));pointer-events:none}` (opaque --bg = seamless with lockScreen bg + theme-color; env() = notch-safe; pointer-events:none so it never eats a tap even if exit lags).
- L1 GRID IGNITE — a `#boot::before` reusing the lock grid verbatim: `content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background-image:linear-gradient(rgba(0,229,255,.05) 1px,transparent 1px),linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(0,229,255,.05) 1px,transparent 1px);background-size:40px 40px;-webkit-mask-image:radial-gradient(120% 90% at 50% 42%,transparent 0%,transparent 20%,#000 78%);mask-image:same;opacity:0;animation:bootGrid 620ms cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1) 60ms forwards`. `@keyframes bootGrid{from{opacity:0;transform:scale(1.04)}to{opacity:1;transform:scale(1)}}` — the HUD substrate powers on (settles from a 4% over-scale = "focus pulling in"). 40px/.05/same mask = identical to #lockScreen::before → literal continuity.
- L2 SCAN SWEEP — a `#boot::after` single-pass band: `content:"";position:absolute;left:0;right:0;height:2px;top:0;background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,rgba(0,229,255,.55),transparent);box-shadow:0 0 18px rgba(0,229,255,.4);opacity:0;animation:bootSweep 720ms cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1) 240ms forwards`. `@keyframes bootSweep{0%{opacity:0;transform:translateY(0)}12%{opacity:1}100%{opacity:0;transform:translateY(70vh)}}` — ONE cyan scan line runs down the field once (the "reading the machine" beat), fades as it clears. Single pass, not a loop.
- L3 WORDMARK RESOLVE — the centered wordmark `.boot-logo` (Orbitron 900, letter-spacing .16em, #eaffff, text-shadow var(--glow) — IDENTICAL to `.lockcard .lk-logo` so it's the SAME wordmark): `font-size:clamp(30px,9vw,44px);opacity:0;animation:bootLogoIn 520ms cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1) 380ms forwards, bootLogoGlitch 420ms steps(2) 380ms 1`. `@keyframes bootLogoIn{from{opacity:0;filter:blur(6px);letter-spacing:.34em}to{opacity:1;filter:blur(0);letter-spacing:.16em}}` (resolves from blurred/wide → sharp/settled = "coming into focus"). `@keyframes bootLogoGlitch{0%{clip-path:inset(0 0 62% 0);transform:translateX(-2px)}50%{clip-path:inset(48% 0 20% 0);transform:translateX(2px)}100%{clip-path:inset(0 0 0 0);transform:none}}` — a 2-step clip/translate jitter that settles clean (mirrors the lock's lkBoot glitch language, one-shot). Lands at .16em = exactly the lock logo's tracking → the two wordmarks are visually identical at hand-off.
- L4 SYSTEM LINE — under the wordmark, `.boot-sys` (Share Tech Mono, `--cyan`, uppercase, letter-spacing .28em, clamp(10px,2.6vw,12.5px)): `opacity:0;animation:bootSysIn 400ms ease-out 720ms forwards`. `@keyframes bootSysIn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(4px)}to{opacity:.85;transform:none}}`. Text: `// SYSTEM ONLINE` (the "//" command-center prefix matches lk-sub "// SECURE ACCESS · MONTHLY KEY" and the eyebrow voice). Serious, factual, no hype.
- L5 EXIT — JS-driven at `max(ready,900ms)`: `#boot` gets `.boot-out` → `transition:opacity 260ms ease-out;opacity:0`, then on transitionend (or a 320ms fallback timeout) `#boot.remove()` (or display:none). Cross-fade reveals #lockScreen (or the already-unlocked app) beneath. The GRID doesn't blink out — it dissolves, and the lock's own grid is behind it at the same 40px/.05, so the substrate appears continuous through the fade.
- Total choreography: 60ms grid → 240ms sweep → 380ms wordmark+glitch → 720ms system line → hold to 900ms → 260ms fade. ≈1.16s worst-case on an already-ready device.

### CANVAS / PARTICLE BUDGET
- **BOOT = ZERO canvas, ZERO particles.** Deliberate (see relationship decision). The only canvas in the whole cold-boot is the lock screen's existing #lkfx, which starts at 5231 as it does today and is the "alive" payoff the boot fades into. Phone cap ≈72 / desktop ceil 140 — UNCHANGED, not touched by this feature. No new DPR math. This is the coherence win: one canvas, handed off, never two.

### TEARDOWN CHOKEPOINT (zero cost once gone, exactly like __lockFX)
- Mirror the __lockFX pattern with a tiny `window.__bootFX` IIFE:
  ```
  window.__bootFX = (function(){
    var start = (window.performance && performance.now) ? performance.now() : Date.now();
    var done=false, MIN=900, FADE=260;
    function kill(){ var el=document.getElementById('boot'); if(el){ el.classList.add('boot-out'); var gone=function(){ if(el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el); }; el.addEventListener('transitionend', gone, {once:true}); setTimeout(gone, FADE+80); } }   // remove from DOM = zero rAF/paint/listener after
    function ready(){ if(done) return; done=true; var now=(window.performance&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now(); setTimeout(kill, Math.max(0, MIN-(now-start))); }
    // safety net: if init throws before ready() is called, never trap the user behind the boot
    setTimeout(function(){ if(!done) ready(); }, 4000);
    return { ready:ready };
  })();
  ```
- No rAF at all (pure CSS anims, GPU-composited opacity/transform/filter). Teardown = removeChild → the element, its ::before/::after animations, and the transitionend listener all vanish; nothing persists. Even lighter than __lockFX (no canvas to clear, no resize listener). The 4000ms safety net guarantees a boot can NEVER strand the user if the sync init errors before `.ready()` — it self-dismisses. Call `window.__bootFX.ready()` once, at the end of the sync init (right after line 5231).

### REDUCED-MOTION FALLBACK (calm static, still branded)
- ALL five animations are declared ONLY inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`, AND the global reduce wildcard (513-514 `*,*::before,*::after{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}`) kills anything that leaks — belt-and-braces, same as Desktop/Lock Alive.
- Under reduce: `#boot` shows a STATIC frame — grid at full opacity (no ignite), no sweep, wordmark fully resolved at .16em, system line at .85 opacity. i.e. the END-STATE of every animation, painted immediately. Still handsome, still the wordmark + "// SYSTEM ONLINE", zero motion.
- The EXIT still happens (it's JS/`__bootFX`, not the reduce wildcard — but the fade transition is killed by the wildcard, so for reduce users set the exit to a hard `display:none`/remove with NO fade). Net for reduce: a calm static boot card for `max(ready,900ms)`, then it's simply gone. No jarring flash because #lockScreen bg == #boot bg == --bg.
- Belt-and-braces so a reduce user isn't stuck staring at a static card longer than needed: keep the 900ms floor (it's short, and a still branded frame for <1s is fine), OR Kaito may drop MIN to ~400ms under reduce if it feels long — his call, non-blocking. I'd keep 900 for consistency.

### RESPONSIVE / NOTCH / DPR
- Layout is a centered flex column → phone/tablet/desktop identical, self-centering, no breakpoints needed. Wordmark `clamp(30px,9vw,44px)`, system line `clamp(10px,2.6vw,12.5px)` — scale with viewport, never clip. `padding:max(24px,env(safe-area-inset-*))` → respects notches/home-indicator. No canvas → no DPR concern in the boot. Grid mask centered at 50% 42% (slightly above center so the wordmark sits in the calm hole, like the lock's 50% 35%).

### COPY STRINGS (minimal, serious — flag i18n)
- ONE string: `// SYSTEM ONLINE` (the .boot-sys line). `data-i18n="// SYSTEM ONLINE"`, Share Tech Mono uppercase, factual/serious, "//" command-center prefix matching the app voice. → **@Mikoto: 1 new key, 7 langs.** Length-sensitive but trivial (2 words, centered, no wrap risk at clamp size). The wordmark "MRLN" is a proper noun, NOT translated (data-i18n-skip). No other text. (This is the SAME string I'd flagged in my deferred Effect-7 boot spec — reuse it; note I'm using the shorter "// SYSTEM ONLINE" here, not the longer "// MRLN COMMAND CENTER — SYSTEM READY", because the wordmark already says MRLN directly above it and a 2560px-safe short line reads cleaner + translates easier.)

### WHY THIS READS PREMIUM (grounded rationale)
Premium app-opens share three properties, and this hits all three. (1) The first frame is intentional, not blank — a designed dark field with the brand substrate igniting reads as "a crafted instrument booting," the Mass-Effect/Cyberpunk cold-boot idiom, vs a white flash or a spinner (spinners signal "waiting," the enemy of premium). (2) Motion is honest — the boot OVERLAPS real readiness with a short 900ms floor and exits on `max(ready, floor)`, so it never outlives the app (the Stripe/Linear discipline: a splash that makes a ready app wait is the single fastest way to feel cheap/slow). (3) Continuity — the boot resolves the SAME wordmark, at the SAME tracking, over the SAME grid that the lock screen shows, then dissolves into that already-living field; the eye reads ONE continuous power-on, not two screens, so the "living, breathing" promise is paid off by the lock's real particle field rather than faked twice. The cubic-beziers are purposeful: `cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)` (expo-out) for the grid-ignite and wordmark-resolve gives that "settling into focus" deceleration premium boots use; `cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)` (Material standard) for the sweep is a neutral, confident pass. Sub-1.2s total = a beat, not a wait.

### WHAT I'M DELIBERATELY NOT DOING (and why)
- **No second particle canvas in the boot.** The whole coherence point: one canvas (#lkfx), handed off. A boot canvas would be redundant, jank-risk on cold start, and would upstage the field it fades into.
- **No count-up finale / no numbers.** My deferred Effect-7 boot wanted "numbers count up from 0" — that needs item A (parse-then-count, still deferred w/ Akashi) AND it would mean the boot touches real figures (poison surface). This boot is PURE decoration, zero data, so it stays wordmark-only. Count-up can join later as an app-reveal flourish, not a boot dependency.
- **No hardcoded long timer.** 900ms FLOOR that overlaps readiness, not a fixed 2-3s wait. Non-negotiable per the anti-fake-delay rail.
- **No audio/haptic.** Serious-tone + Akashi/CLAUDE.md — a boot chime is exactly the "party" register the brand forbids.
- **No standalone-only gating.** My Effect-7 spec gated boot to installed-PWA-cold-launch to avoid annoying browser reloads. Reconsidered: at 900ms + honest exit it's short enough that showing it on every open (browser AND standalone) is FINE and gives the premium first-impression Osefe asked for on the hosted link too (which is how most first-time users arrive). If Osefe finds it repetitive on rapid reloads, a `sessionStorage` "seen this session → skip" flag is a trivial backlog add (flag me). Not gating in v1.
- **No boot on re-lock / expiry / logout.** The boot is a COLD-OPEN beat (page load only). expireNow/logout re-show the LOCK screen (with its own field), never re-run #boot — #boot is removed from the DOM after first dismiss and never recreated. Correct: re-locking mid-session is not a cold boot.
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review vs this spec): the `#boot` static markup (first body child, wordmark "MRLN" + `.boot-sys` "// SYSTEM ONLINE"), the CSS block (all 5 anims under no-preference + reduce static end-state + env() padding), the `window.__bootFX` IIFE (start-ts + ready() + kill() removeChild + 4000ms safety net), and the ONE integration line `window.__bootFX.ready()` at the end of sync init (after 5231). Colors: --bg, --cyan, #eaffff, --glow — all existing tokens, no magic values. Zero network, zero asset, system/bundled Orbitron+Share Tech Mono only.
- Still open / next: (1) Kaito builds the boot per this spec (owner); I re-review vs spec — watch: boot exits on max(ready,900) NOT a fixed timer; returning-user auto-unlock hides the gate UNDER the boot so no lock-flash; removeChild leaves zero rAF/listener; reduce shows static end-state + hard remove (no fade); env() notch padding; ONE canvas total (no boot canvas). (2) @Akashi: pure decoration, no data/network/storage, no figure→no poison; new JS = the __bootFX IIFE (reads NO app state, only its own timestamp + a DOM node) → expect trivially SAFE, his pass per pipeline. (3) @Mikoto: 1 new string "// SYSTEM ONLINE" (7 langs); "MRLN" stays raw. (4) @Hugo: pure CSS/DOM, no node-testable logic; a smoke check that #boot removes itself + doesn't trap the user (the 4000ms net) is the only QA note. (5) BACKLOG: sessionStorage "skip if seen this session"; optional app-reveal count-up (waits on item A). Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab.

All tokens (--bg/--cyan/#eaffff/--glow), no magic numbers, no canvas in boot. Then LIVE: Playwright 1.56 (global /opt/node22) + Chromium chrome-linux/chrome, font routes aborted. - Found / verdict: **P0 SHIP-BLOCKER (SEV-0, NOT in my features) + both my features = SHIP once the P0 is fixed.**
  ### P0 — THE ENTIRE APP SCRIPT IS DEAD (syntax error). Route: @Kaito (owner) / @Mikoto (whose commit caused it).
  - Live load throws `PAGEERR: missing ) after argument list`. Consequence: `window.LOCK`, `window.STATE`, `window.applyChange`, `window.MEDIALOG` are ALL `undefined` — the main <script> block (starts index.html:1951) fails to parse, so NOTHING runs: no lock verify, no unlock, no app, and my boot never gets its `ready()` (it exits via the 4000ms SAFETY NET at ~4.35s, not max(ready,900)), and cursorWake never registers (it lives in the same dead block).
  - ROOT CAUSE (clean differential, verify-not-trust): checked index.html syntax at four commits via vm.Script with absolute line offsets — `2f3c9cc^` = ALL OK, `2f3c9cc` = BROKEN, `dbf3469` (before my 2 features) = OK, tip = BROKEN. So the break was introduced by commit **2f3c9cc "Translate boot-screen system-status line to all 6 non-English languages"** (Mikoto i18n), NOT by 591d807/b02da33. That commit MINIFIED the feature-translations IIFE from pretty-printed multi-line into one line AND left a spurious closing brace behind.
  - EXACT FIX (pinpointed + proven): index.html **line 5427 reads `});` — it must be `);`.** The object argument to the merge IIFE already self-closes with `}}` at the end of line 5426 (`…"// RENDSZER ONLINE"}}`), so line 5427's leading `}` is an orphan with no opener → the call's `)` can't bind → "missing ) after argument list". Proven: `node --check` on line5426 + `);` = OK; with `});` = FAIL. A patched copy (5427→`);`) passes vm.Script clean across the whole file.
  - WHY THE GATE MISSED IT (worth flagging to Kaito): green.js runs node suites against EXTRACTED functions, not the HTML as a browser parses it; preflight counts "script tags balanced (5)" = structural brace/tag balance, NOT JS-syntax validity; Akashi's SAFE explicitly reviewed "functional delta only; i18n noise ignored" — the break is IN the i18n commit he skipped as noise. Nobody loaded the page in a browser and listened for `pageerror`. A one-line browser-parse smoke (load file:// → assert 0 pageerror → assert typeof window.LOCK!=='undefined') would have caught it and should join Hugo's preflight.
  ### FEATURE 1 cursor-wake @591d807 — SHIP (verified on a syntax-patched copy).
  - Canvas z:0, pointer-events:none, sized 1400×800 @DPR. Drove the mouse in the LEFT gutter (x~60-70, well outside the 1100px-max centered .wrap) → drew 2062 non-transparent px; after 800ms idle → **0 px** (rAF stopped + clearRect). Trail (spawn-by-distance-or-time) not dots; occlusion works (canvas z:0 behind .wrap z:1 → ripples read only in the dark gutters, p_wake.png confirms). Phone gate (390px + touch): media query `false` → wake OFF (no listener/canvas/rAF), correct. Reads premium/ambient, not distracting. Matches my spec on every physics constant.
  ### FEATURE 2 cold-boot @b02da33 — SHIP (verified on the patched copy so the app script runs).
  - Frame-1 paint: #boot present at goto-commit, z 100001, opaque bg rgb(5,8,15)=--bg, wordmark "MRLN" + "// SYSTEM ONLINE" — premium first frame, no white flash. ✓
  - Choreography (p_boot_700.png): grid ignites under the radial mask (center calm), single cyan scan sweep mid-pass (glow band ~55% down), wordmark resolving out of blur/glitch. Matches the 60/240/380/720 timeline. ✓
  - Exit (patched): removed at ~1188ms (motion) / ~1257ms (reduce) = max(ready,900)+260 fade — NOT the 4000ms net (0 pageerrors, LOCK exists). Self-removes (removeChild → boot absent, zero residue). ✓
  - CONTINUITY: boot wordmark letter-spacing 7.04px @44px = .16em; lock wordmark 4.16px @26px = .16em → SAME tracking RATIO (.16em), the absolute px differ only by font-size, which is the CORRECT proportional continuity, not a defect. One continuous power-on into the gate. ✓
  - Reduced-motion (p_reduce.png): static end-state — grid full, wordmark opacity 1 / blur none / .16em, system line .85, no sweep/glitch; then clean removal. Handsome, zero motion. ✓
- The `both` fill-mode DEVIATION — **CORRECT, I bless it (better than my written `forwards`).** My spec said `forwards` with the base CSS at the START (hidden) state. Kaito used `both` with the BASE CSS set to the END state. This is strictly better on two counts: (a) `both` also applies the start keyframe during the pre-delay window (e.g. wordmark's 380ms delay), so there's no 1-frame flash of the end-state before the anim begins — cleaner than `forwards`, which would show the base (which for me was the hidden start, so it'd be fine, but Kaito's base=end means the reduced-motion path gets the end-state FOR FREE); (b) the global reduce wildcard (`animation:none`) kills the anims under reduced-motion, and because the BASE is the end state, reduce users see the fully-resolved static card with zero extra CSS — exactly my reduced-motion spec, achieved more elegantly. No redline; keep as built.
- Specs given: NONE for my two features — both SHIP as built once the P0 lands. The ONE change routed is the P0 fix (not my feature, not my spec): **@Kaito change index.html line 5427 `});` → `);`** (owner of index.html; Mikoto's i18n commit caused it but the fix is a 1-char structural delete — Kaito's call whether he or Mikoto patches under the i18n exception). Re-run a browser-parse smoke after.

## [2026-07-01] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — MOTION RE-REVIEW: cold-boot retimed to ~3s @ tip 61c8363 → SHIP (pacing right) + 2 folds
- Asked: Osefe found the v17 cold-boot too fast ("make it like 3 seconds"). Kaito retimed @61c8363 (floor 900→3000ms; grid 900@120, sweep 1700@500→82vh, logoIn 1000@900 + glitch 560@900 + NEW bootLogoBreath brightness 1→1.22 2.4s@1900 infinite, sysIn 600@1750 + NEW bootSysPulse .85↔.5 1.5s@2350 infinite). Judge LIVE: does 3s read as deliberate premium cold-boot; is the ~2.3→3.0s hold genuinely ALIVE or static; curves/gaps well-paced (no dead gap sweep-end→hold; glitch lands; sweep not sluggish); is 3s too long on EVERY open (sessionStorage skip?); reduced-motion static-card-for-3s acceptable? Read-only, route redlines to Kaito.
- Studied / referenced: `git show 61c8363 -- index.html` (the retiming diff, CSS 120-138 + __bootFX MIN 900→3000 @ ~759). Then LIVE in Chromium (global playwright /opt/node22 + /opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194, font routes googleapis+gstatic→abort per Kaito's caveat, else the @import hangs ~13s). Sampled computed styles of #boot/.boot-logo/.boot-sys across 50→3800ms on BOTH motion + reduce paths (0 pageerrors both — P0 from last run stays fixed), + 2 screenshots (b_1300 mid-resolve w/ scan band; b_2700 living hold). Ref cadence: Mass Effect N7 load / Cyberpunk 2077 boot-logos / Destiny orbit-load all run 2–4s power-on ceremonies — 3s sits inside that premium window.
- Found / verdict: **SHIP — the 3s pacing is right and the hold is genuinely ALIVE (verified numerically, not by eye).** Live timeline (doc-time): grid ignite 120–1020ms; wordmark dissolve-resolve 900–1900ms (op 0→1, blur 6px→0, ls .34→.16em) w/ glitch finishing 1460ms (well before resolve — lands clean); slow single scan sweep 500–2200ms to 82vh (~48vh/s — deliberate ceremonial, NOT sluggish); system line in 1750–2350ms; then the HOLD. Living-hold proof — two INDEPENDENT oscillations running 1900–3000ms: wordmark brightness 1.005→1.02→1.084→1.169→1.20→1.219 (breath inhale) WHILE sys opacity 0.556→0.845→0.695→0.581→0.505 (pulse dim). Not a frozen card. Exit: floor hits 3000ms → boot-out class → 260ms fade → removed ~3.26–3.36s (matches Kaito's 3.36s). Self-removes (present=false, zero residue).
  - NO dead gap sweep-end→hold: breath starts 1900 (= exact logoIn end) and pulse 2350 (= exact sysIn end) — seamless handoffs, breath's 0% kf = brightness(1) = resolved state so NO snap (confirmed 1923ms blur(0.009)→2228ms brightness(1.005), smooth). The breath's `filter:brightness()` overrides logoIn's held `filter:blur(0)` on the same property — harmless (blur 0 is a no-op). The 2.4s breath never completes one cycle before the 3s exit → we see one partial INHALE (glow ramps up as it hands off) — reads "powering up," better than seeing an exhale/dim. `infinite` is functionally a single ramp here but future-proof; fine.
- Specs given (2 folds routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; NEITHER is a ship-blocker for the pacing):
  1. **[Accessibility POLISH — recommend fold before gate] reduced-motion floor.** With MIN=3000 a reduced-motion user gets a fully STATIC card (breath+pulse+sweep all killed by the reduce wildcard — verified: anim=[none], op frozen) held 3 full seconds. The 3s only exists to SHOW motion the user opted OUT of → for them it's a pointless motionless delay (the "is it stuck?" beat, worsened by zero liveness cue). FIX (~1 line in the __bootFX IIFE): `var MIN = (window.matchMedia&&matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches) ? 900 : 3000;` — reuse the proven-good 900ms (anti-flash only; bg==lock bg==--bg so no white flash), NO new magic number. Motion majority keeps the 3s ceremony; reduce users get a calm quick entry.
  2. **[Fast-follow, not a blocker] sessionStorage session-skip (my old backlog item — NOW is the time).** At 900ms re-seeing the boot was cheap; at 3s, EVERY full page-load/reload/PWA-relaunch taxes the user 3s — a power-user who reloads to recheck a number waits 3s each time (my own invariant 7: motion must not cost attention). RECOMMEND: first cold-open of a session = full 3000ms; subsequent loads SAME session = shortened floor (~900ms), NOT zeroed (a 0ms boot risks a flash). Mechanism: `sessionStorage.getItem('mrln_booted')` absent→MIN=3000 + set flag; present→MIN=900. Use sessionStorage NOT localStorage: session-scoped means a genuine cold-start (new day / PWA relaunch = new session) earns the full ceremony again, while rapid same-session reloads skip. **@Akashi**: it's STORAGE (one boolean flag, same-origin, ephemeral, no PII/figure/network) → trivially safe but route per pipeline for his nod before build.
- Reduced-motion path otherwise CORRECT: static end-state (grid full, MRLN resolved .16em, "// SYSTEM ONLINE" .85), transition also killed by wildcard so it hard-removes (op→0 instant, no fade), gone ~3.35s. Handsome, zero motion — my original spec, just too LONG (see fold #1).
- Process flag (not my lane, just noting): 61c8363 MOVED the tip past the Pending candidate c5f92bb → per freeze-the-candidate the gate REOPENS for 61c8363; Akashi SAFE / Mikoto MISSING:0 / Hugo GREEN must re-sign the new tip. Kaito's commit says dict byte-identical (timing-only) so Mikoto's MISSING:0 trivially holds; Hugo/Akashi re-run on 61c8363. If Kaito folds #1/#2 the tip moves again → re-sign that tip.
- Still open / next: (1) @Kaito: fold #1 (reduced-motion floor, recommend before gate — 1 line, same file) + decide #2 (session-skip, fast-follow, needs @Akashi nod on the sessionStorage flag). (2) I re-glance only if a fold perturbs timing (it won't touch layout). (3) The @import font-hang (Akashi backlog) still real, non-gating, separate. Read-only, no lock (didn't touch index.html). Refs from craft memory: iOS UIButton/cell highlight (instant <100ms tactile press = the #1 "alive on touch" signal), Material ripple/state-layer (press-origin feedback, but I keep it token-light + reduced-motion-gated), Stripe/Linear/Vercel hover-state discipline (per-element-tuned, gated to fine pointer, never blanket), Apple "reduce motion" contract, game-UI selection feedback (Destiny/CP2077 — every focusable element visibly reacts), inverse-scale-by-size principle (big surfaces push-in via brightness not shrink, small chips can scale hard — keeps it premium not rubber).

  SHARED TOKENS (add to :root, reused by every primitive, no magic numbers): `--press: transform .09s cubic-bezier(.2,0,0,1), filter .09s ease, box-shadow .12s ease;` `--ease-out: cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1);` `--hover-t:.16s var(--ease-out);` `--focus-ring:0 0 0 4px rgba(124,255,178,.14);`. Press magnitude INVERSE to size: chip/small `scale(.95)`, medium btn `.97`, large rows/cards/cells `scale(.99)+brightness(1.06)` (push-in, not shrink). GPU only.
  TIER 1 (BUILD FIRST — one CSS block ~70-100 lines, pure presentation, no JS, no i18n, no Akashi; kills ~90% of the PNG feel): 1a universal `:active` press on all dead/partial elements (rows/sub-item/exp-head/cal-cell at .99+brightness NO translate so a scroll-drag doesn't bounce; iconbtn/cal-nav/catsw/savebtn/savefab/chattoggle/lk-btn/modal-actions/chatx at scale(.96)+brightness; .box scale(.9); loantoggle .99+brightness). 1b universal hover DESKTOP-GATED `@media (hover:hover) and (pointer:fine)` tuned per element (rows brighten border+bg NOT lift; small buttons +translateY(-2px) border-brighten). 1c focus-visible EVERYWHERE — widen rule 213 to `a,button,input,select,textarea,[tabindex],.item-row,.exp-head,.cal-cell,.iconbtn,.catsw,.check li .box:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--lime);outline-offset:2px;box-shadow:var(--focus-ring)}`; inputs KEEP cyan glow + add lime ring; clickable divs that need keyboard reach (.exp-head, .cal-cell) get tabindex=0+role=button (DON'T make rows focusable if their actions are already buttons inside). RISK: 2b change-gating (must prev≠next); ripple overflow/perf/will-change. Akashi: 2b glance only (wraps poison-gated setters, reads/adds no figure).

RISK: perf on low-end (desktop gate), will-change budget, taste; 3c needs render-identity + Akashi (render path).
  BUILD ORDER: Tier1 (immediate, zero-dep) → 2b+2c+2d → 2a+2e → Tier3.
  HARD CONSTRAINTS baked in: transform/opacity/filter only; all motion no-preference-gated + still fallback (global wildcard 513-514 backstop); real focus-visible; ≥44px; pure CSS+vanilla JS no libs; touch=press/ripple, desktop=hover/cursor-reactive, neither pays the other's cost; will-change only during active interaction; NO regression to Desktop/Lock Alive/boot/cursor-wake (different elements/properties → verified no collision).
- BOOT LOADING-DOTS (#boot .boot-dots @ f2c7302, index.html:124-133) — **BLESSED, ON-SYSTEM, SHIP.** 3×5px cyan dots gap8, glow, traveling opacity+translateY(-2px) pulse (bootDot 1.4s ease-in-out staggered .24/.48s), fade in 1850ms (bootDotsIn), aria-hidden, base opacity:0 → correctly INVISIBLE under reduced-motion (bootDotsIn only runs no-preference). Matches system voice (GPU, subtle, motion-gated, restraint) + timing is coherent (fades in at 1850ms exactly as the 1900→3000ms living-hold begins → reads "system standing by," not a fake-wait spinner since boot exits on max(ready,3000)). Optional-only note (NOT a change): the translateY(-2px) bounce is the only faintly-playful beat — fine at 2px (matches app's 2px-lift vocab); drop to opacity-only if Osefe wants max-serious. The code is a traveling-glow wave across 3 always-present dots (cleaner than literal 1→2→3 add/remove; line-122 comment oversells slightly, no code change). Ship as built.

## [2026-07-01] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — RE-REVIEW: Interaction Layer Tier 1 BUILT @ c4bc7e6 → POLISH (1 live-confirmed desktop-press conflict + omissions)
- Asked: director-only re-review of Kaito's Tier-1 interaction layer (index.html:216-249, after the press/hover seeds) vs my [Jul-01] BUILD-SPEC (log 634-640). Judge press magnitude per element (inverse-size), desktop hover taste + sticky/scroll risk, any OVER-reach/regression to already-alive (.btn/.tab/.scen-btn/.card/inputs/accordion), rule on the DEFERRED tabindex/role for clickable divs, confirm loading dots. Verify LIVE. Read-only, route redlines to Kaito.
- Studied / referenced: byte-read the built block (shared transition 224-227; 1a press 229-231; 1b desktop hover 235-241; 1c focus-visible 245-249) + every affected element's BASE rule for cascade conflicts (item-row/sub-item; exp-head 349-350; cal-cell 427-428; catsw 262-263; cal-nav 422-423; localebar iconbtn 789; savebtn 440-443; savefab 448-449; chattoggle 450-451; lk-btn 480-482; modal-actions button 692-697; chip 719; check li .box 316; loantoggle 381; chatx 454). LIVE in Chromium (playwright /opt/node22 + chromium-1194; fonts.googleapis+gstatic ABORTED per caveat; LOCK.unlock()): confirmed context media hover:hover=true/pointer:fine=true/no-preference=true; used CDP CSS.forcePseudoState + a 220ms transition-settle read (an immediate read caught transitions mid-flight → false identity values; the settle fixed it) to measure the CASCADE WINNER of press-vs-hover on visible injected instances.
- Found / verdict: **POLISH — the build is faithful, safe, touch-correct, and does NOT regress the already-alive primaries; but ONE live-confirmed desktop-press conflict + a few spec omissions + dead-hover cleanup. None block a touch-first ship; F1 is cheap + on-goal, recommend fold before ship.**
  LIVE-CONFIRMED GOOD (say why): item-row:active → scale(0.99)+brightness(1.07), NO translate → no scroll-twitch (exactly the large-surface "push-in" spec). Hover gated (hover:hover)+(pointer:fine) → zero mobile cost, no sticky-hover on touch. All press/hover under no-preference (+ global reduce wildcard backstop). 0 pageerrors, window.LOCK present, #boot + .boot-dots intact → no P0/regression, loading dots read right. NO over-reach: .tab/.btn/.scen-btn/.card/inputs are NOT in the new selectors → no double-transition, no fighting hover; inputs keep their cyan :focus glow (excluded from focus-visible as specced); accordion rowIn stagger untouched (targets exp-body tr, not exp-head).
- Specs given (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review):
  **F1 (POLISH, med — the one real defect, LIVE-CONFIRMED). Desktop press-squeeze DEFEATED by pre-existing :hover{translateY} on 4 control types.** Measured with settled transitions: `.catsw` press-only → scale(0.95) ✓ but hover+active (mouse press) → translateY(-2px) [hover wins]; `.savebar .savebtn` hover+active → translateY(-2px) [hover higher specificity wins]; `.modal-actions button` hover+active → translateY(-1px) [hover later-source wins]; `.lockcard .lk-btn` → same specificity story (bare test showed scale only because outside .lockcard; real in-app lk-btn defeated like savebtn). So on DESKTOP these 4 give no press-squeeze — only the held hover-lift. Touch is fine (no hover state → scale(.95) applies). This is exactly the conflict Kaito ALREADY solved for `.tab` (`.tab:active{transform:translateY(0) scale(.97)}` cancels the hover translate) + `.btn` — he didn't extend the cancel to these secondaries → inconsistent + undercuts the "senses cursor" goal on desktop. FIX (mirror the .tab cancel; MATCH each hover's specificity so the press wins): `.catsw:active{transform:translateY(0) scale(.95)}`; `.savebar .savebtn:active{transform:translateY(0) scale(.95)}`; `.modal-actions button:active{transform:translateY(0) scale(.95)}`; `.lockcard .lk-btn:active{transform:translateY(0) scale(.95)}`. Keep the filter brightness.
  **F2 (POLISH, low) — 3 elements from my spec MISSED.** `.loantoggle` (381, big label surface → add to the large press group scale(.99)+brightness); `.check li .box` (316, my spec: scale(.9) — add to a small press rule); `.chatx` (454 → add to the scale(.95) small group). Point of the tier was "every tappable."
  **F3 (a11y — AGREE with the deferral, but PIN it). Keyboard reach for clickable divs.** `.exp-head`/`.cal-cell` (and `.check li .box`) are NOT keyboard-focusable, so their focus-visible rings are currently INERT and a keyboard-only user cannot expand the expense accordion (WCAG 2.1.1-A). AGREE it's a fine Tier-1 follow-up — it's PRE-EXISTING (not regressed), touch/mouse (the overwhelming majority; MRLN is touch-first) are fully served, and proper role=button + aria-expanded + Enter/Space keydown-that-fires-the-handler-without-double-firing is a genuinely different RISK CLASS than pure-CSS Tier 1 (right to keep Tier 1 zero-risk). The inert rings are harmless (can't be focused → not misleading), leave them in. NOT a blocker for THIS ship — but it must be the COMMITTED #1 item of the next tier, not open-ended "follow-up."
  **F4 (cleanup, low) — dead/shadowed NEW hover rules.** New `.exp-head:hover` gradient (238) is fully overridden by the pre-existing flat `.exp-head:hover` (350); new `.cal-cell:hover` (239) border:cyan is downgraded to cyan-dim by pre-existing (428). Elements still hover (via the old rules) so not broken, but Kaito's new hover code is partly inert. Either delete the redundant new rules or consolidate to win. Also `.exp-head` transform-press snaps (its transition is `background .15s` only @349, no transform transition) — very minor; add transform to its transition or accept the snap.
  **F5 (consistency, low) — `.chip` asymmetry.** `.chip` gets press (231)+transition (225) but is absent from the 1b hover list (240) AND the focus-visible list (248); plain `.chip` buttons also get no focus ring at all (rule 213 covers only tab/btn/scen-btn). Wiz chips hover via `.wiz .chip:hover` (719) so not dead, but add `.chip` to the focus-visible list for keyboard-a11y + consistency.
- Deferred-tabindex CALL: AGREE it's a fine Tier-1 follow-up (see F3) — not a must-have for this ship, but pinned #1 for Tier 1.5.
- Roadmap CONFIRMED still stands: Tier 2 (2a input life, 2b value-PULSE with the prev≠next change-gate, 2c modal, 2d toast, 2e ripple) → Tier 3 (3a glare-only/no-tilt, 3b button sheen, 3c list add/remove = deferred item C, needs render-identity + Akashi). Item A count-up still deferred (parse-then-count w/ Akashi); 2b pulse is the interim living-data cue.
- Still open / next: (1) Kaito's call on F1 (recommend fold before ship — cheap, on-goal, mirrors the .tab cancel he already wrote) + F2/F4/F5 (fast-follow) + pin F3 as Tier-1.5 #1. Any fold MOVES the tip → gate REOPENS (Akashi SAFE / Mikoto MISSING:0 / Hugo GREEN re-sign; all pure-CSS, no new string). (2) If Osefe wants Tier 1 out tonight: nothing here regresses/errors and touch is correct, so it CAN ship as-is with F1/F2/F4/F5 as a fast-follow — but F1 is the one I'd hold one beat for. LIVE in Chromium (playwright /opt/node22 + chromium-1194; fonts.googleapis+gstatic ABORTED per caveat; LOCK.unlock(); waited out the 3s cold-boot overlay before screenshotting — first shots caught the boot cover): confirmed media hover/fine/≥1024/no-preference all true, 0 pageerrors, LOCK=object, boot+dots intact; pulse class fires on programmatic text change of hero-num/stat-val/bignum/calc-out (before=false→after=true, <60ms); HERO_ANIM valPulse 0.5s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1); sheen ::before opacity 0(no-hover)→1(hover), --mx/--my track pointer; captured card hover vs no-hover stills (Mission-Briefing card) — text crisp/legible in both, sheen a faint cyan lift of the dark panel only; SETTLED input focus read (260ms, past the .18s transition — my earlier frame-0 reads were false "none"s): keyboard-focus input = outline:none + box-shadow cyan rgba(0,229,255,.14) 3px.
- Found / verdict: **SHIP @ b4a7523.** The wave is coherent, premium, and correctly gated; it reads as "living instrument," not toy. One LOW POLISH (input focus language) + one blessed deviation to record.
  - **2b LIVING DATA — SHIP; BLESS the scale-only deviation.** My spec was scale 1.06 + brightness 1.25 @320ms; Kaito shipped scale-only 1.08 @.5s. This is CORRECT, not a miss: .hero-num already runs the ambient `hudGlowPulse` on `filter:brightness` (I flagged this in my Desktop-Alive review) — a brightness pulse would COLLIDE on the same property and freeze/jump the ambient glow during its .5s. Scale-only sidesteps it entirely; 1.08 (vs 1.06) compensates for the dropped brightness punch. The keyframe is a clean pop-and-settle (no overshoot below 1, no bounce) → reads "the number just reacted," which is living data, not a gimmick. Fires ONLY on real change (string compare now!==last), skips first population ('—'→value gated both sides), and can't self-retrigger (observer watches childList/characterData, NOT attributes, so the class toggle is invisible to it). Magnitude right. Optional-only: dial 1.08→1.06 IF the 60px hero reads big on a real phone — NOT a redline.
  - **3a CARD SHEEN — SHIP; BLESS .10 (my spec said ~.06).** Live A/B (hover vs no-hover on the Mission-Briefing card): .10 cyan on the near-black panel is subtle+premium — a soft cursor spotlight that only lifts the dark areas; bright #eaffff/cyan text is fully legible, contrast untouched (z-index:0 paints over content, but at .10 over bright text it's invisible; over dark bg it glows). 300px radius = a localized pool, right for desktop card widths. LOW, fast-follow, does NOT block ship.

Reduced-motion: every effect declared inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference)` (+ global 513-514 wildcard backstop); liveVal observer AND cardSheen listener both early-return under reduce/touch → zero cost on phone/reduce.
- Specs given: ONE POLISH (routed to Kaito, owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds): the `:not(:focus-visible)`-scoped outline reset above so 2a's cyan mouse ring stops eating the Tier-1 lime keyboard outline on inputs. ## [2026-07-02] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — BUILD-SPEC: THE FUTURISM WAVE (Tiers 1-3a shipped → "jaw-dropping"; Wave A/B/C)
- Asked: Osefe wants MAX impressiveness — "as FUTURISTIC as possible, tons of animations," BOTH desktop+mobile, feel like the most advanced thing he's touched. Design the definitive tiered build-spec taking MRLN from responsive (Tiers 1-3a, v19/v20) to jaw-dropping. Director-only, Kaito builds. HARD RAILS: locked 60fps mid-phone (transform/opacity/filter only, cap concurrent, will-change discipline, no perpetual rAF), ALL motion prefers-reduced-motion gated w/ dignified static fallback, serious register (no confetti/toy), FREE·OFFLINE (no libs/assets/network), don't regress shipped ambient/interaction layers, flag Akashi + i18n. MUST-INCLUDE queued: tap ripple 2e, button glint 3b, list add/remove 3c (solve render-identity concretely), input-focus :not(:focus-visible) polish, Tier-1.5 keyboard reach (F3).
- Studied / referenced: byte-grounded in the CURRENT file @ tip 9d2ec15 (Tier2+3a live, sw v20). Confirmed: tab handler `prevTabIdx`+`from-left/from-right` @2539-2546 → panels ALREADY slide (`slideFromR/L .22s cubic-bezier(.25,0,0,1)` @272-275, my item B); `fmtN` @2127 = `Math.round(n).toLocaleString(curInfo().loc)` — INTEGER-rounded (no decimals) so stripping every non-[0-9-] char = locale-agnostic parse (the key that makes parse-then-count SAFE); `#ovLeft` @1190 holds a PURE number, currency is a SIBLING `.ccySuffix` span → count-up-friendly targets; `expMonth`/`gtot` (@2723/2718) embed '~'/' kr'/'/mo' INLINE → NOT clean count-up targets; every list = wholesale `host.innerHTML = arr.map().join('')` (renderExpenses @2698-2721, savings @2996, media/PR/workouts) with NO data-id on rows yet; `.meter>span` @302-306 = gradient bar (span width); NO SVG progress ring shipped (only bodySvg @1579); `navigator.vibrate` = ZERO hits (haptics net-new); 2b liveVal MutationObserver already watches `.hero-num/.bignum/.calc-out/.stat .val` text (count-up MUST guard it or strobe); chat = `.chatwidget` display:none→block snap @2017/2044, `.chatbody` textarea. Refs (gluttony, named per item below): Cyberpunk 2077 boot/scan HUD, Destiny 2 orbit-load + reticle feedback, Iron Man JARVIS holo-assemble, Apple visionOS depth/materialize, Stripe/Linear entrance discipline, iOS UIButton haptic tick, Material state-layer ripple, Robinhood/Revolut number count-up, Android overscroll glow.
- Found / verdict: **BUILD-SPEC — decisive, 3 waves. Wave A = the jaw-drop per effort (numbers spin up, panels assemble like a HUD, every tap ripples + ticks) at locked 60fps; B/C at direction level. This is density-of-RESPONSIVENESS, not noise — every effect is short, GPU-only, gated, and dies to zero cost. Nothing loops forever; nothing fakes a wait.**

### SHARED TOKENS (add to :root once; every primitive reuses — no magic numbers)
- `--ease-out: cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)` (expo-out "settle into focus" — reuse if already present from Tier-1; the shipped valPulse uses this literal). `--ease-hud: cubic-bezier(.25,0,0,1)` (the existing panel-slide curve). `--count-dur:420ms; --ripple-dur:460ms; --draw-dur:700ms; --assemble-step:40ms`.

## WAVE A — SHIP FIRST (biggest visible wow ÷ effort; all touch+desktop; all under @media(prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference) + the global reduce wildcard backstop; all transform/opacity/filter)

**A1 — TAP RIPPLE (queued 2e). Ref: Material state-layer + iOS touch highlight. THE #1 "alive on touch" signal.**
- Mechanism: ONE delegated `pointerdown` listener on document. On a press whose target matches `.btn,.tab,.scen-btn,.item-row,.exp-head,.cal-cell,.chattoggle,.medSeedChip,[data-ripple]`, spawn `<span class="ripple">` positioned at `(e.clientX-rect.left, e.clientY-rect.top)` inside that element. Element gets `position:relative;overflow:hidden` (rounded rows/cards already clip). Keyframe `ripple{from{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(0);opacity:.5}to{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(2.4);opacity:0}}`, `--ripple-dur` var(--ease-out), radial `rgba(0,229,255,.35)`. Remove the span on `animationend`.
- Curve/timing: 460ms var(--ease-out). Perf: one short-lived span/press, `will-change` NOT set (too brief to matter), removed on end → zero residue. Where: every primary/secondary tappable + the big list rows. Risk: overflow clipping on non-clipped ancestors (scope selector to elements already border-radius'd / add overflow:hidden to the ripple set only); double-fire with 2b — none (different elements/props). Reduced-motion: listener early-returns (no spawn). i18n: none. Akashi: none (no data/state read; pure DOM decoration).

**A2 — HAPTIC TICK (navigator.vibrate). Ref: iOS UIButton haptic — mobile's answer to hover.**
- Mechanism: in the SAME A1 pointerdown path, `if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(8)` — a single 8ms tick — but ONLY for PRIMARY controls (`.btn,.tab,.scen-btn,.savebtn,.chattoggle`), NOT rows/cells (buzzing on every scroll-tap = noise). pointerdown IS the required user-gesture. Android-mostly (iOS Safari lacks the Vibration API → silently no-ops, correct graceful degrade). GATE it OFF under reduced-motion too (haptic is motion-adjacent; sensory-sensitive users opt out via the same switch).
- Perf: 8ms tick, negligible battery. Risk: over-buzz → scoped to primaries only + 8ms (a tick, not a rumble). i18n: none. **Akashi: FLAG for the nod — navigator.vibrate is a benign device-capability call, no data/network/storage, but it's a new platform API so route it (expect trivially SAFE).**

**A3 — NUMBER COUNT-UP (the signature futurism move; = deferred item A, NOW solvable). Ref: Revolut/Robinhood balance count-up + Cyberpunk HUD spin-up. Composes with the shipped 2b pulse.**
- THE PARSE PROBLEM, SOLVED CONCRETELY: fmtN rounds to INTEGER → no decimals → stripping every char except `[0-9-]` removes ALL locale grouping separators (da-DK '.', en-US ',', the NBSP space) safely, yielding the pure integer, in EVERY locale. And the clean targets (`#ovLeft`, `.hero-num`, `.stat .val`, `.calc-out`) hold a number-ONLY string (currency lives in a sibling `.ccySuffix` span) → nothing else to parse. This is why parse-then-count is safe now where my original parseFloat spec wasn't.
- Mechanism: helper `countUp(el, toValue)` — (1) read start = current on-screen value via `parseInt(el.textContent.replace(/[^0-9-]/g,''),10)`; NaN/'—' → start 0 (first-paint counts 0→N = the dashboard-boot flourish, pairs with the cold-boot). (2) if `Math.abs(to-start) / Math.max(Math.abs(to),1) < 0.02` OR start===to → `el.textContent=fmtN(to)` instantly, return (2% floor kills strobe on trivial slider deltas). (3) cancel any in-flight `el._cuRAF`; rAF-tween start→to over `--count-dur` (420ms) var(--ease-out); each frame `el.textContent = fmtN(cur)` (grouping stays locale-correct every frame); on end set exactly `fmtN(to)` + store `el._cuVal=to`.
- INTEGRATION (call-site, not observer — cleaner): replace the direct `el.textContent = fmtN(x)` at the clean sinks — setHeroLeftover, the #ovLeft/#ovComfort bignum writes, stat-val writes, .calc-out — with `countUp(el, x)`. countUp lands EXACTLY on `fmtN(x)` (the same poison-gated value the sink already computed) → it animates the DISPLAY only, adds/reads no figure. Slider-drag case handled elegantly: start = current on-screen value so rapid drags CHASE smoothly, and the 2% floor snaps tiny deltas → responsive, never laggy.
- **2b COMPOSE/GUARD (must-do, or it strobes):** the shipped liveVal observer watches text changes → countUp's per-frame writes would re-fire it every frame. FIX: countUp sets `el._cuAnimating=true` at start / clears on end; the liveVal observer early-returns while `el._cuAnimating` (so valPulse fires ONCE — the "reacted" punch — when countUp STARTS, then count-up does the settle; pulse+count-up compose exactly as I flagged in the Tier-2 review). Route the 1-line observer guard to Kaito.
- Curve/timing: 420ms var(--ease-out). Perf: rAF ONLY during the tween, cancels on complete — NO perpetual loop (battery rail honored). Where Wave A: `.hero-num`, #ovLeft/#ovComfort, `header.hud .stat .val`, `.calc-out` (all number-only). DEFER (→ Wave C): expMonth/gtot (inline '~'/kr/'/mo' — need a digit-span wrap; 2b pulse covers them meanwhile). Risk: re-entrancy w/ 2b (the guard) + landing exactly on fmtN(to) (spec'd). Reduced-motion: set final instantly (no tween). i18n: none. **Akashi: glance only — countUp re-formats the SAME value the sink computed, no new figure, same class as the 2b pass he already SAFE'd.**

**A4 — METER DRAW-ON. Ref: Destiny 2 / CP2077 bars that fill on reveal.**
- Mechanism: `.meter>span` currently sets width inline. Switch the fill to GPU: `transform-origin:left; transform:scaleX(var(--p))` where `--p` = 0..1 set inline per render; `transition:transform var(--draw-dur) var(--ease-out)`. On (re)render the bar animates from its prior scale to the new one; on first paint from 0. (scaleX stretches the 90deg gradient imperceptibly on a 14px bar — acceptable; avoids the layout cost of animating width.)
- Curve/timing: 700ms var(--ease-out). Perf: pure transform, GPU, one-shot per render. Where: every `.meter` (savings progress, budget bars). Risk: gradient stretch (negligible at bar height). Reduced-motion: wildcard kills the transition → bar just appears at target. i18n: none. Akashi: none.

**A5 — PANEL-ASSEMBLE on tab switch (upgrade the current slide → HUD boot). Ref: JARVIS holo-assemble + visionOS materialize.**
- Osefe wants MORE than the current whole-panel slide. Keep the directional slide on the panel CONTAINER, ADD a staggered materialize of the panel's direct `> .card` children so the panel ASSEMBLES card-by-card like a HUD powering on. Keyframe `assembleIn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}` 260ms var(--ease-out); nth-child delay `calc(var(--assemble-step) * n)` (40ms step), CAP at 8 children (9th+ = 0 delay, snap) so a dense panel never feels slow. Total assemble < ~320ms.
- Where: `.panel.show > .card` (fires on every tab activation, the most-repeated moment → highest cumulative wow). Perf: only VISIBLE cards animate (~3-8), GPU transform/opacity, one-shot. Does NOT collide with the accordion rowIn (targets `.exp-body tr`, different element) or the slide (container vs children). Risk: repetitiveness across 17 tabs → keep short (260ms) + capped stagger; if Osefe finds it much, drop step to 30ms. Reduced-motion: wildcard kills it → cards just present (slide also gone → instant panel, dignified). i18n: none. Akashi: none.

**A6 — INPUT-FOCUS CONSISTENCY (the queued 1-liner from my Tier-2 review). a11y + system coherence.**
- 2a shipped `input:focus{outline:none}` which eats the Tier-1 unified lime keyboard `:focus-visible` outline on inputs. Scope the reset: `input:focus:not(:focus-visible),textarea:focus:not(:focus-visible),select:focus:not(:focus-visible){outline:none}` → mouse-focus keeps the clean cyan ring, keyboard-focus keeps the lime outline+ring like every other control. Pure CSS, no string. Fold into Wave A.

## WAVE B — RICH (medium effort; ship after A)
**B1 — LIST ADD/REMOVE ENTER-LEAVE (queued 3c; = deferred item C). SOLVES THE RENDER-IDENTITY PROBLEM without a VDOM/reconciler.** The app rebuilds `host.innerHTML` wholesale every render, so a removed node is destroyed before it can animate and an added node is indistinguishable. Concrete solution reusing THIS codebase's proven `just-opened` event-gate pattern (the item-E fix):
  - Give every list row a stable `data-id="<STATE item .id>"` in the map() template (media/PR/savings/workouts already carry `.id`; expenses render from MODEL.groups — key rows on `group+index` or add ids).
  - ENTER: module-level `justAdded` Set. Each add handler (applyChange add, MEDIALOG.add, addPR, addSavingsBox…) pushes the new id. After each render's `innerHTML=`, query `[data-id]` rows whose id ∈ justAdded → add `.row-enter` (`rowBorn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px);background:rgba(124,255,178,.14)}to{opacity:1;transform:none;background:transparent}}` 520ms var(--ease-out)), then CLEAR the set. Consuming the set each render = no re-trigger on unrelated re-renders (the exact discipline that fixed item E).
  - LEAVE: shared `animateRemove(rowEl, done)` — add `.row-leave` (`rowDie{to{opacity:0;transform:translateX(12px);max-height:0;padding:0;margin:0}}` 260ms ease-in), on animationend run `done()` = the REAL STATE mutation + re-render. Delete handlers call `animateRemove(el, ()=>{ …existing delete… })` instead of deleting immediately → animate-out THEN rebuild. Small local change per delete handler, no engine.
  - Perf: transform/opacity (+ max-height on leave — one row, acceptable). Reduced-motion: skip both classes → instant add/remove. **Akashi: FLAG — add/delete handlers may carry the figure poison; we only add a class + DEFER the existing mutation (don't touch figures) → glance on the deferred-delete path.** i18n: none.
**B2 — SAVINGS PROGRESS RING draw-on (= roadmap item J, net-new). Ref: Apple Activity ring / Destiny bounty ring.** ~44px SVG in the header pillbar beside 🔥 streak. Track `var(--line)` 3px; fill lime(on-pace)/amber(behind); `stroke-dasharray = C`, animate `stroke-dashoffset` from C→C*(1-progress) over 700ms var(--ease-out) on render. The "watch it grow" daily-return mechanic. Perf: SVG stroke, one-shot. **Akashi: pure local math, no network — flag per protocol (trivial).** i18n: none (numeric).
**B3 — BUTTON POINTER-GLINT (queued 3b). Ref: Stripe/Linear hover sheen; composes w/ shipped 3a card-sheen.** Desktop-gated `(hover:hover)+(pointer:fine)+min-width:1024px+no-preference`. `--mx` set on pointermove over `.btn/.tab`; a `::after` linear-gradient highlight tracks the cursor (rgba(255,255,255,.06)). rAF-coalesced (one write/frame), `will-change:transform` ON only while hovered / OFF on leave. Perf: desktop-only, tiny. Risk: will-change budget (per-hover toggle). i18n/Akashi: none.
**B4 — SCROLL-REVEAL (IntersectionObserver materialize). Ref: visionOS/modern editorial reveal.** Cards/sections `.card` fade+rise (translateY(16px)→0, opacity, 400ms var(--ease-out)) as they enter viewport, ONCE (unobserve after reveal — never re-animate on scroll-up). CRITICAL fail-safe: content starts VISIBLE; IO only ADDS a `.revealed` enhancement (if JS/IO absent OR reduced-motion → everything visible immediately, never trapped hidden). **Akashi: IntersectionObserver reads layout only, no data/net → flag for the nod (trivial).** i18n: none.
**B5 — OVERSCROLL GLOW (mobile). Ref: Android overscroll edge-glow.** A cyan glow band at the scroll boundary on over-pull (touch-driven, visual only). **I REJECT literal pull-to-refresh — honest cut: MRLN is a local offline app; there is no meaningful "refresh" action to pull for (nothing to re-fetch), so a pull-to-reload gesture would confuse (a 9-yo pulls, expects something, nothing happens or the app re-renders pointlessly).** The GLOW gives the tactile "edge of the data" feel without a phantom action. Mobile-only. Reduced-motion: skip. i18n/Akashi: none.
**B6 — ASSISTANT / CHAT SURFACE. Ref: JARVIS reveal + honest-latency discipline.** (a) Widget entrance: replace the display:none→block SNAP with scale(.96)+translateY(8px)+opacity→none, 200ms var(--ease-out) (mirror the shipped modal 2c). (b) Answer materialize: the answer computes instantly (local) — sell "intelligence" with a fast scan-line reveal of the answer text (a 240ms cyan wipe), NOT a fake typing delay. **HONEST CUT: no artificial "thinking…" wait — faking latency on an instant local answer is the exact anti-premium fake-wait I've argued against (Stripe/Linear discipline). Materialize fast, don't stall.** i18n: none (no new strings; motion only). Akashi: none.
**B7 — TIER-1.5 KEYBOARD REACH (queued F3; committed a11y, WCAG 2.1.1-A).** `.exp-head` + `.cal-cell` are click-only divs → not keyboard-operable (their focus-visible rings are currently inert). Add `tabindex="0"` + `role="button"` (+ `aria-expanded` on `.exp-head`) + a keydown handler firing the SAME click handler on Enter/Space (preventDefault on Space to stop scroll; guard against double-fire). Not "wow" but OWED — ships with the wave, not forgotten. i18n: none. Akashi: none (a11y wiring).

## WAVE C — DEEPEST (taste/perf risk; mostly desktop-gated; ship last)
- **C1 Header/HUD parallax depth + holographic accents** — extend the shipped Desktop-Alive cursor-parallax to layer the header (grid behind / hero mid / chrome front) with small differential offset on cursor (desktop) + subtle holographic hue-shift accent on the hero number band. Higher perf/taste risk → desktop-gated, capped offsets. Ref: visionOS depth, CP2077 layered HUD.
- **C2 Odometer per-digit roll** — upgrade A3 from a value-tween to a per-digit vertical roll (each digit a column that rolls to its target). Richer, but needs digit decomposition + handles the width reflow → effort/risk. Simple count-up (A3) is ~90% of the wow; this is the flourish. Ref: mechanical odometer / Robinhood.
- **C3 Count-up on inline-suffix numbers** — the deferred A3 half (expMonth/gtot): wrap just the digits in a `<span class="odo">` so count-up targets the digit span, leaving '~'/kr/'/mo' static. Finishes the count-up coverage.
- **C4 Glass/holographic card treatment + ambient data-glyphs** — subtle frosted-depth on cards + 2-3% opacity drifting data glyphs (extends the shipped ambient layer). Pure flourish; taste-gated.

## HONEST CUTS (things I'm NOT doing, and why — per the "cut only if it can't be done well" rule)
- **Literal pull-to-refresh** — no meaningful refresh in a local offline app; the gesture promises an action that doesn't exist → confusion. Replaced by overscroll GLOW (B5), which delivers the tactile feel without the phantom action.
- **Micro-tilt on cards** — toy-risk for a serious finance tool + hurts number legibility; glare/sheen (3a shipped, 3b B3) gives the cursor-reactive depth without the wobble. Consistent with my prior 3a call.
- **Fake "thinking…" delay on the assistant** — dishonest latency = anti-premium; B6 materializes fast instead.
- **Count-up on inline-suffix numbers in Wave A** — parse ambiguity with embedded '~'/kr/'/mo'; deferred to C3 via digit-span wrap; 2b pulse covers them meanwhile. Honest defer, not a skip.

## HARD-RAILS COMPLIANCE (baked into every item)
- 60fps mid-phone: transform/opacity/filter ONLY; no width/top/left layout animation (meter uses scaleX not width; ring uses stroke-dashoffset which is cheap on a tiny path). Concurrent-cap: ripple one-per-press, count-up cancels prior rAF per el, assemble capped at 8 children. will-change ONLY during active interaction (3b/3a), never static on N elements.
- No perpetual rAF: count-up/ripple/assemble/draw-on are all ONE-SHOT (rAF cancels on completion); the only continuous loops remain the existing idle-stopping ambient ones (gridPulse/Desktop-Alive/cursor-wake) — UNTOUCHED. Battery rail held.
- prefers-reduced-motion: every effect declared inside @media(no-preference) + the global `*{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}` reduce wildcard as backstop; JS listeners (ripple/haptic/countUp/scroll-reveal/glint) all early-return under reduce → zero cost + dignified static end-state (numbers land instantly, panels present, bars at target).
- Serious register: cyan/lime tokens only, no confetti/cartoon/bounce-overshoot; count-up settles (expo-out, no rubber), ripple is a clean scan not a splash.
- FREE·OFFLINE: zero libs, zero assets, zero network — vanilla JS + CSS + one small SVG (B2). System/bundled Orbitron+Share Tech Mono only.
- No-regress: A1 ripple / A5 assemble / B1 rows target DIFFERENT elements+props than Desktop-Alive/Lock-Alive/boot/cursor-wake/2b/3a (verified); the ONE coordination point is A3 count-up ↔ 2b observer (the `_cuAnimating` guard) — spec'd.
- i18n: NONE across the whole wave (motion/decoration only) → Mikoto no new keys.
- Akashi flags: A2 haptic (navigator.vibrate nod), B1 3c (deferred-delete path glance), B2 ring + B4 IO (local-math/layout-read nods) — all expected trivially SAFE; A3 count-up = glance-only (re-formats the same computed value, no new figure).

## BUILD ORDER (Wave A first, biggest wow ÷ effort)
A1 ripple + A2 haptic (one pointerdown path) → A3 count-up (+2b guard) → A4 meter draw-on + A6 input-focus 1-liner → A5 panel-assemble. Then Wave B (B1 lists → B2 ring → B7 a11y → B3/B4/B5/B6). Then Wave C on taste.
- Specs given: the above, routed to Kaito (owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review each vs this spec, LIVE in Chromium — aborting fonts.googleapis/gstatic routes to avoid the offline @import hang). Wave A items are build-ready; B/C at direction level (flag me for build-ready detail on any B/C item when Kaito reaches it).
- Still open / next: (1) Kaito builds Wave A; I re-review live per item (watch: ripple overflow-clip + reduced-motion skip; A3 lands EXACTLY on fmtN(to) + the _cuAnimating guard stops pulse-strobe + 2% floor on slider drags; A5 doesn't fight accordion rowIn; A6 keyboard lime ring restored). (2) A2/B1/B2/B4 need the Akashi nods above before/at build. (3) F3 (B7) closes the committed Tier-1.5 a11y debt. (4) Item A count-up is NO LONGER deferred — A3 solves the parse concretely (integer-strip). Read-only, no lock (Kaito holds index.html when building). Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 9d2ec15.

No regression (own keyframe on .chatwidget only; modal 2c untouched).
  **B7 = SHIP.** exp-head: tabindex 0 / role button / aria-expanded reflects .open on render (true on the default-open first group). Real-Tab focus (#15) → :focus-visible TRUE, outline 2px solid lime rgb(124,255,178) + offset 2px + box-shadow 0 0 0 4px lime.14 = the Tier-1 ring. Enter: open true→false, aria "true"→"false", clicks=1 (NO double-fire — divs get no native Enter/Space activation, el.click() is the sole trigger). Space: toggles back, aria flips, clicks increments by 1, scrollY unchanged (preventDefault stops page scroll). cal-cell: 31 day cells tabindex0/role button; empty padding cells correctly carry NO tabindex/role/data-date (matcher `.cal-cell[data-date][role=button]` skips them). Real-Tab focus → :focus-visible TRUE, lime ring present after the .12s transition settles (the 0px I first saw was mid-transition, not a defect). Focus ORDER natural (day 07-01 → Tab → 07-02). No focus-order weirdness, no regression to rowIn stagger / any shipped motion.
  **SEVERE PRE-EXISTING BUG surfaced (NOT B6/B7): the calendar DAY MODAL is broken — throws on every open, mouse AND keyboard.** renderDayModal @4567 declares `var t=document.getElementById('dayMTitle')` — a LOCAL `t` that SHADOWS the global translation fn `t()`. Lines 4573-4574 then call `t('Delete')` / `t('Nothing on this day yet…')` → `TypeError: t is not a function`. The throw happens INSIDE openDayModal (@4561) BEFORE it reaches `dayBack.classList.add('show')` (@4562), so the modal never opens. Proven identical on a direct mouse `c.click()` and on keyboard Enter (same stack: renderDayModal 4574 ← openDayModal 4561 ← calGrid click 4483; the keyboard run adds the extra frame kbdActivate @8510 el.click() — proof B7 routes through the EXACT same handler mouse uses). `git blame` → introduced in **a47f63e (2026-06-27, "i18n: translate all conditional/dynamic UI")** when the t() calls were added into a fn that already shadowed `t`. So it's been shipping broken for 5 days; B7 merely made me exercise the path. NOT a B6/B7 regression, NOT a gate blocker for this batch (the bug is already live), but a P1 UX defect: the calendar day-detail/add-event modal is dead on all platforms.
- Specs given (routed to Kaito — JS logic bug, find-xor-fix, I don't code): rename the local at renderDayModal:4567 `var t=…dayMTitle` → e.g. `var ttl=…; if(ttl) ttl.textContent=…` so lines 4573-4574 resolve to the global translation `t()`. Verify by opening any calendar day → modal shows, "Delete"/"Nothing on this day yet…" render translated, 0 pageerrors. (Note the inline day PANEL renderDay@4581 is fine — it has no local `t` shadow — only the MODAL is broken.) No design change to B6/B7 — both ship as built.
- Still open / next: (1) @Kaito fix the renderDayModal `t`-shadow P1 (separate from this gate). (2) B6/B7 = SHIP; gate already FULL @e976932 (Akashi SAFE · Mikoto MISSING:0 · Hugo GREEN) → awaiting Osefe's explicit "ship it" (sleep-mode, no auto-publish).
