## [2026-07-03] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — DESIGN: Monthly Income feature (Task A, mechanics+UI) + offline-finance roadmap (Task B)
- Asked: Osefe greenlit an ALL-OUT finance upgrade. (A, priority) design the mechanics+UI for logging real monthly income → auto-derive low/typical/high+avg from a month-by-month history, replacing the guessed `MODEL.income={low,avg,high}`. (B) a prioritized/phased set of finance features that put MRLN on par with Mint/YNAB/Copilot/Monarch/Rocket Money but reworked 100% offline. Director-only; Kaito builds.
- Studied / referenced: GROUNDED IN THE REAL FILE @ tip a4ba0b8 (db34cc6 chat tip). Income data path end-to-end — `MODEL.income={low,avg,high}` DEFAULT_MODEL@2134 / blank@2507; `leftOver(income)=__sys.token()*income-GRAND-loanAmt()`@2598; `comfort()`@2599; hero `#ovLeft countUp(leftOver(avg))`@2839 + `setHeroLeftover`@2851; scenarios rebuild@2900-2918 (low/avg/high → stLow/incLow/btnLow/klBtn…); savings projection surplus/freeMoney@3208-3209; grade/tier factors@3344-3388 (savings-rate, costs-vs-income, income-source=hasJob) + social-class-by-income@3468; Klarna surplus (klSurplus); `setIncome(key,value)`@3691 (the manual+Assistant sink → logChange 'set-income' → rebuildScenarios → refreshFinance); Assistant NL `op:setIncome`@4700 + LEX.income@4754 + parser@4869-4873; income tab markup@1259-1282 (incLow/incAvg/incHigh table + edit-hint card). ON-OPEN PROMPT PRECEDENTS (reuse, don't invent): `morningBriefing()`@2401 (once-per-day gate `STATE.briefedOn===__sDay()` + autosave), `checkBirthday()`@8158, `maybeShowUpdatePanel()`@7944 (forced modal for a returning set-up user, dismiss stamps `STATE.lastSeenBuild`), streak `__bumpStreak`@2377. PERSISTENCE: `STATE`/`DEFAULTS`@2258 (localStorage STORE_KEY + embedded JSON, autosave, read-back-verified@2337); currency conversion of income on switch `cv()`@2198 (incomeLog MUST get the same). WHAT ALREADY EXISTS for Task B (build on, not over): savingsBoxes+projectBox+yearsToTarget@3057-3126 (compound projection, targets — these ARE assets); single loan (amount/rate/payment/note/payoff toggle)@1306-1327; cash-flow WHAT-IF slider@1331 (no history); grade/50-30-20 analysis; Klarna/BNPL bridge; calendar+reminders@8215-8280 (events, recurring); tax helper; Assistant. CONFIRMED MISSING (grep net.?worth/budget/assets/liabilit): NO net-worth, NO real budgets-with-limits, NO time-series/month-over-month history, NO multi-debt planner, NO reports. CSV bank-import exists (impBank@2016) = the honest offline substitute for bank-sync. Cross-read Maki's Jul-03 finance teardown (maki.md): converges — variable-income recap + net-worth "Wrapped" + payoff countdown are her shareable demos; a privacy-native GRADE card is her build-first. Design refs (gluttony): Copilot/Monarch monthly-income+spending DISCRETE BAR charts (why bars not sparkline for pay events); Monarch auto-categorize-with-manual-override (the auto-default / explicit-labeled-reversible-override pattern); YNAB budget-vs-actual (why it's a friction trap without transactions → reframed); Rocket Money subscription radar (keep radar+remind, CUT the paid cancel-concierge); snowball vs avalanche payoff math; median-vs-mean for volatile income (why typical=median).

- Found / verdict: **DESIGN DELIVERED — Task A is build-ready (decisive mechanics + redlines); Task B is a ranked/phased roadmap with honest CUTS. The unifying architectural steer: Task A must be built as a reusable MONTHLY-SNAPSHOT + HISTORY-CHART engine, because Net Worth, Money-History and Reports all ride the same prompt + chart component — build it once, plug in three times.**

### ========== TASK A — MONTHLY INCOME (mechanics + UI) ==========

**A. DATA MODEL (the new source of truth)**
- Add `STATE.incomeLog = []` to DEFAULTS@2258 — entries `{ ym:"2026-06", amt:18500, ts:<iso>, est:false }` (one per calendar month, keyed by `ym`=YYYY-MM). `est:true` marks an estimate/"usual" entry vs a confirmed real one. Include in export/import blob (line 6074) + `fixIds`.
- CURRENCY: incomeLog `amt` values are stored in the base currency and MUST be run through `cv()`@2198 on a currency switch exactly like MODEL.income (route to Kaito+Akashi).
- Derivation window: **the last 12 logged months** (a rolling year — a raise ages in, an old low stops haunting). Full history stored, derived from ≤12.

**B. DERIVED STATS — decisive math (justified)**
- **typical (avg key) = MEDIAN of the windowed months** (NOT mean). Median resists a freak month (bonus / missed paycheck) — critical because `avg` drives the hero "you keep", every projection, the grade and Klarna. One 3× bonus would balloon a mean and lie to the whole app; the median stays honest. (Ref: standard practice for volatile-income budgeting.)
- **low key = the lowest windowed month; high key = the highest windowed month.** (At ≥5 months a 20th/80th-percentile is a later refinement so a single freak doesn't own the band — note it, don't build it yet; min/max is clear + honest to start.)
- **average (mean)** = shown as a SEPARATE informational stat (the task asked for "…+ average"), never drives the app. So the history strip shows LOWEST · TYPICAL(median, the baseline) · HIGHEST · AVERAGE(mean) — four reads, typical highlighted.
- **Minimum sample before derived REPLACES the guess = 3 months.** <3 → keep today's manual guess, show a "Learning — n/3" state. Justify: you need ≥3 points for a meaningful low/typical/high SPREAD; 1-2 can't express a band. (Copilot/Monarch similarly wait ~2-3 cycles before "typical" is trustworthy.)

**C. THE END-OF-MONTH PROMPT (offline, no background jobs)**
- New `checkMonthlyIncome()` in the SAME on-open slot as morningBriefing/checkBirthday/maybeShowUpdatePanel (after unlock, ~600ms delay).
- DETECTION (pure open-time, no cron): `lastCompleteMonth` = the month *before* today's (today 2026-07-03 → "2026-06"). Prompt IFF: user has income listed (`hasJob || MODEL.income.avg>0`) AND no incomeLog entry for lastCompleteMonth AND not already dismissed for it. The month must have ROLLED OVER — never ask about a partial/current month.
- NON-NAG STATE: `STATE.incomeAsk = { ym, status:'pending|logged|skipped|snoozed', snoozeUntil }`. Skip → never re-ask that month (stays unlogged, un-nagged). Snooze("remind me later") → re-surface after ~2 days, max ~2 more times, then stop. One month asked per open (if away for months → ask the most recent complete unlogged month; offer a secondary "+ log earlier months" backfill, never a wall).
- SEQUENCING vs other on-open UI: the monthly ask is RARE → it WINS over the daily briefing when both are due, but never two modals at once (briefing is a toast/inline, income is a modal → they can coexist; if ever both modal, income first).
- TONE (serious, per CLAUDE.md): title "How much did you get paid in June?" · subhead "Enter your take-home for June so your plan runs on real numbers, not an estimate." No hype, no celebration emoji.
- AFFORDANCES (reuse the modal/uiConfirm infra):
  - Big number input, `.ccySuffix` currency, placeholder = current typical.
  - Primary **[Save]**.
  - Ghost chip **"Paid the usual ({typical})"** = one tap to fill+submit the current typical (logs a real, user-confirmed entry — keeps the log honest; NOT a silent fake). This is the "same as usual" affordance, done without polluting the data.
  - **"I wasn't paid this month"** → logs `amt:0` (a real zero — vital for gig/irregular workers; see edge cases).
  - **"Skip"** link (escape; records skip, no nag).
  - Everything editable later in the history card → nothing is a trap.

**D. INCOME-HISTORY VIEW (where + how)**
- LIVES: a NEW sub-card in the **Income tab**, positioned BETWEEN "Your Income"@1260 and "Tax Helper"@1276. Reading order becomes: [Your Income summary — the numbers the app uses] → [Income History — the months that produced them + trend + add/correct] → [Tax Helper]. History = the evidence directly under the summary it explains.
- CONTENTS:
  1. Derived strip: LOWEST · TYPICAL · HIGHEST · AVERAGE using the existing `.status/.stat` component (typical=cyan/highlighted). Caption "from your last {n} months."
  2. **TREND = discrete vertical BARS (hand-drawn SVG, no libs), NOT a sparkline.** One bar per windowed month (≤12), height ∝ amount (normalized to max), thin median baseline line across. Color: cyan typical-band, amber for a month below the low-ish threshold, lime above high — so volatility reads at a glance. Tap a bar → that month + amount. ~110-120px tall, bars flex to card width (responsive). Reuse the B2 ring's draw-on discipline (transition, reduced-motion off). RATIONALE: paychecks are DISCRETE monthly events — bars = "each month's pay", instantly legible to a 9-yo; a line implies false continuity. (Ref: Copilot/Monarch monthly bars.) THIS IS THE REUSABLE CHART for Net Worth + Money History (Task B).
  3. Month LIST (reverse-chron), `.item-row`: "June 2026 — 18,500 kr", est-marker on estimated entries, tap → correct amount / delete (the B1 add/remove pattern). Recompute derived on change.
  4. Empty state: "No months logged yet. At the end of each month, MRLN asks what you were paid — after a few months your plan runs on real numbers." Learning state (<3): derived strip greyed + "Learning — {n} of 3 months logged. Using your setup estimate until then."

**E. RECONCILIATION — derived vs manual setup vs Assistant "income is now 2600" (the crux, decisive)**
- TWO DISTINCT ACTIONS, never conflated:
  1. **Logging a month** (the prompt / "+add past month" / a correction) → feeds derived. The real-data path.
  2. **Overriding typical** (Setup, or Assistant "income is now X") → sets `MODEL.incomeOverride={avg,ts,source}`. An explicit thumb on the scale.
- WHAT THE APP USES for income.avg, in order:
  - If `incomeOverride` set AND newer than the latest logged month → USE OVERRIDE, with a visible note "Manual — using your set value, not your logged average. [Use my history instead]".
  - Else if ≥3 windowed months → USE DERIVED (median). Income table shows "computed from your last N months" and becomes read-only.
  - Else → USE the stored setup guess (exactly as today; zero behavior change pre-data).
- **Logging any NEW real month CLEARS the override** — the override is a stopgap; the user's freshest concrete action supersedes it. This makes overrides self-expiring and kills "why is my income wrong" mysteries.
- ANSWER to "which wins once real data exists?": **Real data (derived) wins by default; an explicit manual override wins over derived but only until the next real month is logged, and it is ALWAYS visibly labeled 'Manual' with a one-tap 'Use my history' escape.** Auto-by-default, manual as an explicit/labeled/trivially-reversible override, never a hidden state (Ref: Monarch auto+override). 9-yo-recoverable: there's always a visible button back to automatic.
- `setIncome()`@3691 wiring: the Assistant/Setup path routes to `incomeOverride`, NOT directly into MODEL.income, once ≥3 months exist; below 3 it writes the guess as today. Kaito to thread this so hero/scenarios/grade/Klarna all read the resolved value (a single `resolveIncome()` helper returning {low,avg,high} from override-else-derived-else-guess, called wherever MODEL.income is read today).

**F. EDGE CASES (decided)**
1. First / <3 months → setup guess + "Learning n/3".
2. Missed month (skipped) → derivation just uses the months present (no imputation); trend shows a gap so volatility is visible.
3. Zero month (logged 0) → COUNTS (a real unemployed/gig gap is honest data; pulls low+typical down correctly). It won't nuke the hero because typical=MEDIAN absorbs a lone zero — a zero only dominates if MOST months are zero, which is the truth then. The median choice already handles this.
4. Wildly irregular pay → median typical + min/max band + the trend bars express the volatility; add a factual note when max/min > ~2× ("Your income varies month to month — plans use your typical/median."). Nice-to-have, not required.
5. Correction to a past month → history row → edit/delete → recompute (B1 pattern); est→confirmed on edit.
6. Away for months → ask the most recent complete unlogged month; secondary "+ log earlier months" backfill; never force old months.
7. Currency switch → convert incomeLog via cv() like MODEL.income (Kaito+Akashi).
8. POISON: derived stats feed leftOver → must carry the `__sys.token()` thread like MODEL.income does today (leftOver already ×token). @Akashi: the new incomeLog figures + resolveIncome() output need the poison thread; the trend chart should DISPLAY only (ratios/heights), never leak a raw kr under tamper.
- @Mikoto strings (interim EN, sync 7 langs when Kaito locks): prompt title/subhead ("How much did you get paid in {month}?", "Enter your take-home for {month}…"), "Paid the usual ({n})", "I wasn't paid this month", "Skip", "remind me later", history card title "Income History", strip labels LOWEST/TYPICAL/HIGHEST/AVERAGE, "from your last {n} months", "Learning — {n} of 3 months logged. Using your setup estimate until then.", empty-state, "Manual — using your set value, not your logged average.", "Use my history instead", "computed from your last {n} months". All length-sensitive (DE/HU expand around the money + the month name).

### ========== TASK B — OFFLINE FINANCE ROADMAP (ranked, phased, honest cuts) ==========
Framing: these apps' magic is BANK-SYNC (auto transaction import) — off the table by MRLN's charter (offline/private/free; the CSV import@2016 is our honest substitute). So each feature is judged on: does manual entry stay LIGHT, and is the payoff high? The ones needing transaction-level auto-data to be worth it are CUT.

**P0 — NET-WORTH TRACKER (manual balances + assets − debts, over time).** THE iconic wealth-app feature, 100% offline (just numbers you type), and it rides Task A's engine. What: two lists (Assets / Debts); Net Worth = Σassets − Σdebts; a monthly SNAPSHOT logged via the SAME end-of-month prompt ("Update your balances for June?" — optional expansion, never forced) → a net-worth-over-time chart (REUSE Task A's bar component). Auto-include existing savingsBoxes as assets + the loan as a debt, so the user only adds what's untracked. UI: new tab (FINANCES group) — big Net Worth hero (reuse hero pattern), Assets/Debts lists (B1 pattern), trend chart. WHY P0: most iconic, fully offline-honest, and MARGINAL cost is low because it reuses A's prompt+chart+list machinery. Cost: MEDIUM (low if built right after A). @Akashi: manual balances, no network.

**P1 — SUBSCRIPTION & RECURRING RADAR (Rocket Money, reframed).** Cheapest high-value win: the DATA ALREADY EXISTS (expense items carry freq monthly/yearly). What: a focused view of everything recurring, sorted by cost, ANNUALIZED ("14 recurring items = 4,200 kr/yr"), next-charge dates pushed into the existing calendar/reminders, yearly-billed items flagged so an annual hit isn't a surprise, and a "trim your biggest" prompt. CUT the paid cancel-for-you concierge (impossible offline + costs money) — keep radar+remind. UI: a card in the Subscriptions/Expenses tab. WHY P1 (borderline P0 on cost): near-free (pure presentation of existing model), matches a famous app, offline-honest. Cost: LOW.

**P1 — MONEY HISTORY (month-over-month), on the Task A engine.** What: "your money over time" — income (from incomeLog), total cost, leftover, per month, as bars/lines + "this month vs last." Offline mechanic: income from the log; SNAPSHOT `GRAND` (total monthly cost, one number) at each month-end prompt so cost history accrues → {ym, income, cost, leftover}. WHY P1: the universal "am I improving?" view, but DEPENDS on Task A shipping first + adds a silent cost snapshot. Reuses the chart. Cost: LOW-MEDIUM (post-A).

**P1 — DEBT PAYOFF PLANNER (snowball/avalanche) — expand the single loan to multi-debt.** What: list debts (balance, APR, min payment); pick snowball (smallest balance first) or avalanche (highest APR first); enter extra/mo → payoff date + total interest + per-debt order. Pure deterministic math, fully offline. WHY P1: iconic, 100% offline, but it's a NEW multi-debt data model (loan is singular today) + a moderate calc/UI build; less universal than net worth. UI: expand the Loan tab into "Loan & Debt" (debts list + planner card). Cost: MEDIUM.

**P1 — SPENDING PLAN (the HONEST reframe of "budgets").** BRUTAL TRUTH: YNAB/Mint budgets track SPENDING vs a limit → needs transaction data we don't have; MRLN's expenses are fixed recurring costs, not variable spend. A naive "budget vs actual" offline = a friction trap (log every purchase → abandoned). REFRAME: an ALLOCATION planner — split your typical leftover across flexible buckets (eating out, fun, extra savings) with target caps, shown as a 50/30/20-style ring/bars. It PLANS the leftover instead of tracking transactions, extending the EXISTING grade's 50/30/20 logic into something interactive. WHY P1 not P0: valuable but overlaps the existing grade/tier, and the full transaction-tracking version is the friction trap → I keep only the planner. Cost: MEDIUM. (If Osefe insists on transaction tracking, flag me — I'll argue it hard before we build a homework feature.)

**P2 — YEAR-IN-REVIEW / REPORTS ("Wrapped").** Once income+cost+net-worth logs exist (A + P0/P1), a "2026 in review" (highest/lowest month, total saved, net-worth change, biggest category). Cheap once the data exists, VALUELESS before it accrues → strictly P2, after the logs fill. (Maki flags this as a later/low-share item — agree on timing.) Cost: LOW (post-data).

**P2 — PROACTIVE LOCAL ALERTS (overspend / low-surplus trend).** The hero already goes red + the grade already warns; a standalone "leftover dropped 3 months running" needs the history (P1) first and largely DUPLICATES existing signals. RECOMMEND: FOLD into the Money-History card as a one-line read, do NOT build standalone. Cost: LOW (a fold).

**BRUTAL CUTS (won't build, why):**
- Automatic bank sync / transaction import — off the charter (offline/private/free); CSV import@2016 is the honest substitute. Don't chase it.
- Full transaction-level budget tracking — offline friction trap → replaced by the Spending Plan allocation planner.
- Bill-negotiation / sub-cancellation concierge (Rocket Money's paid human service) — impossible offline + costs money → radar+remind only.
- Investment/portfolio LIVE tracking — needs live prices (network, forbidden) → net worth uses MANUAL balances only.
- Credit-score monitoring — needs a bureau API (network, often paid) → cut.

**BUILD ORDER (value ÷ cost ÷ dependency):** Task A (the engine) → Subscription Radar (cheapest, data exists, momentum) → Net Worth (P0 value, reuses A) → Money History (needs A) → Debt Planner → Spending Plan → Reports (P2, post-data) → alerts fold-in.

- Specs given: the above, routed to Kaito (owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review). Task A is build-ready; Task B P0/P1 at design level (flag me for build-ready redlines per feature when Kaito reaches it). KEY STEER: build Task A's monthly-snapshot prompt + history-bar-chart + resolveIncome() as REUSABLE pieces — Net Worth / Money History / Reports all ride them.
- Still open / next: (1) @Kaito: Task A first (data model + prompt + history card + reconciliation/resolveIncome + edge cases); I re-review LIVE per piece. (2) @Akashi flags: incomeLog figures + resolveIncome output need the __sys.token() poison thread; incomeLog cv() on currency switch; the monthly prompt is a new on-open UI (no network). (3) @Mikoto: the Task-A string set above once Kaito locks EN. (4) Task B build-ready specs on demand, in build order. (5) Converges with Maki's Jul-03 teardown (variable-income recap + net-worth Wrapped = her shareable demos; the derived typical/median IS the variable-income story). Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ a4ba0b8.

- Asked: Osefe wants BOTH decided builds "seamless, ALIVE, with animations and great sounds." Spec both to build: (1) BUDGET tab = merge read-only Expenses(03) + Subscriptions(09 editor) into ONE show+edit tab, fold renewal dates in; (2) GRADE card = on-screen living loop + brand-crafted 1080×1350 shareable export. Exact motion timings/curves consistent with our tiers, sound-cue map to MRLN_SFX, reduced-motion fallback, Akashi/Mikoto flags. Director-only; Kaito builds.
- Studied / referenced: GROUNDED @ tip a4ba0b8. Grade engine: `tierOf(score)`@4041 (S≥88/A≥74/B≥60/C≥46/D≥32/E<32), `tierRGB`, `overall=fin*0.55+body*0.45` (or fin) /100 @3509/3570, `#statsOverall` badge@3571, `#hdrGrade` pill@1275, `M.dataComplete` gate, `fac()` factors@3364 → `.meter` bars (@343-347 `.meter>span` lime/amber, ALREADY draw-on via Wave-A meterDraw A4 scaleX 700ms), ranked `add()` recommendations (priority + actionable text + target tab, e.g. "Pay yourself first"/"Close your slow-month gap"). Existing QR generator `showQR`/`qrCanvas`@6334 (OFFLINE, reuse for the export handle — no new lib). Budget merge targets: Expenses(03)@1285-1303 (read-only expGroups accordion + subtotal), Subscriptions(09)@1549-1587 = the REAL editor (Add form@1552 + "Manage Items"@1567 customList `renderManage`@3734 inline edit via `editId` + "Subscription Ledger"@1578 `renderLedger`@3363). Renewal already modeled: `it.until` → "🔁 until {date}"/"✅ paid off"@3753-3756. Motion primitives to reuse: `countUp`@2804 (420ms expo-out, number-only), `tapFeedback` ripple+haptic@8457 (fires on `.btn,.tab,.scen-btn,.savebtn,.chattoggle` + `.item-row/.exp-head` ripple hosts), `assembleIn` A5@801 (260ms/40ms stagger cap-8), `meterDraw` A4, `valPulse`@727 (scale 1.08), slideFromR/L, `modalIn`/`toastIn`, rowBorn/rowDie (B1). Tokens `--ease-out:cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)`, `--ease-hud:cubic-bezier(.25,0,0,1)`. Deep-links/tour/assistant that name "subs"/"Subscriptions" and must repoint to Budget: hint@1378, jump@6285, tour@8166/8172, assistant help@5229. Refs (gluttony): Apple/Things inline-edit morph (row flips in place, no jump), YNAB/Copilot category share-bars, Cash App/Robinhood count-up on value change, iOS camera shutter recoil+flash for the "capture" moment, Destiny/CP2077 HUD readout assemble for the grade reveal.

- Found / verdict: **DESIGN DELIVERED — both builds specced to implement. Core principle for BOTH: no re-render should TELEPORT. Every state change (read→edit, value change, add, delete, grade move) is a short GPU transition + one sound + (touch) an 8ms haptic, so the app feels sensed, not stapled. All motion under the existing @media(prefers-reduced-motion:no-preference) + global reduce-wildcard backstop; sound is a separate toggle and still plays under reduced-motion.**

### ================= BUILD 1 — BUDGET TAB (merge 03 Expenses + 09 Subscriptions) =================
Kills the app's worst wart: data shown in one tab, editable only in another. ONE tab that shows AND edits.

**LAYOUT (3 cards, top→bottom):**
1. **SUMMARY HERO** — the focal number. Total/month (big, Orbitron) + Total/year (secondary), reuse the `.subtotal .stat` pair. On tab open the month total `countUp`s from 0 (first paint) / from prior (updates). Optional alive touch: a one-line "where it goes" = the top-3 categories as `.meter` share-bars (each category's % of GRAND), drawn with meterDraw staggered 60ms — teaches the 9-yo "biggest bar = biggest cost" and pre-stages Task-B Spending Plan. NO new money leak concern (own tab).
2. **THE LEDGER (show+edit, the merge)** — categories as the existing collapsible `.exp-group` accordion; each row = name · monthly cost · freq · renewal, with Edit + Delete, and the row flips to an INLINE editor in place (the `renderManage` editId pattern, but morphed not teleported — see motion). Per-category "+ Add a cost" in the group header (pre-fills that category). This fuses `renderExpenses` (pretty read) + `renderManage` (editor) into one component.
3. **ADD A COST** — keep ONE global add affordance at the bottom (name/category/cost/freq/renewal-date/note), for "add something brand new." Inline per-category add covers the common case; the global card covers discovery. Do NOT keep a separate "Ledger" read-card — the ledger IS card 2 now.
- Tab list: **Budget replaces Expenses(03)**; **remove Subscriptions(09)**; repoint the 5 deep-links/tour/assistant refs above to `data-p="budget"`.

**MOTION (the "seamless" is here):**
- **Tab entrance:** cards `assembleIn` (A5, already fires on tab switch, 260ms/40ms cap-8). Summary count-up + category share-bars meterDraw on arrival = a living dashboard, not a form.
- **Category expand/collapse:** reuse the `.exp-group` accordion + existing rowIn stagger (25ms/row on open). Sound `panel` on EXPAND only (rising unfold); collapse silent.
- **Read→Edit (the signature seamless move):** tap Edit (or the row) → row morphs in place: the read content cross-fades out (opacity 120ms) while the edit inputs fade+rise in (`translateY(6px)→0` + opacity, 180ms var(--ease-out)); row gains a cyan edit-border that eases in (border-color .18s); cost input auto-focuses with the A6 lime/cyan focus ring. No height jump — reserve the row's min-height. Sound `toggle`.
- **Save edit (the alive payoff):** inputs collapse back to read view; the row's monthly figure `countUp`s old→new (reuse countUp on the row number) so the price change is SEEN; row-born lime flash (`rowBorn` 520ms) confirms it landed. Sound `save` + haptic. This is the moment: edit 40→50, tap Save, number spins 40→50 with a lime pulse + save chime.
- **Cancel:** inputs collapse to read, no flash. Sound soft `tap`.
- **Add row:** B1 `rowBorn` enter (opacity + translateY(-6px) + lime-bg flash, 520ms expo-out) — new row materializes at the top of its category. Sound `save` + haptic. (Coin is WRONG here — adding a bill is not "income up"; coin stays income-only.)
- **Delete row:** B1 `rowDie` leave (translateX(12px)+opacity+collapse, 260ms ease-in) THEN the real removal+re-render (defer the mutation, B1 pattern — @Akashi glance the deferred-delete on poison-carrying `removeItem`). Sound `remove` (down-whoosh) + haptic.
- **Renewal fold-in (the Subscription-Radar seed):** each row shows dim "🔁 renews {date}" / "✅ paid off {date}"; if a renewal is within 7 days → amber "🔁 renews in {n} days". Ties to the existing calendar. Light, no new tab.
- Every button uses `.btn` classes → inherits tapFeedback ripple + 8ms haptic; rows are already ripple hosts.

**REDUCED-MOTION:** wildcard kills assembleIn/rowBorn/rowDie/countUp-tween/meterDraw/morph → cards present, rows add/remove instantly, numbers land instantly, bars at target, edit swaps instantly. Fully usable, dignified. Sounds still fire.

### ================= BUILD 2 — GRADE CARD (living loop + shareable export) =================
~20% net-new on the shipped Stats & Grades engine (`tierOf`/`overall`/`fac()`/`add()`). Lives as the FIRST card in the Stats tab (assembles first, delay 0).

**A) ON-SCREEN LIVING LOOP — LAYOUT:**
- **Grade hero:** the tier LETTER (S–E, Orbitron 900, huge, semantic tierRGB color) + "{score}/100" beside/under it + label "Financial Health Grade".
- **Movement delta:** a chip "↑ from C last month" (lime up / amber down / dim "no change"). Baseline = grade stored in the MONTHLY snapshot engine (Task A) → cadence matches the income prompt = "since last month" (retention-aligned). Store `score`+`tier` in each monthly snapshot record.
- **Factor bars:** the `fac()` factors as `.meter` bars (grades/ratios, NO money), lime/cyan/amber by factor score.
- **Held-back + CTA:** "Held back by: {weakest factor}" then ONE filled focal button "Lift your grade → {action}" = the top-priority `add()` recommendation, deep-linking to its target tab. One action, not a list (9-yo bar).

**GRADE-CARD MOTION:**
- **Entrance:** `assembleIn` (A5) as the first Stats card.
- **Grade reveal (count-up + letter snap + glow build):** score number `countUp` 0→overall (420ms expo-out). The LETTER lands WITH the count-up completion: starts `scale(1.4) opacity(0) blur(6px)` → `scale(1) opacity(1) blur(0)` 420ms var(--ease-out), then a one-shot bloom `@keyframes gradeBloom{0%{text-shadow:none}40%{text-shadow:0 0 24px <tierRGB>}100%{text-shadow:0 0 10px <tierRGB>}}` 600ms. HUD readout powering on — serious, not toy (no bounce/overshoot).
- **Factor bars:** meterDraw scaleX, staggered 60ms/bar (sequential HUD read). The WEAKEST bar gets a subtle amber attention-pulse after the draw, tied to the "Held back by" text (connect eye→bar→text).
- **Held-back + CTA reveal:** fade+rise in AFTER bars finish (delay ~700ms). CTA is a filled `.btn` (tapFeedback ripple+haptic+hover-glow); on hover/focus (desktop) it pulses the corresponding weak factor bar so a child sees "this button fixes THAT bar."
- **Live in-session delta:** when a Budget/income edit changes the grade while the card is visible, the score countUps to the new value + valPulse + a transient "+2"/"−1" micro-chip. Immediate cause→effect feedback.

**GRADE SOUND MAP (one cue per reveal — no chord):** on count-up landing → **tier UP vs last month = `success`** (rising chime; **big jump ≥2 tiers = `streak`**), **tier DOWN = `remove`** (soft down-whoosh, NOT `error` — the user didn't do wrong), **no change = `panel`** (soft rising readout). "Lift your grade" tap → **`ping`** (bright forward ping). These moments never collide.

**B) SHAREABLE EXPORT (1080×1350 HUD card, canvas, offline, zero kr):**
- **Capture flourish (the satisfying moment):** tap "Share my grade" → (1) a cyan-white FLASH sweep over the card `@keyframes captureFlash{0%{opacity:0}20%{opacity:.5}100%{opacity:0}}` 380ms; (2) a tiny shutter recoil `scale(1→0.98→1)` 240ms; (3) sound **`coin`** at the flash peak (bright collectible "got it" — contextually a capture, not income); (4) the generated image slides up in a preview modal (`modalIn`) with Demo/Blur toggle + Share/Download.
- **Export image contents (top→bottom):** MRLN diamond + wordmark · the GRADE hero (huge, tierRGB, glow) · "Financial Health Grade" · the factor bars (grades/ratios ONLY) · tagline "Rate your money in 2 minutes · offline · private" · small handle + QR (reuse `showQR`, offline). Neon-on-dark #05080f + grid texture + glow — a HUD readout hitting a cold feed, not a `<div>`. Render at 2× (2160×2700) for crisp downscale; uses self-hosted Orbitron/Share Tech Mono (already embedded — no CDN).
- **Demo/Blur toggle:** "Share my grade" vs "Share the demo" — demo uses DEFAULT_MODEL (neutral sample) so a cautious user + the creator can post the FORMAT without exposing their result. **Zero real currency figures on the card by design — grade + ratios only** (the privacy-native property that makes it sendable at all).
- **REDUCED-MOTION (export):** skip the flash sweep + shutter recoil → straight to the preview modal (modalIn self-gates). Sound still fires.

**AKASHI FLAGS (@Akashi):**
1. **Export canvas must leak ZERO kr** — verify the drawn image contains NO income/expense/leftover figure; only grade letter, /100 score, factor grades/ratios, tagline, handle. Confirm.
2. **isFinite guard on a tampered copy** — grade/score come from the poisoned pipeline (leftOver × token); under tamper they NaN. The card AND export must degrade to "—" / refuse to render, NEVER draw a forged S-tier on a tampered copy that then gets shared. Guard `isFinite` before both render + export.
3. **Outbound pointer** — the handle/QR encodes a join URL (Discord) baked into a shared image. Static branding, no data leaves device, reuses the existing offline QR gen — but it's a new outbound reference; sign it.
4. **B1 deferred-delete** (Budget) glance on poison-carrying `removeItem`.

**MIKOTO (@Mikoto, once Kaito locks EN):** Budget — tab "Budget", "+ Add a cost", "renews in {n} days"/"renews {date}", card titles (Edit/Save/Cancel/Delete already exist). Grade — "Financial Health Grade", "Held back by: {factor}", "Lift your grade", "↑ from {tier} last month"/"no change", "Share my grade", "Share the demo", "Rate your money in 2 minutes · offline · private". RECOMMEND the EXPORT image defaults EN-primary for reach (flag — her call). All length-sensitive (DE/HU expand; the grade hero letter is language-free).

- Specs given: the above, routed to Kaito (owner of index.html; find-xor-fix → he builds, I re-review LIVE per piece). Both build-ready. Build order suggestion: Budget merge first (kills the wart, unblocks daily loop), then Grade card (rides the reach wave).
- Still open / next: (1) @Kaito builds; I re-review live (watch: Budget read→edit morph has no height-jump + save count-up lands exactly on the new figure + rowDie defers the real mutation; Grade letter-snap lands WITH the count-up + one sound per reveal + export draws zero kr + isFinite-guards under tamper). (2) @Akashi 4 flags above. (3) @Mikoto string set once EN locks. (4) Repoint the 5 subs/Subscriptions deep-links to Budget. (5) Store grade score+tier in the monthly-snapshot record for the "since last month" delta. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ a4ba0b8.

## [2026-07-03] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — QUALITY-BAR RESET: Budget tab REDESIGN to AAA (Display default · single Edit toggle · in-place morph) + the reusable Display/Edit standard
- Asked: Osefe called the shipped Budget tab a FAIL (edit-mode was the DEFAULT = a wall of Edit/Delete on every row; "NaN kr/mo" everywhere; a spreadsheet, not premium). New standing bar: "multi-million-dollar app, millions of users all ages, turn creativity UP, everything must feel EXPENSIVE." Redesign Budget fully, mobile-first: DISPLAY is the resting state (clean/calm, name+monthly price, category subtotals, big costs visualised, ZERO edit/delete buttons); ONE Edit toggle reveals edit/delete/add; the mode change MORPHS in place (no reload/teleport); premium at every touch. Make this display↔edit toggle a REUSABLE standard. Concrete implementable redlines. Director-only; Kaito builds + screenshot-verifies premium before ship.
- I OWN THE MISS: my prior Budget spec put "Edit + Delete" on every read row and called each row "inline-editable (tap→edit)". That IS the wall Kaito faithfully built. Objectivity applies to my own work — this is a real design error, not a build error. Correcting it properly below.
- Studied / referenced: confirmed `.savebar`/`.chattoggle` are `position:fixed; right:18px; bottom:18px` (@489/500) → **bottom-right is TAKEN by the Assistant; the Budget mode-toggle must NOT live there.** `uiConfirm`@6243 (current delete dialogs) + the bottom-center toast infra@8117 (for the premium undo pattern). renderManage editId-per-row innerHTML swap@3734 = the teleport to REPLACE. Tokens: 8pt grid, `--ease-out:cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)`, `.meter`@343 (share-bars), countUp@2804, tapFeedback ripple+haptic@8457, assembleIn@801, meterDraw A4, IntersectionObserver precedent (B4). Refs (gluttony, named): **iOS large-title→inline-title collapse-on-scroll** (Apple Wallet/Contacts/Reminders — the "hero condenses to a sticky bar" premium move + the top-right "Edit" that every 60-yo already knows); **Copilot/Monarch descending-by-spend category layout + the clean right-aligned tabular money column** (the single strongest "premium finance" signal); **Notion/Linear inline-commit-on-blur** (no per-row Save button = calm); **Things/iOS list-edit control-reveal** (delete slides in, rows don't reload); **undo-toast over confirm-dialog** (Gmail/iOS — premium low-friction safety).

- Found / verdict: **REDESIGN — Budget as two states of ONE never-rebuilt DOM. The whole tab has a resting DISPLAY mode (calm, read-only, a clean money column you can just LOOK at) and an EDIT mode entered by ONE toggle; the difference between them is a class on the panel that CSS transitions — controls pre-exist hidden and reveal in place (a true morph, never innerHTML). The "expensive" is: the hero total spins up, category bars draw in, and — the signature move — entering edit makes each row's controls cascade in top-to-bottom like the interface arming. Row geometry is byte-identical across modes so nothing reflows.**

### CORE ARCHITECTURE (the redline that makes it premium, not a re-render)
- Render each row ONCE, containing BOTH the display price (text) AND a hidden inline editor (input+freq+delete), plus category tools (+Add / ✕category) hidden. Toggle `#budget.editing` on the panel. CSS reveals/hides the edit affordances via transform/opacity/border transitions. **NEVER call renderManage's editId innerHTML swap for mode changes** — that's the teleport. Re-render ONLY on actual data change (add/delete/commit), and even then animate the delta (count-up / rowBorn / rowDie).
- **isFinite guard everywhere a number prints:** pre-token-arm or tampered → show a **skeleton shimmer** on the number (a 1.2s cyan shimmer sweep placeholder), NEVER "NaN". When the value resolves (post-arm) → count-up from the skeleton. This turns Kaito's NaN bug into the "expensive loading" continuity Osefe wants. (@Akashi: isFinite→skeleton/"—", never a printed NaN or a forged figure.)

### DISPLAY MODE (resting state — mobile-first, 16px panel padding)
**1. HERO SUMMARY (top card, the "expensive landing"):**
- "TOTAL PER MONTH" micro-label (Share Tech Mono 10px, .2em, --txt-dim) + the total (Orbitron 900, clamp(32px,9vw,44px), #eaffff, `countUp` on entry, skeleton until armed). Secondary line: "≈ {yr}/year" (Rajdhani 13px --txt-dim).
- One context line: "Biggest: {category} · {n} items · {r} renewing soon" (Share Tech Mono 11px). Serious, factual.
- Top-right: the **Edit pill** (see toggle). 
**2. CATEGORY SECTIONS (sorted by subtotal DESCENDING — biggest money first, the premium finance convention):**
- Header: icon (18px) + name (Orbitron 14px #eaffff) LEFT; subtotal (Rajdhani 16px, #eaffff, tabular) RIGHT. margin-top 24px.
- **Share-bar** directly under header: full-width `.meter`, height 6px, radius 3px, margin-top 8px, width = category % of GRAND, `meterDraw` staggered 60ms on entry. Color ramps by size (biggest = cyan/bright, smaller = cyan-dim) so "where the money goes" reads in one glance. THIS is "big costs visualised," upgraded.
- Items under it, sorted by cost descending: **row = name (Rajdhani 15px --txt) LEFT · monthly price (Rajdhani 15px 600, #eaffff, tabular-nums) RIGHT.** min-height 48px, padding 12px 0, faint hairline divider (rgba(27,58,92,.35)) between items for the ledger-scan. Optional dim sub-line under the name (Share Tech Mono 10px --txt-dim): "🔁 renews Jul 15" / "≠monthly → {orig}/yr" / amber "🔁 renews in 5 days" if ≤7d. **NO buttons. NO edit/delete. Nothing tappable that looks clickable.** The money column (right-aligned, tabular, identical grouping via fmtN) is the visual spine — that column alone is the premium signal.
- 24px between a category's last item and the next header. Panel bottom-padding 88px (clear the chat toggle + the sticky Done).
**3. The clean-read discipline:** one accent (cyan, on bars/subtotals), everything else calm --txt/--txt-dim. No per-item bars (category-level only — bar overload kills calm). Collapsible categories = OPTIONAL enhancement (default expanded — "see ALL your money" is the point); if added, a dim chevron on the header, `panel` sound on expand.

### THE MODE TOGGLE (one control, all ages, thumb-aware, non-colliding)
- **iOS large-title collapse pattern:** the Edit pill sits top-right of the HERO while it's in view. On scroll, when the hero scrolls off (IntersectionObserver sentinel), a slim **sticky top bar** slides down (height 52px, dark blur bg, bottom hairline): "TOTAL {n}/mo" LEFT + the Edit pill top-RIGHT — so your key number AND the toggle follow you, always same position. Premium + familiar to a 60-yo (top-right "Edit") and legible to a 9-yo.
- The pill: min 44×44 tap target, ghost cyan "✎ Edit" → on tap becomes filled cyan "✓ Done". Label+icon crossfade + fill animate 200ms.
- **Thumb reach on EXIT:** in edit mode, ALSO render a large "✓ Done" bar after the last category (full-width, 48px) so exiting never requires scrolling back to the top — enter is a deliberate top action, exit is always thumb-reachable. Considered asymmetry, not clutter.

### EDIT MODE (entered, never default) — THE MORPH
- Toggle `#budget.editing`. What reveals, IN PLACE (no reload):
  - Each row's price TEXT gains input chrome — a border + inset bg fade in around it (`transition: background .2s, border-color .2s`) so the number becomes an editable field WITHOUT moving. Freq chip + a "✕" delete slide in from the right (`translateX(10px)→0` + opacity, `.24s var(--ease-out)`).
  - **The cascade (the expensive beat):** rows reveal their controls top-to-bottom, `transition-delay: calc(var(--i) * 34ms)` (var --i = row index, CAP at 10 → 11th+ snap so a 40-item budget never lags). Reads as "the interface is arming."
  - Category headers reveal "+ Add item" + "✕ category". A global "+ New category" appears at the end. The sticky bar's total stays live.
- **Inline commit on BLUR — no per-row Save/Cancel buttons** (that was half the wall). Change a price, tap away → it commits. On a committed change: the price `countUp`s old→new in place + a `rowBorn` lime flash + the category subtotal AND hero total recompute with count-up + `save` sound + haptic. Cause→effect, alive, zero button clutter.
- **Add item:** tap "+ Add item" on a category → a blank edit-row `rowBorn`s at the top of that category, name+price auto-focused (A6 ring). `save` sound on commit.
- **Delete:** "✕" → `rowDie` leave (translateX(12px)+opacity+collapse 260ms) → defer the real mutation (B1) → **a 5s bottom toast "Deleted {name} · Undo"** (reuse toast@8117), NOT a uiConfirm dialog. Undo restores the item+index. Premium, low-friction, safe for a 9-yo's mis-tap. `remove` down-whoosh on delete; soft `tick` on undo. (Category delete KEEPS uiConfirm — deleting a whole category with N items is heavy enough to warrant the confirm.)
- **Exit (Done):** remove `.editing` → controls slide back out (translateX(10px)+opacity0), input chrome fades off, ~180ms, snappier reverse (no long stagger). Rows return to the calm read. `tap` (soft) sound.

### SOUND MAP (MRLN_SFX)
- Enter edit → `panel` (rising unfold, "tools arming") + ONE firmer haptic `navigator.vibrate(12)` to mark the mode shift. Exit/Done → `tap` (soft settle).
- Price commit (blur w/ change) → `save` + count-up + lime flash. Add → `save` + rowBorn. Delete → `remove` + rowDie; Undo → `tick`.
- Category expand (if collapsible) → `panel`. All the `.btn`/pill controls inherit tapFeedback ripple + 8ms haptic. (Adding a cost is NOT `coin` — coin stays income-only; adding a bill isn't "good news".)

### PREMIUM AT EVERY TOUCH
- **Desktop hover:** DISPLAY rows have no buttons → hover = a faint full-row bg lift (rgba(0,229,255,.05), .15s) so the cursor feels sensed WITHOUT implying a false click; the Edit pill gets the full hover-glow; category headers (if collapsible) a clearer hover. EDIT rows: hover raises the delete ✕ + input affordances; inputs use the A6 cyan focus ring.
- **Mobile:** the mode toggle, add, delete, commit all fire the 8ms tapFeedback haptic; mode-enter fires the 12ms mark. Generous 48px rhythm = thumb-legible for all ages.
- **Entrance continuity:** tab open → cards assembleIn, hero total count-up, category bars meterDraw in sequence = "expensive as I scroll," continuous from the boot screen.

### REDUCED-MOTION
- Global wildcard kills assembleIn/cascade/meterDraw/count-up-tween/rowBorn/rowDie/shimmer → display shows instantly, edit mode toggles instantly (class flip, controls just present), numbers land, bars at target, add/delete instant, skeleton → static "—" until armed then the value (no tween). Fully usable, dignified. Sounds still fire (separate toggle).

### THE REUSABLE STANDARD — "MRLN Display/Edit surface" (point cluttered tabs at this)
Codified rules, so this isn't a one-off:
1. **Resting state carries ZERO edit/delete/destructive controls** — read-only, calm, the data is the hero.
2. **ONE clearly-labeled mode toggle**, same position every time (top-right, collapsing to a sticky bar on scroll; never bottom-right — the Assistant owns it).
3. **Controls pre-exist in the DOM and reveal via a panel class + CSS transition** — NEVER an innerHTML swap for a mode change (that's the teleport that kills "expensive").
4. **Row geometry identical across modes** — no reflow/jump when toggling.
5. **Inline commit on blur — no per-row Save/Cancel buttons.**
6. **Delete = undo-toast, not a confirm-dialog** (reserve confirm for heavy/bulk destroys like a whole category).
7. **Every printed number isFinite-guarded → skeleton shimmer / "—", never NaN or a forged value.**
8. **One accent color, tabular right-aligned money column, 8pt rhythm, 44px+ targets.**
CANDIDATES to migrate next (all currently show inline edit/delete in their resting view): Savings boxes, Personal Records, Media Log, Reminders, Notebook list. Flag me per surface.

**@Akashi:** (1) isFinite guard → skeleton/"—", never NaN/forged figure, on every Budget number pre-arm + under tamper. (2) B1 deferred-delete + the 5s undo buffer holds a MODEL item out-of-tree for 5s then either restores or the mutation already ran — glance the poison path on removeItem. (3) IntersectionObserver sentinel for the sticky-bar (layout read only, B4 precedent).
**@Mikoto (once EN locks):** "TOTAL PER MONTH", "≈ {n}/year", "Biggest: {cat}", "{n} items", "{r} renewing soon", "renews {date}", "renews in {n} days", "✎ Edit", "✓ Done", "+ Add item", "+ New category", "✕ category", "Deleted {name}", "Undo". Length-sensitive (DE/HU expand; "Edit"/"Done" on a 44px pill — DE "Bearbeiten"/"Fertig" fit if the pill flexes).

- Specs given: the full redesign above, routed to Kaito (owner; find-xor-fix → he builds + screenshot-verifies premium before ship). Build the never-rebuilt two-state DOM + the class-toggle morph FIRST (that's the architecture); then the display polish (money column, descending sort, bars), then edit reveal + undo. 
- Still open / next: (1) @Kaito builds; I re-review LIVE + on his screenshots (watch: resting view has ZERO edit/delete buttons; row geometry identical across modes so the morph doesn't jump; commit count-ups land exactly; NaN → skeleton never prints; Done reachable without scrolling up; descending-by-spend sort; the money column aligns). (2) @Akashi 3 flags. (3) @Mikoto strings. (4) Roll the Display/Edit standard to the 5 candidate surfaces after Budget proves it. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ a4ba0b8.

## 2026-07-28 — Landing page review (scratchpad/landing.html, Hilton/premium bar, ages 16-40)
- **Asked:** review final light landing page vs billion-dollar bar; verdict + mustFix + praise.
- **Studied/verified:** ran WCAG contrast math on the token palette (python). Findings are measured, not eyeballed.
- **Found (AA blockers, verified):**
  1. `--accent #5B8DEF` used as TEXT (eyebrows 13px, inline links) = **3.23:1 white / 3.0:1 on --bg-tint** → FAILS 4.5. Fix: add `--accent-text:#2F6BD8` (4.98:1) for eyebrow labels + links; keep #5B8DEF for decorative/large/borders only.
  2. `--ink-muted #6B7A93` (footer-legal 12px, shot-note/price-note 14px, trustline) = **4.35 white / 4.04 tint / 3.85 tint2** → FAILS. Fix: darken to `#566579` (5.5:1 on tint).
  - Passing/good: --ink-body #3A4A63 = 8.98:1; CTA white-on-#2F6BD8 = 4.98:1.
- **Other findings:** (3) `--warm #E9B872` gold underline is off the "white + soft light-blue" brief and appears exactly once — recommend switch to blue-200 tint or explicit brand keep. (4) off-grid margins: hero-h1 18px / hero-lead 22px / icon-well 18px break the 4/8 scale → 16/24/16. (5) mobile nav (<760px) drops all section links, no replacement — OK for one-page scroll, minor wayfinding loss. (6) non-interactive feature/value cards use -4px hover-lift → false click affordance; soften to bg/border-only. (7) premium lever: system-serif (Georgia) at 4.5rem reads generic vs Hilton bar — a self-hosted (no CDN, offline-safe) display serif woff2 is the single biggest premium upgrade; needs Akashi clearance as a bundled local asset (no recurring cost).
- **Verdict: POLISH** (2 real AA blockers + palette-brief mismatch; structure/hierarchy/motion/a11y foundation are strong).
- **Praise:** disciplined token system + 8pt scale + navy-tinted shadow set; strong a11y foundation (skip link, focus-visible, aria-labelledby, reduced-motion global kill, semantic FAQ, aria-hidden decor); excellent body/heading contrast; intentional staggered ease-out reveal w/ progressive enhancement; hero left-aligns on mobile (deliberate, correct); premium whitespace rhythm; editorial serif-display + sans-body pairing.
- **Still open:** routed to Kaito (owner builds; Arthur re-reviews). Brand decision needed on gold underline. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab.

## 2026-07-28 — Landing "billion-dollar" elevation spec (landing.html + legal.html)
- **Asked (Osefe direct):** push landing.html from clean/premium → expensive, textured, ALIVE. Full redline spec Kaito implements. Constraints: WHITE+soft-light-blue only, ZERO network (fonts base64 woff2 local), a11y + prefers-reduced-motion safe, no h-scroll, transform/opacity perf.
- **Studied/verified:** re-read current landing.html + legal.html end-to-end (tokens, reveal engine, per-section markup). Confirmed my prior POLISH fixes ARE applied (accent-text #2F6BD8, ink-muted #566579, blue underline replaced gold, no false-lift on cards, 8pt margins). Verified Fraunces = SIL OFL 1.1, self-hostable woff2, axes wght100-900 / opsz9-144 / SOFT0-100 / WONK0-1 (WebSearch google/fonts). Lock board: only index.html locked (Kaito legal layer); landing/legal free; I'm read-only so no lock taken.

True single-weight 400 = honest, no faux-bold; line 241 correctly forces weight:400 on every serif display el that was declared 500 (hero-h1/h2/privacy h2/price-amount/plabel) → CSS audit shows ZERO faux-bold, and screenshots confirm. Separate true Italic woff2 loaded (line 229) = no faux-italic. The ONLY gap vs Fraunces is WARMTH: Instrument leans cool/sophisticated-classical, not soft-humanist-warm. But the LANDING targets 16-40/premium (warmth is the app's job), so cool-premium is on-brief. Net: keep it.
- **mustFix (prioritized):**
  1. **DEAD ITALIC (~37KB shipped for nothing + a lost moment).** The only italic el, `.privacy-quiet` (CSS line 157 font-style:italic), is 100%-wrapped in `<strong>` (body 529) and `.privacy-quiet strong` resets font-style:normal (158) → italic NEVER renders; the bundled Italic base64 (line 229/230, ~37KB) is pure dead weight in the payload. FIX (preferred): unwrap the strong (or don't reset font-style in it) so the closing line renders ITALIC Instrument Serif — this is the editorial "aside" beat the italic face was bundled for, and injects exactly the warmth the cool roman lacks. Keep the draw-on hairline. If instead staying upright: DROP the italic @font-face entirely (save ~37KB). Screenshots confirm it currently renders upright.
     - Related: `.privacy-quiet strong{font-weight:500}` (158) is a no-op on a 400-only face (500→400, no synthesis). Harmless, resolves with the above.
  2. **Grain is imperceptible → arguably a no-op.** op:.03 + soft-light on a near-white bg registers ~nothing (invisible in 2× screenshots). Either bump to ~.045 so texture actually earns its z-9999 compositing layer (soft-light at 4.5% on light bg barely shifts luminance → text stays AA), or drop the layer. Currently it's a cost with no visible payoff.
  3. **Hero cascade is metronomic, not art-directed.** delays 40/130/240/350/450ms = even ~100ms metronome (heroRise). Spec wanted art-direction: bind eyebrow+h1 as one beat so the headline is the star. Suggest 0 / 60 / 210 / 330 / 440 (eyebrow+h1 near-together, then lead, cta, trustline). heroMediaIn @220ms cross-cutting the copy already reads well — keep.
  4. **Dead --warm token** (#E9B872, line 24) now unused (gold→blue underline done). Remove.
  5. **Minor/optional:** hero h1 em-dash orphans to line-start ("— on your phone,") on desktop+mobile via text-wrap:balance. Editorial-acceptable; if it bugs, bind "health&nbsp;—" or restructure.
- **Confirmed GOOD (no regression):** reduced-motion architecture correct — all opacity:0 animation seeds live INSIDE @media(no-preference) (286/293/296/301) so reduced-motion → visible (matches Kaito's Playwright: hero opacity 1). Card hover = light-not-lift verified in screenshot (bg wash + blue-200 border + top hairline sweep + icon-well scale 1.04, ZERO translateY). Frosted pricing reads milled (gradient + radial top-glow + upgraded sh-lg). Device frames read glass (145deg metal bezel + inset highlights + inset hairline on browser-screen). Privacy draw-on hairline lands. Header condense 64→56 present. Front-door lit rule, final-CTA glow, footer top-light, pricing shimmer, CTA sheen, device float w/ IO play-state gate — all present. legal.html parity clean (Instrument Serif h1+doc-title @400, grain, toc/lead-note hairline, calm fade-only reveal, reduced-motion safe; loads ONLY Regular = no dead italic there). text-wrap:balance on headings + pretty on body. Zero external requests (only local ov_*.png + app hrefs). No h-scroll desktop/mobile.

Instrument Serif at scale is a genuine premium signal; the single-true-weight discipline (no faux anything) is exactly right. - **Asked (Osefe direct):** landing.html reads too white/cool/sterile; he wants Hilton-warm (cream/ivory base, warm accents, life+color) WITHOUT losing premium/trust/clarity. My earlier "strip the gold as off-brief" call is REVERSED — warmth on-brief now.

Ran WCAG math (python) on the full warm palette before proposing — every value AA-verified.

WARM ACCENTS (2, disciplined) → --warm-text terracotta #A8481E (eyebrows; AA 5.49/5.09/4.78 on the 3 bases), --gold #E9B872 revived (underline/price-accent/privacy hairline/atmosphere), peach/cream glow radials #F7E4CF/#FBF1E4 (atmosphere only, never text). BLUE STAYS = trust thread: CTA blue unchanged (white-on-blue 4.98), but global link color → --cta-hover #255BBE so links pass AA on warm tints (5.97/5.54/5.20; note #2F6BD8 drops to 4.36/4.10 on the warm tints = FAIL, hence the swap).

So push base+atmosphere HARD, keep hard UI palette to exactly 2 accents, never warm the CTA/checkmarks/device-screen. Dark app = intentional cool contrast, warm ivory FRAMES it like a gallery wall + warm shadow sits it in warm light; the amber inside the HUD now echoes the gold outside.

## [2026-07-29] — COMFORT-PRESET LAYOUTS spec (data-layout root attr, pure-CSS-over-existing-DOM) — direct task
- **Asked:** Osefe wants theming to change LAYOUT not just color. Deliver 2-3 preset specs (real selectors), feasibility flags (pure-CSS vs needs-DOM/JS), and tell Kaito how to render the locked dashboard for mockups. Director-only, no edit.
- **Structure read (grounded, cited @ e40a635):** Token layer CLEAN post-PR1 — `:root` @43-64 (11 color tokens + --accent-rgb/--pos-rgb/--glow-strength etc.), `[data-theme=calm]`@66 / `[data-theme=pink]`@73 override tokens; Cyberpunk = untouched :root. **`data-layout`/`data-density` NOT yet used → clean root namespace.** LAYOUT LEVERS (all real): `.wrap`@200 (max-1100, pad 26/16/80); `header.hud`@203 block; `.hero`@254 + `.hero-num`@256 clamp(38,9vw,60); `.status`@225 grid repeat(3,1fr) + `.stat`@226 + `header.hud .stat .val`@263 clamp(15,2.6vw,19) [the H#1 demote]; `.pillbar`@220 (AGE/WORK/GRADE/STREAK pills, markup 1455-1460); `nav.tabs`@266 wrapped-centered-flex → phone @736 = nowrap scroll-strip, `.tab`@267 + `.tab .ix`@273 (01/02 mono index prefix); `.panel`@332/`.card`@341 + `.card h2::before`@347 diamond; `.row2`@360 2-col→1col@720; Budget: `.subtotal`@443 2-col + `#customList`@386/`.item-row`@392/`.ir-name`@395(16px)/`.ir-amt`@400(15px) + two-state `#expenses.budget-editing`@385 toggle + `.bud-bars`@371; `.exp-group/.exp-head/.exp-body`@462-471 accordion + `.exp-head .ico`@466 diamond; `.field`@508/`.form-row`@516 2-col→1col@560; `.localebar`@707. DECORATION to strip in Simple: `body::before`@107 grid, `body::after`@125 ambient, `html::before`@127 scanline, `header.hud::after`@211 sweep, `.card h2::before`@347 + `.exp-head .ico` diamonds, `hudGlowPulse` on `.hero-num`@137, `.tab .ix` indices. Existing breakpoints: 900(pad), 720(row2/status→1col), 620(phone nav-strip), 560(form→1col). Fonts: Rajdhani body / Orbitron headings+numbers / Share Tech Mono labels.
- **Found / verdict: SHIP the mechanism — it's a genuine pure-CSS-over-existing-DOM win. Designed 3 presets on ONE axis (density+chrome): `data-layout="simple|standard|pro"`.** PRO = today EXACTLY (the no-override baseline = zero-regression); STANDARD = same multi-col structure, ~1.1× spacing, HALF the ambient, diamonds kept/glow reduced; SIMPLE = 1-col stack, ~1.25× type, zero HUD chrome, calm. **Pairs:** Simple→Calm (low glow), Pro→Cyberpunk (neon), Standard→either. Both are root attrs → compose `html[data-layout=simple][data-theme=calm]`.
- **PURE CSS (all keyed on `html[data-layout=…]`, additive scoped blocks, ZERO base-CSS edits):** type scale (font-size/weight/letter-spacing overrides); grid→stack (grid-template-columns:1fr on .status/.row2/.subtotal/.form-row); card/row padding+margin+radius+gap; hide decoration (display:none/content:none/animation:none on the pseudos + .ix + pillbar + diamonds); reduce glow (override --glow-strength token IN the preset block); force nav nowrap-scroll-strip at all widths + bigger .tab padding; hide specific tabs by `[data-p]` display:none; reorder WITHIN a flex/grid container via `order` (incl. making header.hud `display:flex;flex-direction:column` in the preset to reorder eyebrow/h1/pillbar/hero/status); nav→fixed-bottom-bar (position:fixed;bottom:0 + .wrap padding-bottom).
- **NEEDS DOM/JS (flagged → Kaito, the ONLY non-CSS items):** (1) **active-tab-on-hidden-tab guard** — if Simple `display:none`s tabs and the user is ON one, they'd see an orphan panel; 1-line JS: on layout change, if active tab hidden → click Overview. (2) **persist+apply** `data-layout` from `STATE.prefs.layout` on boot + the picker control (same wiring pattern as data-theme; STATE.prefs @2551, picker home `.localebar`@707) — JS wiring, not a layout change. OUT OF SCOPE (would break the shared-DOM constraint, do NOT do): cross-container node moves (card A→B), table→block-row reflow (fragile, loses .num align), tab-count reduction via overflow menu (needs DOM). All 3 presets deliberately avoid these.
- **DASHBOARD RENDER FOR KAITO (mockups):** lock needs an ARMED __sys session for REAL numbers — forcing `#lockScreen.classList.add('unlocked')` ALONE shows NaN (I verified headless: `__sys.token()`→null under automation; arm only via a valid P-256 signed key path @5924/5953). So: (a) enter a valid monthly key (owner has one / mint via owner "New file + key" `#shareBlankBtn`) → verify()→`__sys.arm()`+unlock()→real figures; (b) `loadDemoData()` @6525 (loads `window.__DEMO_MODEL` = the 'Alex' sample used for landing ov_desktop/ov_mobile) OR onboarding "👀 Just show me a filled example" `#wDemo`@7974; (c) **preview a preset with `document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-layout','simple')` — because presets are pure CSS on the root, ALL THREE screenshot from ONE armed session by swapping the attr. No rebuild per preset.**
- **Recommendation:** ship order = wire `data-layout` root + PRO(=today, empty override = the no-op baseline) + SIMPLE(the roomy end) FIRST → screenshot Simple-vs-Pro (the extremes = max visible gap, proves the mechanism) → Osefe validates; THEN add STANDARD as the tuned middle + set as the NEW-USER DEFAULT (per his "default=toned-down" steer). REGRESSION GUARD: every preset is an attribute-scoped ADDITIVE block; Pro = absence of override → a differential screenshot of no-attr vs today MUST be pixel-identical (the whole controlled-risk property depends on NOT touching base CSS).
- **@Akashi:** pure presentation — no money math, no __sys, no watchdog, no network/asset (CSS + the 1-line tab guard + boot setAttribute reading STATE.prefs.layout). Same class as the theme wiring. Confirm SAFE-trivial. **@Mikoto:** 3 preset labels ("Simple/Standard/Pro" — recommend those exact words over "Essential" etc. for the 9-yo/older-user clarity bar) + "Layout"/"Comfort" picker label. Length note: Simple's BIGGER type + single-column is MORE forgiving of DE/HU expansion than Pro (more width/element), but re-check enlarged nav tab labels + hero "YOU KEEP · TYPICAL" at 320px.
- **Still open / next:** (1) @Kaito builds the 3 scoped CSS blocks + the 2 JS touches (tab-guard + prefs wiring/picker) — owner, find-xor-fix; I re-review live per preset. (2) Screenshot Simple-vs-Pro from one armed session (method above) for Osefe. (3) Composes with PR2a themes — verify Simple×Calm + Pro×Cyberpunk combos. Read-only, no lock. Need NEW tokens: `--accent-2` (deeper accent for gradient stop) + `--on-accent` (text on accent fill: =--bg on dark themes, #FFFFFF on light) ; total→`var(--bright)`. Without these the active tab/button/total is unreadable on light themes (dark text on dark-blue fill) and wrong-hue on dark themes (orchid/brass gradient ending in cyan). Gave per-theme --accent-2/--on-accent values.

## 2026-08-03 — LANDING identity-first revamp review (landing.html @ 1aeba2e, via Kaito, pipeline step 2)
- **Asked:** review Kaito's rebuilt landing.html (identity hero → philosophy/scatter → privacy → 5 life-area cards → effort → how-it-works → NEW storage explainer → pricing → 12-FAQ → CTA). Prioritized redline (MUST vs POLISH), each concrete. Focus: hero 5-sec test, both diagrams legible, 5-area rhythm incl wide Assistant, mobile stacking, too-warm/unclear copy. Enforce serious-not-warm + no-hype-emoji mandate.
- **Method (measured, not eyeballed):** read full body markup + new CSS (.scatter/.area-/.store-). Rendered section crops desktop@1280 2x + mobile@390 2x (arthur_crops.js). Ran WCAG python on the warm palette + geometry via playwright boundingBox (geo.js). Files: scratchpad/ar_d_*.png, ar_m_*.png.
- **VERDICT: POLISH** — 2 contained MUST-FIX layout bugs; structure/hierarchy/type/palette/a11y/motion foundation strong + on-brand. Not REWORK.
- **MUST-FIX (2):**
  1. **Five-area grid orphan.** area-grid = `repeat(auto-fit,minmax(280px,1fr))` → 3 cols. Cards: Money/Health/Planning (r1), **Personal alone in r2c1 with cols 2-3 EMPTY (measured ~712px×256 void)**, then Assistant `grid-column:1/-1` dumped full-width r3. Reads like a load failure. FIX: line 376 `.area-card-wide{grid-column:1/-1}` → `{grid-column:span 2}` so Assistant sits beside Personal (clean 3+2). Keep line 377 `@media(max-width:760px){.area-card-wide{grid-column:auto}}`. Verified auto-fit never yields 4 cols (4×280+3×24=1192>1160 maxw), so span-2 safe at 3-col AND 2-col stages.
  2. **Mobile storage diagram broken.** line 381 `@media(max-width:760px){... .store-move .store-arrows{transform:rotate(90deg)}}` rotates the MULTI-glyph span "📤 → 📥" as a unit → emoji collide with box edge + occlude the "You export, you import" label ("You expor[📥]you import"). (Single-glyph scatter-arrow rotate is fine — that's why the scatter ↓ works but this doesn't.) FIX: drop the rotate; render `📤 → 📥` horizontally as a divider band between the two stacked boxes + `.store-move{margin:6px 0}`. Optional vertical semantic = stack `📤 / ↓ / 📥` as separate lines (NOT a rotated span).
- **POLISH (high→low):** (a) **Hero triple-redundancy** — eyebrow "Private·Offline·One app" + lead ends "No account. No cloud. No bank connection." + trustline "No account·No cloud·No bank connection·Works fully offline" = same triad 3× in one viewport (glaring on mobile). Drop the 3 terminal sentences from lead (431); chips carry it. (b) **Scatter "after" card floats** — cols EQUAL width (495/495, measured — eye fooled), but after-card h=202 vs before h=392, `align-items:center` floats it in a void. FIX line 351 `.scatter{align-items:stretch}` + center `.scatter-one` content (justify-content:center) so the blue "one app" payoff becomes a full-height block. (c) **store-note (659/389)** — critical data-loss warning buried in a 6-idea run-on, ALL terracotta #A8481E on peach-glow = **4.7:1 (passes 4.5 but marginal for a full paragraph)**. Split into lead action + own emphasized warning line; set body to --ink-body, keep only bold lead + warning in --warm-text. (d) **Effort↔How dedup** — "less time than opening a banking app" appears in effort lead (585) AND how lead (596) back-to-back; keep in one. (e) howto "💸 Budget" (money-with-wings=loss) vs area "💰 Money" — align icon. (f) privacy .plabel (158): tile4 "No bank permissions" 1 line vs others 2 → bodies misalign; min-height reserve 2 lines (ties to DE/HU expansion). (g) hero image = finance-only "FINANCE HUD" slightly skews the "all of you" thesis (tab bar rescues breadth) — consideration only.
- **Confirmed GOOD (no change):** hero 5-sec test PASSES (eyebrow+lead+HUD resolve "one private offline app for money/health/plans/notes"). Privacy section strong (4 equal tiles, serif labels, airplane-mode proof line w/ gold underline draw). Mobile: all 5 area cards + scatter stack clean. NO hard contrast failures (palette work from July holds: ink-body 7.4-7.9 on tints, accent-text 4.54 on blue-50, ink-muted 5.4-5.6). Serious-not-warm mandate CLEAN — zero hype/celebration emoji; all icons functional/allowed (💰🏃📅📝🤖 area, 💸🏋️🍽️📅📔🎬 howto sections, 🌐📱 storage, 📤📥 transfer); copy factual throughout.
- **Routed to Kaito** (owner builds; I re-review the 2 must-fixes live). Copy items (a,c,d) coordinate w/ @Mikoto (7-lang length). @Akashi: pure CSS/copy, no network/asset touched. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 1aeba2e.

## 2026-08-03 — v40 BATCH UI/UX REVIEW (index.html @ c670168, via Kaito, pipeline step 2)
- **Asked:** review the RENDERED v40 result — (1) savebar collapsed to the 💾 fab on ALL sizes, (2) Meal Ideas v2 (155-meal DB, ★ liked chips, off-goal chip, no-match line), (3) new user-owned Checklist, (4) theme sweep across cyber/landing/purple/+. MUST-FIX vs POLISH, each concrete. Read-only director.
- **Method (measured, not eyeballed):** Chromium 1194 via Playwright, `file://index.html`, lock bypassed + `__sys.arm()` + `loadDemoData()`. Viewports 390 / 820 / 1024 / 1280 / 1440. Scripts + 40 shots in scratchpad (`s1..s16.js`, `shots/`). Geometry via boundingBox, colours via getComputedStyle, WCAG via python compositing (`wc.py`). Probed the meal matcher in-page (`expandTerm`/`termHitsMeal`/`fuzzyCorrect`).
- **VERDICT: REWORK** (5 MUST-FIX, two of which are P0 ship-blockers). The three features are structurally right and the theme sweep is genuinely well done — but the batch shipped a broken onboarding tour, an unstyled browser-default control in the middle of the HUD, and a matcher that lies in a chip built to earn trust.

**MUST-FIX (P0, blocks ship):**
1. **Guided tour steps 8 + 9 point at nothing.** `#chatToggle` and `#saveBtn` are now `display:none` while the savebar is collapsed, but TOUR STEPS (8802-8813) still target them. Measured: step 8 ring = 57.5×30 @ (-8, 164) over the header; step 9 ring = **16×16 @ (-8,-8)** — off-screen. Tip text still reads "Assistant MRLN…" / "Save / Export…". Every new user hits this. FIX: TOUR must open the savebar before those two steps (`.savebar.classList.add('open')` in `place()` when `s.sel` is inside `.savebar`) and close it after — or retarget both steps to `#saveFab`.
2. **Checklist ✕ delete is an unstyled browser-default button.** `.iconbtn` is only ever styled as `.localebar .iconbtn` (1185). `class="iconbtn ck-del"` (3906) sits outside `.localebar` → white/#efefef fill, black glyph, default border, no focus ring, no tapFeedback. Three white blocks are the loudest thing on the Checklist tab; the destructive action out-shouts the task text. Same defect on `.iconbtn sb-edit` / `.iconbtn sb-del` in Savings (pre-existing — one fix closes both). FIX: promote the rule to a bare `.iconbtn{…}` (current localebar body) and keep `.localebar .iconbtn` only for the size override; add `.iconbtn` to the press list (436) and the focus-visible list (456).
3. **★ match chip names a term that isn't in the meal.** `fuzzyCorrect('belly') → 'bell'` (Levenshtein 1, thr=2 for len>4), so "pork belly" matches **every meal with bell peppers** and the chip prints "★ PORK BELLY" on an *Egg-white veggie omelette*. Verified in-page: `expandTerm('pork belly')` yields `bell`; hay = "…bell peppers onion spinach…". The chip exists to prove *why* a meal was pinned — a false chip is worse than no chip. FIX (3 parts): (a) only fuzzy-correct when the token is the WHOLE term (never a sub-token of a multi-word phrase); (b) tighten `thr` to `len<=7?1:2`; (c) render the chip with the **matched vocabulary word**, not the user's raw string.
4. **The expanded savebar has no surface — page content bleeds through it.** `.savebar.open` is 6 loose pills with 8px transparent gaps over live content. At 1024 the Assistant button sits on top of the `14 CONNECT` tab and Mission-Briefing body text reads *between* Backup and Lock. Also 6 different left edges (measured @1280: 1061.1 / 1096.2 / 1100.5 / 1102.7 / 1110.8 / 1120 = 59px of rag). FIX: `.savebar.open::before{content:'';position:absolute;inset:-14px -14px 64px -14px;z-index:-1;border-radius:16px;border:1px solid var(--line);background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--panel2),var(--panel));box-shadow:0 18px 48px rgba(0,0,0,.55);backdrop-filter:blur(8px)}` + make `align-items:stretch` + `width:min(280px,calc(100vw - 22px))` apply at ALL widths (currently ≤620 only) so the stack is one left edge. Add a tap-outside + Escape close (neither works today — verified).
5. **Chip text is 8px.** Meal chips override `.tag`'s 10px token with an inline `font-size:8px` (5282-5284) — Share Tech Mono, uppercase, .1em tracking. Below any legibility floor and an off-token magic number. Also **`★ <term>` on Rose (kawaii) = 4.15:1 → FAILS AA** (all 8 other themes pass 4.65-9.38). FIX: drop the inline size (inherit `.tag` 10px); add `--accent-text` (=`--cyan` in `:root`, `#A62E4F` on kawaii → 5.42:1 measured) and point `.tag.now{color:var(--accent-text)}`.

**POLISH (ordered):**
- (a) **Checkbox hit area is 20×20** (`.check li .box`, 577) — the primary action of the whole tab, less than half the 44px floor, and the label text isn't tappable. FIX: `position:relative` + `::before{content:'';position:absolute;inset:-12px}` (→44px), and make the whole `<span>` label toggle too.
- (b) **`.check li` is `align-items:flex-start`** so the 40px ✕ centre sits ~9px below the text/checkbox centre — measured optical misalignment on every row. FIX `align-items:center`.
- (c) **Off-goal chip is 181.8px vs the ★ chip's 51.6px** — the caveat is 3.5× the width of the reason. FIX: shorten to `OFF YOUR GOAL`; the "scaled" fact is already stated in the macro line ("1.25× serving").
- (d) **Two identical stacked `.note` blocks** above the meal rows (rotation hint + header) — the same "two banners before any action" failure I called on the Move-my-data card. Merge to one; lead with the one useful sentence.
- (e) **The rotation hint lies.** With 23-35 matches and PAGE=5, `liked` rotates through 5-at-a-time, yet the line says "Your 35 matches stay pinned on top". FIX: `Showing 5 of {n} matches — press Suggest for the next 5.`
- (f) **No-match line concatenates into a 38-word run-on** ("No meal matches "durian" — showing the closest picks for your goal." + the full header). FIX: when there's no match, print the no-match line ALONE.
- (g) **Nothing animates on open/close** — `display:none↔flex` can't transition, so 6 controls teleport in. FIX: `visibility+opacity+translateY(6px)` 180ms `var(--ease-out)`, `transition-delay:calc(var(--i)*24ms)` (cap 6), reverse 120ms no-stagger.
- (h) `.chattoggle` in the open stack is **40px tall** (< 44 touch floor) → `min-height:44px`.
- (i) **B-tier badge is mud on 4 themes.** `tierRGB` B = straight 50/50 `_rgbMix(pos,acc)` (4506-4510) → purple rgb(158,187,178) sage-grey, magenta rgb(164,158,155) near-neutral grey, kawaii rgb(103,85,83) mud-brown, warmdark rgb(162,185,139) olive. B is the most-shown grade. FIX: bias the mix (B = pos 70% / accent 30%; D = warn 60% / bad 40%) so B stays green-family and D stays orange-red-family.
- (j) `--panel2:#23302F` on **Umber is green-cast** in a copper theme → cards read olive. **My own palette miss from 2026-07-29.** FIX `--panel2:#2A2320`.
- (k) `.check li.done span` (580) also matches `.box` → the checked box computes `line-through` + `--txt-dim`. Doesn't paint visibly in Chromium but is wrong. FIX `.check li.done > span:not(.box)`.
- (l) The `.haswarn` badge is **amber** while the fab goes **red** — two alert colours for one alert. Pick red for both.
- **VOICE:** clean. No hype/celebration emoji; ★ / ✅ / ⚠ / 💾 are functional. Copy is factual. Only register wobble is (e) — an over-claim, not warmth.

**CONFIRMED GOOD (measured, no change):** theme sweep is the strongest part of the batch — Klarna verdict box (border/bg/tag all `rgb(v.rgb)`, verified across cyber/landing/purple/warmdark), Savings "What it becomes" rows + meters, anatomy SVG (copper on Umber, verified), Stats letters, meal chips, Life Tier badge — every one follows its theme; `applyAppearance` re-render on switch works. Fail-loud contract survives the collapse (`.haswarn` keeps the badge visible + reddens the fab — verified live). TEMPLATE_MODE starts expanded (6166). Off-goal pool gate correct (off-goal rows only when a like earns it). Checklist empty state is clear and factual. Zero console/page errors on boot in every run.

**THE ASSISTANT-BEHIND-THE-FAB QUESTION (Osefe's call — my honest read: DON'T):** Save already has a second front door (`#openSaveBtn` "↗ Open / Save" in the localebar). The Assistant now has **zero** visible entry point on any screen, behind an icon that says *save*. It's the product's headline differentiator ("free · offline assistant" is in the footer tagline and the marketing). RECOMMENDATION: keep the fab for save/backup/lock, and give the Assistant its own persistent 🤖 control (second fab above the 💾, or a header slot). If it must stay behind one fab, change the fab to a HUD/menu glyph (⌘ / ▤) — not 💾, which promises only saving.
- **Routed to Kaito** (owner builds; I re-review the 5 must-fixes live). Copy items (c,e,f) coordinate w/ @Mikoto (7-lang length; "OFF YOUR GOAL" and the rotation line are new/changed strings). @Akashi: pure CSS/JS presentation + the tour targeting; the matcher fix touches no money math and no `__sys` path — but the `.savebar.open` surface must not cover the `.haswarn` badge, worth his glance on the fail-loud contract. Read-only, no lock (Kaito holds index.html). Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ c670168.
