## [2026-08-05] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — DESIGN EXAMINATION: Osefe's full frontend BLUEPRINT (Inter · 8pt · card modules · 15 tabs → 5-item bottom nav)
- **Asked:** the design authority's examination of Osefe's blueprint (transcription @ scratchpad/blueprint-2026-08-05.md + Kaito's conflicts C1–C11 + binding scope addenda S1 reskin-over-existing-backend / S2 no-mistakes / S3 responsive-everywhere). Deliver: verdict, design-system spec mapped onto our 9 themes, my own 15→5 IA map, responsive ruling inputs, the underspecified list, and the 3 things I'd fight Osefe on. Read-only.
- **Studied / MEASURED (not eyeballed) @ tip 7cc6068, working tree (index.html held by Mikoto):**
  - Live Chromium (playwright /opt/node22 + chromium-1194), lock bypassed, 4 viewports. **Phone 390px: nav strip scrollWidth 1574px in a 390px viewport (4.0×), only 4 of 15 tabs fully visible, tab height 33px (below the 44 floor). Phone 320px: 3 of 15. Tablet 820px: nav WRAPS to a 149px-tall block. Desktop 1440px: nav 96px tall, .wrap 1100 centred, 170px dead gutter each side, card width 1068 — desktop is the tablet column, not a desktop layout.**
  - **`html[data-layout=canvas]` ALREADY ships a fixed bottom nav — and it is STILL a horizontal scroll strip: 55px tall, scrollWidth 1434 in 390px, 4 of 15 visible, tab height 36px.** The code comment admits it ("13 tabs won't fit abreast"). Shipped proof that the current IA cannot express itself in a bottom bar.
  - Spacing audit of the component CSS (lines 340–1120): **~167 off-4pt padding/margin/gap/radius declarations vs ~109 on-grid; 27 distinct px values** (10px×43, 9px×22, 14px×22, 6px×17, 11px×13, 13px×12, 5px×10, 18px×10, 22px×6, 7px×5, 26px×4…). The app has no spacing system, it has 27 spacing values. The blueprint's 8pt claim identifies a REAL defect.
  - IA inventory: **16 panels / 45 cards.** overview 1 · income 3 · expenses 3 · loan 1 · flow 2 · rule 4 · checklist 2 · klarna 7 (display:none, 8 KB dead) · **gym 7 (13.7 KB)** · food 3 · calendar 4 · **stats 1 (630 bytes)** · notebook 1 · media 3 · connect 6 (10.7 KB) · log 3. Nav slots are equal weight; content is 20:1.
  - Font payload measured: 10 @font-face, **~134 KB decoded woff2 / 179 KB base64 in a 1.98 MB file. Rajdhani = 108 KB across 8 files (80% of the payload); Orbitron variable 500–900 = 11.5 KB; Share Tech Mono = 13.2 KB.**
  - **NEW BUG, proven at glyph level via CDP `CSS.getPlatformFontsForNode`: Orbitron and Share Tech Mono have NO latin-ext face (unicode-range is latin-only). "Előfizetések" in Orbitron renders 11 glyphs Orbitron + 1 glyph LIBERATION SERIF (the ő). "Ellenőrzőlista" in Share Tech Mono = 12 STM + 2 Liberation Serif. 523 occurrences of ő/ű/Ő/Ű in shipped strings → Hungarian headings/labels carry serif fallback glyphs today.** (I first hypothesised a shadowed-@font-face bug from a bad regex and DISPROVED it myself — Rajdhani's latin-ext ranges are correctly declared and scoped. Recording the false start so I don't re-raise it.)
  - Dependency measurement for C2: **7 of 11 tour steps target `#tabs .tab[data-p=…]`**; `__tabReorder`/`applyTabOrder` (double-tap reorder, guarded by reorder_test.js) is live; the Smart-Onboarding priority picker writes STATE.tabOrder ("your first pick becomes tab 1"). A fixed 5-item nav breaks all three.
  - Component gaps: **`stroke-dashoffset` count = 0 → no ring gauge exists**; 4 `<svg>`, 1 polyline, 0 sparkline; `font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums` used exactly **2×** in the whole app. incomeLog HAS shipped (14 refs + an Income-history card), so an income sparkline is data-backed.
  - Fixed-layer collision: `.savebar{position:fixed;right:18px;bottom:18px}` (11px phone) + `.chattoggle`; only prior art is the canvas-layout offset `bottom:calc(66px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))`. `env(safe-area-inset-*)` used only 4× in the whole file.
  - Craft refs (named, current): Material 3 window size classes (compact <600dp bar / medium 600–839 rail / expanded ≥840 drawer); Apple HIG tab bars (3–5 max on iPhone, sidebar on iPad); mobile-nav research on buried "More" tabs (79% abandon over confusing navigation); Inter variable latin subset sizing (~40–60 KB subset vs ~100 KB full latin).
- **Found / VERDICT: SHIP-CONCEPT on the skeleton · REJECT-PARTS on the skin.** The blueprint is a better product than what ships — but only built as a system. Split verdict, evidence per part: **IA 15→5 = SHIP-CONCEPT** (the current nav measurably fails on phone, tablet AND desktop; this is not taste). **8pt spacing = SHIP-CONCEPT** (167 off-grid declarations). **Card-module layout = SHIP-CONCEPT.** **Typography as drawn (Inter as the ONLY face) = REJECT-PARTS** — it flattens 9 themes into one skin and deletes the brand; adopt Inter for UI + keep a theme-scoped display face. **Copy/voice (C1) = REJECT** (Osefe's call, but it is a design failure, not just a rule breach). **Desktop as drawn = REJECT** (the poster is marketing; the blueprint has no desktop or tablet spec at all).
- **Corrections to Kaito's conflict list (evidence, not opinion):** (1) **C7 is backwards on cost** — Inter is a size SAVING, not a cost: one variable latin+latin-ext Inter subset (~40–60 KB) replacing Rajdhani's 8 static files (108 KB) is **−50 to −70 KB net**. The real cost of Inter is identity, not bytes. (2) **C7 gains a reason to happen anyway** — the Hungarian ő/ű serif-fallback bug above must be fixed regardless of the blueprint. (3) **C2 understates the blast radius** — add tab-reorder + the onboarding priority picker to the tour/screenshot list; those are shipped features the 5-item nav deletes, not just wiring.
- **Specs given (full detail returned to Kaito + posted for the council):**
  - **Design system:** space tokens `--s-1…--s-16` (4/8/12/16/20/24/32/40/48/64, blueprint's scale adopted verbatim); radius `--r-sm 8 / --r-md 12 / --r-lg 16 / --r-xl 22 / --r-full`; **elevation `--e-1/2/3` must be THEME-AWARE** (dark = border+glow, light = real shadow — 6 of 9 themes are dark and a shadow is invisible on them; the light-theme card-shadow rule @136 is the seed); type scale micro 10 / cap 12 / body 15 / lead 17 / h3 18 / h2 22 / h1 28 + num-s 20 / num-m 28 / num-l clamp(36px,9vw,56px); reuse the shipped `--ease-out cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)` / `--ease-hud cubic-bezier(.25,0,0,1)`.
  - **DO NOT adopt the blueprint's 7-swatch palette** (it is single-theme and duplicates itself — "secondary green" == "success green"); adopt its *discipline* (name roles). Our 20 theme-scoped tokens are already the better structure.
  - **Typography ruling (C7): TWO faces.** `--font-ui` = Inter variable (latin + **latin-ext**, 400–700) for body/labels/rows/buttons/inputs/nav — retire Rajdhani entirely. `--font-display` = **theme-scoped: Cyber keeps ORBITRON (it is that theme's identity); the other 8 themes get Inter tight-tracked.** Keep Share Tech Mono (13 KB) as the system/micro-label voice + **add its latin-ext subset**. **Every money figure gets `font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums`** — currently used 2× in the whole app; the cheapest premium-finance upgrade available and the blueprint never mentions it.
  - **Ring gauge:** 270° GAUGE not a 360° ring (a full ring reads "loading spinner"; a gauge reads "meter"); sizes 132 / 88 / 44; viewBox 0 0 100 100, r=45, C=283, track `--line`, stroke-linecap round, start −225°; draw-on `stroke-dashoffset` 283→283(1−p) **700ms var(--ease-out)** one-shot, reduced-motion lands at final; **no text-shadow** (must not out-glow the hero); `role="img"` + aria-label; the letter grade is the redundant coding so it is never colour-only. **Centre of the ring = the MONEY, not the score** (see fight #1).
  - **Stat card:** min-height 88, padding `--s-4`, label 10px/.16em uppercase `--txt-dim`, value num-m tabular semantic; grid `repeat(auto-fit,minmax(150px,1fr))` — NOT today's `repeat(3,1fr)`, which is what creates the 720px stack cliff.
  - **Chip: split the class in two.** `.tag` = display-only, 10–11px, 24px tall. `.chip` = interactive, **≥44px**, 13px. Merging them is what produced the 8px meal chips I flagged at c670168.
  - **Bottom nav:** 56px + `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`, 5 cells, each ≥48×56, 24px icon + 11px label, **labels ALWAYS visible** (icon-only fails the 9-yo bar and every non-English label test), active = accent + filled icon, inactive = `--txt-dim` + outline. Needs **10 inline-SVG icons (outline+filled) that tint with `currentColor`** — emoji cannot take a theme colour and render differently per platform; this is unspecced work the blueprint hides.
  - **MY IA MAP (5 sections):** **HOME** = the leftover hero (moved out of header chrome into the panel) + Life-Grade gauge wrapping it + Money/Health chips + Morning Briefing (3 lines) + streak. **Stats & Grades moves to Home** (1 card / 630 bytes never deserved a top-level slot) and **"Your Life Tier" moves out of Checklist to Home.** No sparkline on Home — Home is a STATE screen, not a data screen. **MONEY** = income(+Tax) · expenses/Budget · loan · flow · rule/Savings (+klarna if ever un-hidden). Do NOT re-split Subscriptions out of Budget — that merge was correct. **HEALTH** = gym's 7 cards + food's 3, unbundled into modules; this is the cleanest 1:1 in the blueprint. **LIFE** = media · calendar · notebook · checklist. **MORE** = connect · Change Log · klarna · settings(localebar) · keys · legal · install.
  - **THE RULE THAT MAKES 5 SECTIONS SAFE: every section's first screen is a visible DIRECTORY of its modules with icons — never a hamburger, never a dumping ground.** That converts 15 flat destinations (11 off-screen, no map) into 5 + a visible index = 2 taps to anything, with a map at every step. It also rescues the weak label: "Life" is unpredictable to a 9-yo *as a word*, and acceptable only if the screen behind it shows its contents. Same for "More". **Nothing a user needs WEEKLY may live in More** (Connect=once, Change Log=rare, Klarna=hidden, Settings=rare — all pass).
  - **Migration (the paying-customer answer):** re-home, don't delete. `__tabReorder` gets re-scoped to reorder MODULES INSIDE a section; the onboarding priority picker chooses which module pins to the top of its section + which section opens first. Plus a one-time, dismissible "we reorganised — here's where your things are" map. The 7 tour steps get retargeted in the same commit as the nav, never after.
  - **Responsive ruling (S3):** **<600px = bottom bar** (5 items). **600–1023px = left navigation RAIL, 88px**, content `margin-left:88px` — this also kills today's 149px tablet nav wall. **≥1024px = persistent sidebar 240–280px listing the 5 sections WITH their modules nested** — desktop gets the flat map back, which is the correct asymmetry (desktop has the room, phone doesn't); `.wrap` max-width 1100→1200–1280 and the measured 170px dead gutters become sidebar + real margin. **≥1600px:** cap measure at ~72ch and spend the extra width on a 2-column card grid, never on wider cards. **Fab collision:** offset the savebar/chat stack by `calc(var(--navbar-h) + var(--s-2) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))` on phone and move it INTO the rail/sidebar ≥600px; the canvas-layout offset is the existing prior art. The Assistant's missing entry point (open since v40) gets fixed here or not at all.
  - **Must be prototyped before anyone says "no mistakes" (S2):** (1) the 5 nav labels in all 8 languages at **320px** (64px per cell; **never ellipsis a nav label** — if German doesn't fit, the label is wrong; Mikoto picks the words BEFORE the design locks). (2) **landscape phone** (844×390) — a 56px bar eats 15% of the height; rail or auto-hide, decide. (3) **the Android keyboard-open state** with a fixed bottom nav over the app's many text fields — the single most common bottom-nav bug, unmentioned in the blueprint. (4) three fixed layers together (nav + savebar + chat widget). (5) the section-directory screen (a NEW screen type) at every width. (6) **the parity harness must be assertions, not eyeballs**: 6 widths × 9 themes × 8 languages = 432 shots/stage is not human-reviewable, so gate on: zero horizontal overflow, every interactive element ≥44px, every text node ≥4.5:1, same STATE mutations pre/post.
  - **UNDERSPECIFIED (→ my redline backlog):** interaction states (hover/focus-visible/pressed/disabled/loading/error/selected — the shipped Tier-1/2/3a system would be silently dropped); empty states for all 15 modules; **the 3 LIGHT themes** (the blueprint is one dark look; light is where our contrast bugs lived); motion re-map (count-up, ripple+haptic, panel assemble, meter draw, valPulse, cold-boot, cursor-wake, lock-alive) — **specifically the section transition, since the panel slide is keyed to a linear tab index that no longer exists**; reduced-motion behaviour of the ring; long-number behaviour (HUF has no minor unit — prototype the hero at 11 digits); section overflow when a section has 10 cards; where a fail-loud ALERT badge lives on a bottom nav; the 10 nav icons; the Assistant's home; header collapse / nav-hide-on-scroll; **what happens to the 5 `data-layout` archetypes** (5 layouts × new nav is the real combinatorial cost and nobody has costed it); keyboard focus order through a hierarchical nav; 44px floor on the blueprint's chips/checkbox/switch.
- **The 3 things I would fight Osefe on if told to build it exactly as drawn:** (1) **the grade ring as Home's hero** — the most important number in a money app is what you have left, not a composite score; 9 numbers on one screen with a composite winning is a scoreboard, not an instrument, and "91/100" over a −$300 month is the app lying with a smile. Counter-offer keeps his visual: money in the centre, grade as the ring around it — one focal object, two data layers. (2) **the copy (C1)** — serious-register equivalents supplied like-for-like ("Good morning. Here's today." / "S-Tier · Money 78 · Health 88." / "A · 86/100 — above your 90-day average." / "12 days" / delete "Let's make it a great day!"). His call to amend the rule, but he should make it knowing the warm copy is the single element most damaging to the paid-serious-private wedge. (3) **"simply look different" as a one-shot rebuild** — it is an IA change wearing a reskin's clothes; "no mistakes allowed" and "one big redesign" are mutually exclusive. Ship 5 independently revertible stages: (1) 8pt + type-scale tokens (no visual intent change) → (2) typography swap + the Hungarian latin-ext fix → (3) **responsive nav bar/rail/sidebar with the SAME 15 destinations** → (4) the 5-section regroup + directories + migration → (5) new components + Home. **Stage 3 before stage 4 is the load-bearing call:** it separates "does the nav work on every device" from "is the map right", so a failure in one cannot hide in the other.
- **Still open / next:** (1) Kaito reconciles this with the engineering council into the build spec; I re-review each stage LIVE. (2) **@Kaito: the Hungarian ő/ű serif-fallback bug is a MUST-FIX independent of the blueprint** (add latin-ext subsets for Orbitron + Share Tech Mono, or resolve it via the Inter swap). (3) **@Mikoto: the 5 nav labels must be chosen BEFORE the nav design locks, and tested at 320px in DE/HU** — this is the one place where her words constrain my layout, not the reverse. (4) **@Akashi: BUILT IN AARHUS badge + the blueprint's import claims** (already routed) + any bundled Inter subset is a new local asset needing his clearance. (5) Osefe's calls, not ours: C1 voice, the Aarhus city line, and whether hidden Klarna (7 cards / 8 KB dead weight) is carried or deleted. (6) My own open item from c670168 still stands: the Assistant has no visible entry point — the new nav is the chance to fix it. Read-only, no lock (Mikoto holds index.html). Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 7cc6068.

This is the strongest single piece of evidence FOR the mock and it is not taste — it is measured.

Space: card pad 16, card gap 12, side pad 16 phone / 24 tablet+, section-label→card 12 — 8pt throughout. Type: card label **12px/600/.02em** sentence-case grey (NOT the 10px .16em Share Tech Mono HUD micro-cap — that is the cyberpunk voice and it fights the calm register); value **28px/700 tabular-nums**; delta 13px/600; screen section label 13px/600 uppercase .06em; hero value 40px/700. **Card anatomy = 2 columns**: left = label over value (stacked, left-aligned); right = viz block ~96-120px wide, vertically centred; min-height 88px. **Viz grammar (4 primitives, each bound to a data SHAPE — codify so nobody mixes them):** area sparkline = *trend over time* (balance, weight) · progress bar = *bounded goal, has a denominator* (calories 1823/2100) · mini bar chart = *discrete countable events* (4 workouts) · donut ring = *parts of a whole* (macros). Never a sparkline for a bounded goal; never a ring for a trend. Reuse `incomeChartSVG`'s hand-drawn `<rect>` approach for bars; `.meter` for the progress bar; the ring is net-new (0 `stroke-dashoffset` today) — build it as the **270° gauge** I already specced in the blueprint entry, r=45 viewBox 0 0 100 100, C=283, draw-on 700ms `var(--ease-out)`. Nav: 56px + `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`, 4 cells ≥48×56, 24px inline-SVG icon (outline/filled, `currentColor` — emoji cannot take a theme colour), 11px label **always visible**.
  - **(2) THEME / LAYOUT / IDENTITY: all three, and mostly the third.** Colours = a 10th THEME (token swap, cheap). Rounded/whitespace = a LAYOUT (`canvas` is ~60% there). But KPI cards + health-on-Home + a 4-item bar are **neither** — they need a **bottom-tab-group ROUTER** mapping 16 destinations to 4 sections + a settings destination. Recommend it ships as a new default identity ("Calm") with **Cyber preserved as a selectable theme+layout pair**, because deleting it throws away shipped, gated work (Lock-Alive particle field, 3s cold-boot, cursor-wake, Desktop-Alive, 3 interaction tiers) AND desynchronises the app from landing.html, whose hero screenshot is the neon HUD. Osefe should make that call knowing the cost.
  - **(3) MY 16→4 MAP + the fifth destination.** HOME = overview + **stats/grade folded in** (630 bytes never deserved a top-level slot) + the KPI cards. MONEY = income · expenses/Budget · loan · flow · rule/Savings (+klarna if ever unhidden). HEALTH = gym (7 cards) · food (3). **DIARY = calendar · notebook · media · checklist** (better word than my earlier "Life"). That leaves **connect · Change Log · settings/localebar with no home — and the mock's GEAR ICON is the answer.** The gear is load-bearing, not decoration: it is destination #5 and it holds settings + Connect (move-my-data/install/keys) + Change Log + legal. All three are *rare* (once at setup, once per update) so nothing weekly hides there — my own rule holds. **If the 4-bar ships without the gear route, 3 features become unreachable.** Non-negotiable companion rule: **every section's first screen is a visible DIRECTORY of its modules with icons — never a hamburger, never a dumping ground.** Blast radius unchanged from the blueprint entry: 7 tour steps target `#tabs .tab[data-p=…]`, `__tabReorder`/`applyTabOrder` (guarded by reorder_test.js) and the onboarding priority picker's "your first pick becomes tab 1" all die — re-scope reorder to MODULES INSIDE a section, retarget the tour in the SAME commit.
  - **(4) 5 KPI CARDS vs TODAY.** GAINED: health reaches Home (the product's actual "all of you" thesis, which today is a finance app with health bolted on); ~1.5s to read 5 numbers vs ~25s for 603 characters; the duplicate leftover figure dies; 275px of settings+chrome leaves the first screen. LOST, and these are real: (a) **the "why" copy** — "after every bill and any debt payment, before savings" is what makes a stranger trust the number; it must survive as a 12px dim sub-label *inside* the card, not be deleted; (b) **the reality-check bullet** ("even a slow month leaves X") has no card — fold it into the Balance card's secondary line; (c) **the focal point.** Five equal 28px values = no hierarchy = my invariant 2 broken. Revolut puts the balance at ~34-40px ABOVE the card stack; Apple Health is a list of equals only because health has no single most-important number — **money does. COUNTER-OFFER: card 1 is not a card. Make it a hero BLOCK — 40px value, no card chrome, full-bleed, sparkline beneath — with the other four as the 88px card stack below.** One focal point, the mock's visual language intact. (d) **"Balance" is the wrong word and must not ship** — MRLN has no bank connection and no account balance; it has leftover-after-bills in a typical month. Labelling it "Balance $1,250.00" is the app asserting a fact it does not possess. Mikoto owns the replacement word; I own the veto.
  - **(5) WCAG — mandates, with the honest boundary of each.** Card label grey on surface (12px = small text → **4.5:1**, no exceptions): `#8A94A6` = **5.40** on #161F30 PASS; `#7B8698` = **4.48 FAIL** — set the floor at `#8A94A6` and forbid anything dimmer. Cyan value on dark surface: `#00E5FF` **10.73**, `#22D3EE` **9.13** — comfortable. Green delta on dark: `#22C55E` **7.33** PASS. White value on surface **16.71** PASS. Progress-bar FILL vs TRACK conveys state → **1.4.11 genuinely applies, 3:1**: `#22D3EE` on `#243044` = **7.35 PASS**; same 3:1 floor on sparkline/bar/donut strokes vs their own ground. **Card surface vs page bg = 1.12:1 — I am NOT calling this a WCAG failure and nobody else should either.** 1.4.11 covers information needed to identify components and states, not decorative containers; and 1.12 is squarely industry-normal (Apple's dark systemGray6 vs systemBackground is ~1.3). It IS a craft/glare/low-vision concern → add a **1px `rgba(255,255,255,.07)` hairline** and lift surface to `#182234` (1.17). **THE REAL LANDMINE — the mock's palette is DARK-ONLY and cannot survive our 3 LIGHT themes:** `#22D3EE` on `#F5F7FA` = **1.68 FAIL**, `#00E5FF` = **1.43 FAIL**, `#38BDF8` = **2.00 FAIL**; green `#22C55E` = **2.12 FAIL**, `#16A34A` = **3.07 FAIL**. Light-theme accent-as-text must resolve to `#0E7490` (4.99) or darker and green to `#15803D` (4.67) or darker — i.e. this needs the per-theme `--accent-text` token I flagged at c670168 and the `--accent-2`/`--on-accent` pair I flagged on 2026-07-29, and **those must land BEFORE the identity, or Glacier/Rose/Daylight ship an illegible dashboard.** Redundancy: the mock is well-behaved — every colour-coded viz already carries its number in text ("1,823 / 2,100", "−0.3 kg", "4 this week"), so nothing is colour-only; keep that discipline. Focus: nav cells + cards need `:focus-visible`; the shipped lime ring clashes with the calm palette → derive the ring from the accent per theme.
  - **(6) MY v1 SHIP BAR (11 assertions, all machine-checkable — the parity harness must be assertions, not 432 eyeballed screenshots):** zero horizontal overflow at 320/360/390/768/1024/1440 · every nav label fits at **320px in DE and HU with no ellipsis** (Mikoto picks the 4 words BEFORE the design locks) · every text node ≥4.5:1 in every theme it ships in · every tappable ≥44px, nav cells ≥48×56 · **one focal number on Home**, not five equals · every section opens on a visible module directory · the gear destination reaches settings + Connect + Change Log · **fabs never overlap body text at any width (they DO today at 390 and 820 — measured)** · bottom nav + fabs + Android-keyboard-open tested together · `prefers-reduced-motion` respected and sparklines don't re-animate on every render · zero regression: all 16 destinations reachable and the 7 tour steps retargeted in the SAME commit. Staging unchanged from my blueprint ruling: (1) 8pt + type tokens → (2) typography + the Hungarian ő/ű latin-ext fix → (3) responsive nav bar/rail/sidebar with the SAME destinations → (4) the 4-section regroup + directories + migration → (5) new components + Home. **Stage 3 before stage 4 stays load-bearing** — it separates "does the nav work on every device" from "is the map right."
- **What I would fight Osefe on (3):** (1) **five equal cards** — counter-offer above, hero block + 4 cards; (2) **"Balance"** — the app does not have one, and a paid finance product must not claim data it lacks; (3) **deleting the cyberpunk identity outright** rather than demoting it to a selectable theme — the neon face is ~2 months of shipped, gated work and it is the face on landing.html.
- **Routed to Kaito** (owner builds; I re-review each stage LIVE). **@Mikoto:** the 4 nav words + a replacement for "Balance" + "Diary" checked in DE/HU at 320px — this is the one place her words constrain my layout, not the reverse. **@Akashi:** identity is pure presentation, no network/asset/figure — but the 10 nav icons must be inline SVG (no CDN) and any bundled face needs his clearance; also the fab/nav fixed-layer restack touches the `.haswarn` fail-loud contract, worth his glance. **@Kaito (independent of this mock):** the fab-over-body-text overlap at 390/820 is a live defect; the Hungarian ő/ű serif-fallback bug still stands. Read-only, no lock. Branch claude/vibrant-pasteur-ie24ab @ 90b5e13.

### ADDENDUM (same run, after `5d384de` "owner addendum 2 — complete reskin, all screens in scope" landed mid-work)
- **The brief itself asks: "where a mock element has no honest backing data … flagged to Osefe in the ruling, not silently resolved." I measured it. Answer: only 2 of the mock's 5 cards are fully backed by data MRLN actually holds.**
  | Mock card | Backing data, verified in source | Verdict |
  |---|---|---|
  | **Calories 1,823 / 2,100 + progress bar** | `FOODLOG` entries carry `date:todayKey()` + `total.kcal` (8110); `todayEntries()` (8121) sums today; `nutritionTargets()` (5175) supplies the denominator; **`#foodKcalBar` progress meter ALREADY EXISTS** (2279) | **FULLY BACKED — ship as drawn** |
  | **Macros 112/180/68 + donut** | same `todayEntries()` sum over `total.p/c/f`; targets from `nutritionTargets()` | **FULLY BACKED** (donut is a net-new component — 0 `stroke-dashoffset` today — but every number exists) |
  | **Balance $1,250.00 + area sparkline** | There is **no balance in MRLN** — no bank link, no account. `leftOver()` is a computed **scalar with no history**. `STATE.incomeLog[{ym,amt}]` IS a real monthly series but it is *income*, not balance | **VALUE substitutable (rename), SPARKLINE UNBACKED** |
  | **Weight 72.4 kg + green "−0.3 kg" + area chart** | `STATE.body.weight` is a **single scalar**, overwritten in place by the BLE scale (4809), `importWeightCSV` (7379) and the assistant (5835). **grep for any weight-history array = 0 hits.** | **VALUE OK; the DELTA and the CHART have ZERO backing data** |
  | **Workouts "4 this week" + bar chart** | `STATE.workouts = {id, day, title, body}` (4473) where `day` is free text — the input placeholder is literally `"e.g. Monday — Push"` (2240). It is a workout **PLAN**, not a dated session log. No date, no timestamp | **"4 this week" IS NOT COMPUTABLE; chart unbacked** |
- **Consequence the Chairman must rule on: the mock's Home cannot be built as drawn without either (a) inventing numbers — forbidden outright in a paid finance+health product, and it would be the app lying with a smile — or (b) adding a weight-history log and a dated workout-session log, which is FEATURE WORK and directly contradicts the brief's own "improve only the presentation / features untouched."** My recommendation: build the 5-card Home with the cards we can honestly fill (leftover hero · Calories · Macros) plus two honest substitutes (Weight = current value, no delta/chart until a weight log exists; Workouts = "N in your plan" or the streak, not "4 this week"), and put **weight-history + dated-workout-log on the roadmap as the feature that unlocks the mock's full Home.** Do not ship a sparkline with no series behind it.
- **Second confirmation from the addendum:** Osefe's own words — *"combine them into less tabs with sub-categories inside of it. Make it very easy for a consumer to consume."* That makes my section-directory rule the **order**, not my preference: every top-level section opens on a visible list of its sub-categories. Also settles the group-count debate in the mock's favour (4) **provided the gear is the fifth destination** — otherwise connect/log/settings are orphaned.

## [2026-08-05] — via Kaito (asleep dispatch) — LIVE DESIGN REVIEW (4th gate seat): RESKIN STAGE 2 typography @ `7b1edcd` (+ Stage-1 tokens folded) → POLISH
- **Asked:** 4th seat on the Stage-2 gate, the FIRST VISIBLE stage. Judge live at ≥390 and 1440, EN+HU+DE: (1) body legibility vs old Rajdhani + is display-vs-body hierarchy working or mismatched; (2) tabular alignment — actually look at columns of digits; (3) Inter's wider metrics — anything newly wrapping/clipping/crowding, DE/HU worst; (4) Stage-1 `--r-l:16` vs `--r-xl:22` coexistence — bless or redline; (5) the 3 regenerated heroes (ov_desktop .png/.hu/.de) — marketing-grade or not. Verdict SHIP/POLISH with measured citations → Kaito. Sleep-mode: sign, don't publish. Read-only.
- **Method (measured, never eyeballed):** Chromium 1194 via playwright /opt/node22, `file://index.html`, lock bypassed + `__sys.arm()` + `loadDemoData()`. **Ran a DIFFERENTIAL against the Stage-1 tip `1de40b8`** (checked out to scratchpad) so every "new" claim is vs the Rajdhani baseline, not asserted — 320/390/1440 × EN/DE/HU, both builds. Tools: text-advance probes injected INTO the real elements (element-box measuring gave false zeros/false equality — corrected mid-run); **fontTools on the embedded woff2 blobs** pulled out of the base64; **CDP `CSS.getPlatformFontsForNode`** for glyph-level face resolution; python-equivalent WCAG in JS on computed theme values; standalone type specimen (`scratchpad/ar/spec2.png`) for the fallback A/B. Files: `scratchpad/ar/{boot,t1_fonts,t3,t4,t5,diff,diff2,probe,wrapcensus,glyph,alt,vis,shot}.js`, `spec2.png`, `fallback_compare.png`.
- **VERDICT: POLISH @ `7b1edcd`.** The swap is correct and I would not reverse it. Posted under Stage-2 Pending + MESSAGES.
- **Q1 hierarchy — IMPROVED, not mismatched.** Inter vs Rajdhani at 100px: `x` 54.59 vs 41.31 (+32%), `H` 74.33 vs 57.0 (+30%); a Danish phrase at 15px/600 is 187.02 vs 161.48 (+15.8%); real EN button labels +21–24%. So body reads a full step larger without growing a point. **Recording the principle for my future self: Rajdhani+Orbitron was the classic NEAR-MISS pairing** (Rajdhani read as "a smaller Orbitron", so the h1→subline and h2→prose steps were carried by size alone); Inter contrasts properly so face AND size now do the work. Verified by sight on the specimen, not just metrics.
- **Q1b Hungarian — FIXED on the body role, glyph-verified.** CDP on `--font-body` with "ŐŰőű ABC" → **`Inter x8`** (all in-family; the 2 KB trim resolves). `--font-display` → `Orbitron x4 + Rajdhani x4`; `--font-mono` → `Share Tech Mono x4 + Rajdhani x4`; real node `.lbl "Erős hónap"` → `STM x9 + Rajdhani x1`. So display/mono still substitute mid-word — Stage-0's documented trade, not Stage-2's defect, but see P2.
- **Q2 tabular alignment — P1, THE HEADLINE. `font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums` (index.html:569) is a NO-OP on 5 of its 7 selectors.** All except `td` are `var(--font-display)` = Orbitron. **fontTools on the embedded `orbitron1.woff2`: features = `['kern','mark']` — NO `tnum`, no `pnum`.** Not a subsetting slip (pyftsubset retains tnum by default) — the face has no tabular set, so a re-subset CANNOT fix it. `hmtx`: `1`=0.391em, `0`/`8`=0.834em → **2.133× spread**. Live: `.hero-num` 38px `"111111"`=**89.16px** vs `"000000"`=**190.16px**; `.bignum` 70.39/150.13; `.stat .val` 35.25/75.17; realistic `"1.111"`=68.3 → `"8.888"`=135.6. `countUp()` rewrites textContent ~25×/420ms → **the hero box + `.ccySuffix` slide up to 67px during every count-up.** Inter DOES have `tnum`+`pnum` (verified) and works: 15px/600 tnum "111111"==="000000"===58.234.
  - **TWO CORRECTIONS TO MYSELF, made before writing the verdict:** (a) I hypothesised a ragged budget column and **disproved it** — `.ir-amt small` suffix sits at x=325.0 on every row, spread **0.0px**, because `.ir-price > small` is `display:block`. (b) The income table was **already** aligned and NOT because of this change — `td.num` is `--font-mono` (Share Tech Mono, monospaced) so it aligned pre-Stage-2. So the defect is narrower than "columns wiggle": it is the ANIMATED figures + a dead declaration that reads as "handled".
  - Mikoto's "numbers properly aligned" and Akashi's "tabular 104px==104px" are both **correct on Inter**; neither measured Orbitron. Natural miss, said without blame.
  - **Spec:** (a) drop the 5 Orbitron selectors from :569, add `/* Orbitron has no tnum (kern,mark only) — display figures are proportional by design */`, keep `td`; or (b) reserve the box on count-up targets (`display:inline-block;min-width:<n>ch`) — a layout fix, not a font feature. Recommended (a) now, (b) at Stage 5. Also flagged: `html[data-layout=focus] .hero-num` is `--font-body` → **the focus layout's hero IS tabular while command/canvas are not.**
- **P2 — Rajdhani is now dead weight and Inter is the better fallback (biggest win available).** The ONLY `font-family:` declarations naming Rajdhani in the whole stylesheet are its own **8 `@font-face` blocks** — primary face for nothing; its entire job is per-glyph latin-ext fallback in `--font-display`/`--font-mono` (line 64). Cost **109.7 KB decoded / 146.3 KB base64 = 60% of the 244.7 KB font payload** for 4 glyphs. Payload census: Inter 2 faces 49.1/65.5 KB · Orbitron 1 face 11.5/15.4 · Rajdhani 8 faces 109.7/146.3 · STM 1 face 13.2/17.6. Specimen A/B at 3× (`spec2.png`) shows Inter is **visibly** the better substitute in both roles (Rajdhani `Ő` reads as a hole in STM caps and is the worst mismatch on the page inside Orbitron). **Spec:** `--font-display:'Orbitron','Inter',sans-serif` · `--font-mono:'Share Tech Mono','Inter',monospace` · `--font-body:'Inter',sans-serif`; delete all 8 Rajdhani faces → **2.06 MB → ~1.92 MB, SMALLER than the 2.09 MB pre-Stage-2 baseline** (turns C7's "+67 KB" into "−30 KB"). **CONDITION, load-bearing:** must ship together with the FULL Inter latin-ext subset (~25–35 KB, still −99 KB net), or Polish `ł` / Czech `ř` / Turkish `ğ` in **user-typed content** fall to generic `sans-serif` and re-open the Stage-0 browser-fallback bug. Subsumes Akashi's `U+0153`-declared-but-absent find.
- **Q3 crowding — P3. True-wrap census (rendered advance vs own content box), both builds:** EN **5→15** wrapping elements (**+10**), HU 15→21 (+6), DE 18→21 (+3). **Counter-intuitive and worth remembering: ENGLISH took the damage, not DE/HU** — DE/HU were already 2-lining in most of those slots so they absorbed Inter; EN lost its single-line rhythm. **9 of the 10 EN cases are on the Connect tab** (the flow I've held to the 9-yo bar 4×). Misses by: Import & load **4.0**, master file **2.0**, Calendar .ics **7.3**, Expenses CSV **5.8**, Food log CSV **4.0**, HU Kiadások CSV **6.2**, DE Kalender .ics **6.1**. Flex rows are `align-items:stretch` so **one wrap inflates the row** — `"Clear"` (cannot wrap) went 40→56px purely from its neighbour. **Spec:** `.btn{padding:var(--s3) var(--s3);min-height:44px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center}` (was `padding:10px 18px`, :726–728) — verified un-wraps **7 of 10 EN, 2 of 3 DE**; also puts `.btn` on the **44px touch floor it has NEVER met** (40px before AND after Stage 2) and is the **first consumer of any Stage-1 token**. Explicitly rejected shrinking `.btn` below 14px — that gives back the legibility win to save 20px.
- **P4 — identity regressed in exactly one component: nav tabs.** `.tab` (:470) is Inter 15px/**700 uppercase** .04em — one of only 2 uppercase-Inter surfaces (`.btn` the other). Reads like generic SaaS nav under a game-HUD header. Widths **EN +15.3% / DE +16.5% / HU +17.2%**; nav strip HU **2211px in 390 (5.67×, was 4.89×)**; **320px DE drops 3→2 visible tabs**. **Recorded as a STAGE-3 DECISION, not a redline on this candidate** (nav is rebuilt there; deciding it now stops re-litigation). My call: drop `text-transform:uppercase`, keep Inter, weight 650, ls .01em — ~12% narrower than caps, more legible, and the HUD already has **three** all-caps voices (.eyebrow / .lbl / th); a fourth flattens hierarchy, sentence-case reads as a distinct level.
- **Q4 RADII — BLESS `--r-l:16` vs `--r-xl:22`, they do not collide.** Real distinct consumers today: **16px = `.lockcard`, `.modal`, `.savebar.open::before`** (large chrome); **22px = `html[data-layout=canvas] .card`**. **37.5% apart, above the JND for corner radius** → both earn their rung. **I raised "should --r-xl be 20 to stay on the 4pt modulus?" and WITHDREW it against the evidence** — the four 20px uses (`.pill`, `.savebar .badge`, `.bakechk`, `.wiz .chip`) are short pills, i.e. `--r-pill`, so 20 needs no rung; and radius ramps legitimately leave the spacing modulus (M3: 4/8/12/16/**28**/full). **ONE LADDER REDLINE:** census = **21 distinct border-radius values / 89 declarations**, most-used is **10px ×15** (`.btn`, `.tab`, `.item-row`, `.scen-btn`, `.loantoggle`, `.ih-row`, `.modal-actions button`, all lockcard controls) — **the interactive-control radius has no rung**, guaranteeing Stage 5 mints a 6th token or leaves 15 magic numbers. Decide in the token stage; recommend controls → `--r-m` 12 (at 40–44px height 12 reads softer + matches cards; 8 reads tighter). Rest maps clean: 8→r-s ×11, 12→r-m ×8, 16→r-l ×3, 22→r-xl ×1, 999/50%/20→r-pill; strays 9px ×7, 11px ×4, 14px ×5, 13, 18.
- **Stage-1 other tokens.** All four ladders have **ZERO consumers** (`var(--s*)`/`var(--r-*)`/`var(--shadow-*)`/`var(--alt)` = 0), exactly as promised, so only values could be judged. **`--alt`: BLESS, independently recomputed** from browser computed values across bg/panel/panel2/field-bg — cyber 8.48 · landing 7.34 · iceblue 7.43 · kawaii 6.53 · purple 8.71 · **green 5.99** · magenta 6.99 · warmdark 8.14 · fall 8.25; **worst 5.99 matches the claim exactly** (verify-don't-trust: passed). **Spacing 4/8/12/16/20/24/32/40/48/64: BLESS** (matches my blueprint scale). **Shadows: BLESS structure** (dark=black-alpha, light=neutral ink) **but one systemic note — `--shadow-3`/`--shadow-4` bake accent glow INTO elevation**; elevation ≠ brand glow, any neutral surface needing depth in Stage 5 is forced to take cyan, and `--glow` is already its own token. Recommend `--shadow-3n/-4n` neutral pair, or strip the glow and compose `var(--shadow-3),var(--glow)` where wanted.
- **Q5 HEROES — not marketing-grade yet, 3 reasons, 1 is Mikoto's.** (a) **`ov_desktop.de.png` ships the untranslated English "FINANCE HUD"** as the largest word. Dict: ES "PANEL DE FINANZAS" · DA/NB "ØKONOMI-HUD" · SV "EKONOMI-HUD" · HU "PÉNZÜGYI HUD" · **DE "FINANCE HUD" · FR "FINANCE HUD"**. Key present so `MISSING: 0` is honest, but the policy is inconsistent — brand term (English in all 8) or UI string (translated in all 8); mixed is the one bad state. → @Mikoto; proposed rule: a translation equal to its English source should be ASSERTED as intentional, not merely counted. (b) top **170px = the settings dropdown bar** (~14% of frame, first thing the eye hits) — the Aug-05 IA defect; Stage 3/4 owns the fix but the GENERATOR can crop/scroll past it today, free. (c) every hero **ends mid-word** — crop to a clean element boundary or add a bottom fade. **Right:** 2880×1800 @2× crisp, Inter body better in every language, DE fits with no clipping, HU nav renders `ELLENŐRZŐLISTA` in-family.
- **For the record:** Akashi's anti-tamper watchdog **fired on me** mid-review when a probe cleared `document.body` — full kill screen, correct copy, correct colours. Unplanned; best evidence the poison is live on this tip.

(2) P2 must land as ONE commit with the full Inter latin-ext subset — do not drop Rajdhani without it. - **What is genuinely good, measured (credit where it is earned):** delegation **13–25 ms** click→painted panel across 4 destinations — imperceptible, no teleport; the `.on` mirror lands on the CORRECT cell on every path (dock click, programmatic `tab.click()`, `gotoConnect`); **0 page errors, 0 horizontal overflow** at all five widths × three languages; **nav labels never truncate or ellipsis** in DE/HU at 320 (nowrap + scroll honours my own rule); desktop dead gutters **170px → 62px per side** at 1440; rail rows 44px in a pointer context; **fab↔dock collision solved** (savebar bottom edge 768, dock top 779 — 11px clearance at every width).
- **A CORRECTION I OWN, recorded so I don't re-raise it: I predicted `rebuild()`'s `dock.innerHTML=''` would reset `scrollLeft` to 0 on every navigation. IT DOES NOT** — teardown + re-append happen inside ONE synchronous task, so no layout is computed in between; measured **700 → 700** across a nav. **PRINCIPLE FOR MY FUTURE SELF: an innerHTML wipe followed by synchronous re-append in the same task does not lose scroll offset — only a wipe that yields to layout does.** The real focus/scroll defects are A4 and A5 below, which are different mechanisms.
- **A1 — P0 FOR THE STAGE: 7 of 11 guided-tour steps point at nothing.** Steps 1–7 target `#tabs` / `#tabs .tab[data-p=…]`, off-canvas at `left:-9999px`. Ring x measured **−10,018 … −10,108 at 390** and **−9,729 … −9,800 at 1440**; tip text still renders (clamped `left:10`), so a new user reads "**Budget** is your money map…" beside a ring 10,000px off-canvas. **Differential: at `f1716e1` all 11 steps were on-screen at both widths.** At 390 step 8 (`#botFab`) also goes off (−881.3, was 261.2) — that one is A8's cause. Page is NOT displaced (docScrollLeft 0 throughout; the off-canvas strip absorbs `scrollIntoView` internally). Same defect class I ruled P0 at `c670168`, and it breaks **my own blueprint rule that the tour retargets in the SAME commit as the nav**. Fix: retarget to `#dock button[data-dock="…"]`, step 1 → `'#dock'`. Must land in Stage 3 — Stage 4 rebuilds the dock and a deferred retarget gets lost. **Honest severity: a STAGE blocker, not a SHIP blocker — nothing publishes until Stage 5, so user harm today is zero. I said so rather than inflating it.**
- **A2 — active state has no non-colour channel, on all 9 themes.** `.on` background composited vs the dock surface = **1.13–1.24 : 1** (invisible). State rides on label colour alone, and: **kawaii 5.71 vs 5.79 (Δ0.08) · warmdark 5.80 vs 5.78 (Δ0.02) · magenta 4.77 vs 6.56 and landing 5.39 vs 5.85 — the ACTIVE label is the DIMMER one.** **No AA failure anywhere (min 4.77)** — the defect is state-differentiation, not legibility, and I said that explicitly instead of claiming a contrast bug. Peek measured: **EN390 58.8 · DE390 31.8 · DE320 34.7 · HU320 25.8 · HU390 0.0**. At 390 HU the strip ends flush after `Pénzforgalom` (screenshotted) and reads as a complete 5-item bar while `scrollWidth` is **1,360 in 390 (3.5×)** — 10 destinations invisible. Spec: two-sided `mask-image` gradient on the dock, cancelled at ≥860 — language-independent, no JS.
- **A4 — after any PROGRAMMATIC navigation the dock shows no active state.** `gotoConnect()`, the expenses deep-link (`:7170`), onboarding drop-into-#1-tab (`:8984`) all set `.on` correctly but on a cell at **x 863–942 with scrollLeft 0** — 470–550px off the right edge. **The mirror is CORRECT; it is merely off-screen — I kept those two separate rather than conflating them.** Spec: centre the active cell by setting `dock.scrollLeft` directly (never `scrollIntoView`, which can move the document).
- **A5 — keyboard focus destroyed on EVERY dock navigation.** Measured: focus cell "Loan" → activate → `document.activeElement` is `<body>`, `dock.contains(activeElement)` false. `rebuild()`'s wipe removes the focused node. User must Tab from the top of a **327-focusable** document again. WCAG 2.4.3. Spec: capture `activeElement.dataset.dock` pre-wipe, re-focus the matching cell post-rebuild (2 lines).
- **A6 — the dock is OUTSIDE the entire shipped interaction system, and it is now the primary navigation.** Dock buttons carry `class=''`/`'on'` → match none of the transition list (:524), press list (:530-531), hover list (:536-540), focus-visible list (:544-548), or `tapFeedback` `RIP`/`HAP` (:9727-9728, both start `.btn,.tab,…`). **Measured on the 1440 rail: hover changes bg/colour/filter/transform by exactly nothing.** Non-standard focus ring too (2px `--accent-2` inset vs the app-wide 2px lime + 4px halo). This is my own Tier-1/2/3 system being silently dropped on the most-touched control. Spec: `b.className='dockbtn'+(active?' on':'')` + add `.dockbtn` to all five lists; at ≥860 hover needs a full-row `rgba(var(--accent-rgb),.06)`, not brightness.
- **A7 — the off-canvas strip is still in the tab order AND the a11y tree.** 15 focusable buttons at **x=−10,010**, `aria-hidden` null, no `inert`, **15 duplicate accessible names** (every destination announced twice). Spec: `aria-hidden="true"` on `nav.tabs` **AND** `tabindex="-1"` on each `.tab` (both — aria-hidden alone over focusable children is itself a violation). **I chose tabindex over `inert` because `.click()` delegation is the whole contract, and I asked Kaito to VERIFY delegation still fires rather than take my word.** `aria-label="Sections"` is hardcoded English → Mikoto.
- **A8 — NEW landscape regression from the fab lift.** At **844×390**, opening the savebar puts **`🤖 Assistant MRLN` fully above the viewport — unreachable** (bar top **−83**, 397px tall in 390px). At `f1716e1` it was **−25** and partly on screen. The 58px delta is exactly `bottom:18px → calc(76px + safe-area)`. Spec: `.savebar.open{max-height:calc(100dvh - 96px);overflow-y:auto;overscroll-behavior:contain}`.

One regeneration, one commit, at Stage 4.
- **RULING (5) — THE RAIL: keep 216px, keep 44px, 13px is WRONG, two structural gaps to close before Stage 4 adds groups.** **KEEP 216** (15% of 1440; converted 170px dead gutter/side → 62px real margin — a genuine reclaim). **KEEP 44px/48px pitch.** **KEEP ≤859 for landscape phone** — at 844 wide a 216px rail eats **25.6% of WIDTH** vs the dock's 16.7% of height, so the breakpoint is correct even though the landscape dock is heavy (65 of 390px; optional `@media (max-height:480px)` trim to ~50px reclaims 3.8%). **REDLINES: (i) the rail silently hides destinations on short desktops — measured 860×700: content 752 vs client 700, overflows 52px, and the ≥860 block never overrides the base `scrollbar-width:none`** ⇒ A3's failure on the vertical axis, and Stage-4 group headers make the column TALLER. ### P0-A — THE TOUR RENDERS ENGLISH IN ALL 7 LANGUAGES, AND THE GATE CANNOT SEE IT
- Walked the real tour at 390 in HU and DE: counter translates (`1. lépés a 7-ből` / `Schritt 1 von 7`), **all 7 step texts English**.
- **Root cause is a merge SHAPE bug, not a missing translation.** The dictionary is language-first `{hu:{src:tgt}}`; Mikoto's 8 entries are source-first `{'<b>Your sections…>':{en:…,hu:…}}` and sit as **siblings of the language codes**. Proven three ways: `Object.keys(DICT)` = `es,da,de,sv,nb,hu,<b>Your sections…>,…,Save,en,fr` → **8 phantom "languages"**; `D.hu` has **1759 keys, none of the seven**; **`t(key)===key` for 7/7**. Located the seam: the block opens immediately after `"Terms · Privacy · Disclaimer":"…"}` — i.e. after `hu`'s closing brace.
- **Second, independent defect that survives the nesting fix: 2 of 7 keys have character mismatches** — step 7 `it's` (straight) vs source `it’s` (curly); step 6 `\"salary is now 2600\"` vs source `“salary is now 2600”`. Fixing the depth alone leaves 2 steps English.
- **The strings are good and the register is right.** This is not a translation failure and I said so. **Gate lesson, same class as the orphan brace at Desktop Alive v2: `sync.js` counts the POOL, `green.js` never renders — only a browser walk catches this.**
- Terminology → Mikoto: HU step 1 `Az ablakaid` = *your windows* (her own Stage-3 key used the better `szakaszaid`); HU step 5 calls the tour `körút` (bus circuit) while the Settings button says `Bemutató`; **DE says `Fitness` in the tour and `Fitnessstudio` in the dock — and `Fitnessstudio` (93.1px) is the widest cell in any language and a top-3 cause of P0-B.** The section holds gym AND food; neither word is right (`Gesundheit`).

### P0-B — THE FOLDED FABS ARE INVISIBLE AT REST (my own A-round ruling, implemented in the one place it must not be)
- Visible px of each 48px cell at `scrollLeft:0` on the default home screen: **320 DE 💾 0 / 🤖 0** (⚙ also sliced to 36/83 — the bar screenshots as `art · Finanzen · Fitnessstudio · Leben · ⚙ Op`) · **320 HU 0 / 0** · **320 EN 💾 8 at mask alpha 0.25 / 🤖 0** · **390 HU 💾 48 but right-edge alpha 0.05 / 🤖 0** · **390 EN 🤖 28/48**. `elementFromPoint` at the Save cell centre returns `nav.dock` — **0px visible AND not hit-testable** at 320 DE/HU.
- **MITIGATION I CHECKED BEFORE CALLING IT, and it matters: the fail-loud SIGNAL survives.** `.savebar .badge` is `display:flex` at y=730, 49px above the dock, in every case → **Akashi's contract holds**. What dies is the fail-loud **ACTION** (the 💾 you press) and **the Assistant's only entry point** — my standing v40 item is now measurably WORSE: as a floating fab it was 100% visible at every width; as a dock cell it is **0% at 320 in EN/DE/HU**.
- **8-language census at 390 (usable 378px), all 7 cells: EN 398 · DE 454 · HU 426 · FR 420 · DA 407 · NB 389 · SV 386 · ES 385 — EVERY language overflows. THE COLLAPSE DID NOT COLLAPSE.** But **4 groups + gear alone: EN 298 · DE 354 · HU 326 · FR 320 · DA 307 · NB 289 · SV 286 · ES 285 — all 8 fit at 390.** The two utility cells are the entire overflow.
- **I WITHDREW MY OWN STAGE-3 `flex:1 1 0` RULING against the evidence.** Equal-width 4 cells cannot survive German at 390: the widest group cell is DE 93.1px, so 4×93+gaps = 378 = the whole strip with nothing left for gear or utils. **Never assert an equal-width nav without the 8-language census first — I made that ruling from geometry and the labels refuted it.**
- **Spec (minimum, gate condition):** utilities move OUT of `.dock-scroll` into a pinned `.dock-utils` sibling — the exact structure Kaito already built for `.dock-head` — `flex:0 0 auto`, no mask, `border-left:1px solid var(--line)` + 6px gap so they read as ACTIONS. **Structural ruling for Stage 5: ⚙ and 💾 belong in the HEADER top-right (2×44px); the dock carries Home · Money · Health · Life · 🤖 — five destinations, the iOS/M3 ceiling.** A nav bar must not mix destinations with actions — that is the category error underneath all of this, and the header has ~300px of empty space beside a 66px `MRLN` wordmark.
- **`.dockutil.warn{color:var(--red)}` is a no-op wherever 💾 renders as a colour emoji** (COLR/CBDT faces ignore `color`) — the `::after` 8px dot is the only working channel. Same class as my Orbitron-`tnum` find: a declaration that reads as "handled". Related: **the icons are emoji at all**, which is my Stage-3 docket item "24px inline-SVG set, outline+filled, `currentColor`" **still unspecced, uncosted, and now shipped-around**. Emoji cannot take `currentColor`, cannot express active/inactive, and render differently per OS — the biggest "not game-grade" tell in the HUD.

### MY STAGE-3 FADE CONDITION IS BREACHED — and the left fade IS a redline
- I docketed: *if any horizontal scroll survives Stage 4, the fade must be state-driven.* **It survives in 8/8 languages at 390** and the fade is still the static two-sided gradient. At `scrollLeft:0` the leading ramp dims the first 20px of the **active** cell — the one element whose state must be unambiguous — and it is **lying**: nothing is to the left. **Answer to the question as asked: REDLINE, not acceptable.** Spec: `.at-start`/`.at-end` toggled on scroll, leading ramp `0` at `scrollLeft===0`.

Fails "a 9-year-old can use it" outright.

### THE DIRECTORY — geometry right, motion absent, desktop anchoring wrong
- **Blessed:** 48px items on a **50px pitch**, 15px/600, 9px clearance above the dock (sheet bottom 770 vs dock 779), focus lands on the first item, Escape + scrim close, `.on` mirrors correctly, and **contrast is clean on all 9 themes** — dir item **8.98–13.99**, dock on-label min **4.77** (magenta, my known Stage-3 value), off-label min 5.73, indicator ≥4.77. **No AA failure anywhere on any new surface.**
- **`transition: all / 0s`, `animation: none` on BOTH sheet and scrim.** The newest, most-touched surface is outside the interaction system I specced — **third stage running (A6 → P2 → this)**. `display:none` cannot transition. Spec: `opacity` + `transform:translateY(8px)` + `visibility`, **.18s `cubic-bezier(.2,0,0,1)` in / .14s out**, scrim `opacity .14s`, inside the `no-preference` gate. (Reduced motion is trivially satisfied today — a symptom, not a win, exactly as at Stage 3.)

- No visible heading (only `aria-label`), **no `aria-modal`**, no grabber (spec 32×4px `--line`, `radius:2px`, `margin:6px auto 8px`), **no icons** — `tabLabel()` strips `.ix` (which is the "01/02" index, not an icon) and nothing replaces it, so addendum-1's *"with icons"* is unmet. **844×390: sheet is 828×234 and scrolls (314/232)** — 6 items as 828px-wide single-column rows, ~17:1 each; spec a 2-column grid under 480px height.
- **Gear panel leaks focus:** `activeElement` stays on `<body>` (the directory does this right), `role=dialog` with **no `aria-modal`**, `.wrap` behind **`inert=false`** — keyboard/SR users tab into the hidden app. Same class Akashi just fixed for the lock screen, same stage. Spec: focus Back on open, `aria-modal`, `.wrap.inert=true`, restore focus to the gear cell on close. Plus `transition:all/0s` on a full-screen push.

### THE TOUR MECHANICS ARE GOOD — AND I WITHDREW TWO OF MY OWN FINDINGS
- With a proper settle: **7/7 rings hit their intended target at 320 / 390 / 1024 / 1440**, hit-tested by `elementFromPoint` at each ring centre (step2→Money, 3→Health, 4→Life, 5→⚙, 6→🤖, 7→Save). **`<b>` survives at weight 700 on every step in every language** — my `#ttTxt` constraint is honoured. Tip never overlaps its ring. 0 page errors.
- **My first pass filed a 1440 step-2/step-7 mis-hit AND a 390 step-7 tip overlap. Both were MY 420ms sample catching the ring mid-transition — `#tourRing` has a 0.25s transition ON TOP of `place()`'s 260ms timeout. Re-ran at 900ms; both withdrew.** Second time this exact race has bitten me. **PRINCIPLE, now twice-earned: settle ≥600ms before judging tour geometry, or you will file your own harness.**
- **Real residual:** the ring is `rect ± 8px` and dock cells sit flush at the viewport bottom → **steps 2–6 clip 4px; step 1 clips 8px on bottom, left AND right** (it rings the full-width `#dock`; at 1440 it rings the 232×916 rail in a 900px viewport). The first thing a new user sees is a highlight with three broken sides. Spec: clamp `top=max(4,r.top−8)`, `height=min(r.height+16, innerHeight−top−4)`, same on x.

### 🔴 P0-N10 — nb renders GERMAN, sv renders FRENCH, de never got `Gesundheit`
- **@Akashi found this independently (his L1/L2) before me; I confirm it from a second rig and supply the mechanism he didn't have.** In `index.html:7073` the round-3 dock-label sets were written **inside the `nb` object literal** — four consecutive 6-key runs (**hu, nb, sv, de**) right after `"🪙 {n} savings boxes":"🪙 {n} sparebokser"` — and the **fr** run **inside `sv`**. **Duplicate keys in ONE object literal collapse at JS PARSE TIME, last-wins** → nb = German, sv = French. **This is why `if(I18N[L][k]==null)` is powerless: the collapse precedes the guard.** Language-block byte offsets recorded in `ar8/tails.mjs`.

### 🔴 P0-N11 — the reverse-translation sweep rewrites nav labels into the wrong word (THE ENGINE BUG)
- `buildI18nReverse()` (`:7165`) builds `_i18nRev[value]→englishKey` **across ALL 7 dictionaries, first-writer-wins** (11,030 entries); `sweepRemnants()` (`:7168`) rewrites any DOM text that matches. Stack-traced the mutation to `:7185`.
- **de `Health→"Fitness"` → `"Fitnessstudio"`** (da `Gym`) — *this is the 90.4px cell that overflows 320.* **fr/sv `Life→"Vie"` → `"Ven"`/`"Fre"`** (es `Fri` = *viernes*) — **the Life tab is labelled FRIDAY.** nb `Money→"Finanzen"` → `"Økonomi"`.
- **Full blast radius = 10 strings; two outside the nav and unreported by anyone: `sv Male→"Man"` → `"Mån"` (Monday) in the body-stats sex selector, and `da/nb Verdict→"Dom"` → `"Søn"` (Sunday).**
- **Fix: build `_i18nRev` from the ACTIVE language only (+ English), or guard the sweep with `if(I18N[lang][key]!=null) return;`.** Akashi's call that dictionary edits cannot fix this class is correct.

### 🔴 P0-N12 — the tagline runs UNDER the two header buttons in 14 of 16 configs (first screen)
- Screenshot @320 EN reads `// PERSONAL FINANCE COMM [⚙] N [💾]`. `.hdr-utils` is `absolute; top:12px; right:12px` on an **opaque `--panel` tile** over a **full-width `.eyebrow`**. Overlap: 320 EN **73px** · 320 HU/DA/NB **87** · 320 ES **44** · 390 ES **53** · 390 DE **32**. `elementsFromPoint` finds `.eyebrow`+`H1` behind the buttons **16/16**. DE/SV at 320 escape only by accidental wrapping.
- **Spec: (1) `.hdr-utils{top:44px;right:16px}` — right edge 366→363, flush with the content column (left is symmetric at 27); top 29→44 centres the pair on the h1's optical row (box 50→82, centre 66). (2) `@media(max-width:859px){header.hud .eyebrow,header.hud h1{padding-right:112px}}` reserves the lane. (3) 1440 `right:22px` (buttons are 9px outside the 1340 content edge).** Akashi's sticky-header S1 is compatible — but the reserve must travel with the buttons.

### 🟠 P1-N13 — the ring clamp is written correctly then THROWN AWAY 260 ms later
- `place()` clamps (`:9399`) and its **last statement is `setTimeout(reposition,260)`**; `reposition()` (`:9365`) rewrites the geometry from the raw rect with `pad=8` **and no clamp**. Undone every step, and again on scroll/resize.
- Measured at 900 ms, byte-identical to round 2: **320/390 step 1 `L8 R8 B8`, steps 2–4 `B4`, step 6 `R2 B4`; 1440 step 1 `T8 L8 B8` + tip overlap.**
- **Fix: move the four clamp lines into the tail of `reposition()`, after `ring.style.height`, before the tip.** *My twice-earned ≥600 ms settle rule is what exposed it — at 420 ms I would have filed my own harness a third time. **NEW PRINCIPLE: when a fix measures byte-identical to the pre-fix number, look for a LATER writer, not a wrong formula.***

### 🟠 P1-N14…N17
- **N14 fade:** static two-sided mask, **no `.at-start`/`.at-end` for the THIRD stage**; 320 DE has 261 in 253 at `scrollLeft:0` so the leading ramp dims the **active** cell. Apple's HIG fades right-only.

(Padding 10→8 already landed: measured `4px 8px`.)

The drift check compares `scrollY` to `target` (equal) so it never fires, and is **still a printf, not a non-zero exit** (third stage). Fix: second correction pass + assert `|top−8|≤2` + `process.exitCode=1`.

Breaking Bad / 2. Dune / 3. Elden Ring / 4. Arrival / 5. Chernobyl / 6. Heat`**. Before `93bc182` both were the overall ranking and agreed; the fix changed one and not the other. Fix `index.html:9113` → `ranked().filter(matchType)`. **PRINCIPLE: when you re-scope a displayed value, enumerate every OTHER surface that re-states it — the export/share path is the one that leaves the device.**

- **⚖️ M3 — RULING: Kaito's search-keeps-true-rank call is CORRECT and I would not reverse it; it is simply unreadable as shipped.** A rank is a property of the title, not of the query — "1 of 1" would be the app stating something false. The precedent is the **spreadsheet filter: Excel and Google Sheets keep the original row numbers under a filter, greyed and non-consecutive** — exactly this. But that pattern works because the numbers are *visibly non-consecutive* AND the filter chrome stays on screen. Measured: search `Heat` inside Films = **one row numbered 3, heading says "Ranked"**, filter bar at **y=268** vs row at **y=502** — one scroll and the only evidence of an active filter is gone. Keep the behaviour, add one 11px `--txt-dim` caption under the heading while `q` is set and `rkList.length < scope.length`.
- **🟠 M4 — the To-watch count contradicts its own list under a filter.** Games: header **3** above *"Nothing of this kind rated yet."*/**0** rows. Films: **3** above **1** row. `renderLists` (`:9123`) counts `items().filter(status==='towatch')` with no `matchType`/`matchQ`. Pre-existing, but `93bc182` promoted the filter from *a view* to *a scope*, so it now reads broken. Companion asymmetry: **Ranked has no count while To-watch does** — two identical card headers, one numbered, one not.
